`addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_input.gd`:

```gd
extends RefCounted
## Input injection for a running game.
##
## Events go through Input.parse_input_event and Input.flush_buffered_events, which is the
## same path a real device uses (including the headless display server). Presses are
## remembered so input.release_all can clear them. A tap is delivered at the start of a
## frame, held for one process frame, then released.

const R := preload("rt_result.gd")

const MOUSE_BUTTONS := {
    "left": MOUSE_BUTTON_LEFT,
    "right": MOUSE_BUTTON_RIGHT,
    "middle": MOUSE_BUTTON_MIDDLE,
}
const JOY_BUTTONS := {
    "a": JOY_BUTTON_A, "b": JOY_BUTTON_B, "x": JOY_BUTTON_X, "y": JOY_BUTTON_Y,
    "back": JOY_BUTTON_BACK, "start": JOY_BUTTON_START, "guide": JOY_BUTTON_GUIDE,
    "left_stick": JOY_BUTTON_LEFT_STICK, "right_stick": JOY_BUTTON_RIGHT_STICK,
    "left_shoulder": JOY_BUTTON_LEFT_SHOULDER, "right_shoulder": JOY_BUTTON_RIGHT_SHOULDER,
    "dpad_up": JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_UP, "dpad_down": JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_DOWN,
    "dpad_left": JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_LEFT, "dpad_right": JOY_BUTTON_DPAD_RIGHT,
}
const JOY_AXES := {
    "left_x": JOY_AXIS_LEFT_X, "left_y": JOY_AXIS_LEFT_Y,
    "right_x": JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_X, "right_y": JOY_AXIS_RIGHT_Y,
    "trigger_left": JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_LEFT, "trigger_right": JOY_AXIS_TRIGGER_RIGHT,
}

static var _keys: Dictionary = {}
static var _mouse: Dictionary = {}
static var _joy_buttons: Dictionary = {}
static var _joy_axes: Dictionary = {}
static var _touches: Dictionary = {}
static var _mouse_pos := Vector2.ZERO


static func methods() -> Dictionary:
    return {
        "input.run": {"fn": run_steps},
        "input.release_all": {"fn": release_all},
        "input.wait": {"fn": wait_frames},
    }


static func run_steps(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var raw: Variant = params.get("steps", [])
    if not raw is Array or (raw as Array).is_empty():
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "input.run: 'steps' must be a non-empty array.")
    var steps: Array = []
    var index := 0
    for entry: Variant in raw:
        if not entry is Dictionary:
            return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d] must be an object." % index)
        var parsed := _parse(entry, index)
        if R.is_err(parsed):
            return parsed
        steps.append(parsed["step"])
        index += 1
    var state := {"steps": steps, "index": 0, "mode": "lead", "results": [], "hold": 0, "release": {}}
    return {"_pending": true, "_tick": func() -> bool: return _tick_steps(rt, state)}


static func release_all(rt: Node, _params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var released := _release_everything()
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    return R.ok({"released": released, "held": _held_summary()})


static func wait_frames(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var frames := clampi(int(params.get("frames", 1)), 0, 600)
    var state := {"left": frames, "start": Engine.get_process_frames()}
    return {"_pending": true, "_tick": func() -> bool:
        if int(state["left"]) > 0:
            state["left"] = int(state["left"]) - 1
            return true
        rt.finish_job(R.ok({"frames": Engine.get_process_frames() - int(state["start"])}))
        return false
    }


static func _tick_steps(rt: Node, state: Dictionary) -> bool:
    var steps: Array = state["steps"]
    var index := int(state["index"])
    var mode := str(state["mode"])
    if mode == "lead":
        state["mode"] = "body"
        return true
    if mode == "hold":
        _release_tap(state["release"])
        state["release"] = {}
        (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": str((steps[index] as Dictionary)["type"]), "ok": true})
        state["index"] = index + 1
        state["mode"] = "body"
        index += 1
        mode = "body"
    if mode == "wait":
        state["hold"] = int(state["hold"]) - 1
        if int(state["hold"]) > 0:
            return true
        (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": "wait", "ok": true})
        state["index"] = index + 1
        state["mode"] = "body"
        index += 1
    if mode == "text":
        return _tick_text(rt, state)
    if index >= steps.size():
        rt.finish_job(R.ok({"steps": state["results"], "held": _held_summary()}))
        return false
    var step: Dictionary = steps[index]
    var kind := str(step["type"])
    if kind == "wait":
        var frames := int(step["frames"])
        if frames <= 0:
            (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": "wait", "ok": true})
            state["index"] = index + 1
            return true
        state["hold"] = frames
        state["mode"] = "wait"
        return true
    if kind == "text":
        var chars: Array = []
        for character in str(step["text"]):
            chars.append(character.unicode_at(0))
        state["chars"] = chars
        state["char_i"] = 0
        state["char_down"] = false
        state["mode"] = "text"
        return _tick_text(rt, state)
    if kind == "key" or kind == "mouse_button" or kind == "touch" or kind == "joy_button":
        var phase := str(step["phase"])
        if phase == "tap":
            _apply_phase(step, "press")
            state["release"] = step
            state["mode"] = "hold"
            return true
        _apply_phase(step, phase)
        (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": kind, "ok": true})
        state["index"] = index + 1
        return true
    if kind == "mouse_move":
        _mouse_motion(float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
    elif kind == "joy_axis":
        _joy_axis(str(step["axis"]), float(step["value"]), int(step["device"]))
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
    (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": kind, "ok": true})
    state["index"] = index + 1
    return true


static func _tick_text(rt: Node, state: Dictionary) -> bool:
    var chars: Array = state["chars"]
    var char_i := int(state["char_i"])
    if bool(state["char_down"]):
        _unicode(int(chars[char_i]), false)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
        state["char_down"] = false
        state["char_i"] = char_i + 1
        char_i += 1
    if char_i >= chars.size():
        (state["results"] as Array).append({"type": "text", "ok": true})
        state["index"] = int(state["index"]) + 1
        state["mode"] = "body"
        return true
    _unicode(int(chars[char_i]), true)
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    state["char_down"] = true
    return true


static func _apply_phase(step: Dictionary, phase: String) -> void:
    var kind := str(step["type"])
    if kind == "key":
        var code := int(step["code"])
        if phase == "release":
            _key_event(code, false)
            _keys.erase(code)
        else:
            _key_event(code, true)
            _keys[code] = true
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
    elif kind == "mouse_button":
        var button := str(step["button"])
        var index := int(MOUSE_BUTTONS[button])
        if phase == "release":
            _mouse_event(index, false, float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            _mouse.erase(button)
        else:
            _mouse_motion(float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            _mouse_event(index, true, float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            _mouse[button] = true
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
    elif kind == "touch":
        var touch_index := int(step["index"])
        if phase == "release":
            _touch_event(touch_index, false, float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            _touches.erase(touch_index)
        else:
            _touch_event(touch_index, true, float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            _touches[touch_index] = Vector2(float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
    elif kind == "joy_button":
        var button_name := str(step["button"])
        var joy_index := int(JOY_BUTTONS[button_name])
        var device := int(step["device"])
        var key := "%d:%s" % [device, button_name]
        if phase == "release":
            _joy_button_event(device, joy_index, false)
            _joy_buttons.erase(key)
        else:
            _joy_button_event(device, joy_index, true)
            _joy_buttons[key] = true
        Input.flush_buffered_events()


static func _release_tap(step: Dictionary) -> void:
    if step.is_empty():
        return
    _apply_phase(step, "release")


static func _parse(step: Dictionary, index: int) -> Dictionary:
    var kind := str(step.get("type", ""))
    var phase := str(step.get("phase", "tap"))
    if not phase in ["tap", "press", "release"]:
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: phase must be tap, press, or release." % index)
    match kind:
        "key":
            var key := str(step.get("key", ""))
            var code := OS.find_keycode_from_string(key)
            if key.is_empty() or (code & KEY_CODE_MASK) == 0:
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: unknown key '%s'." % [index, key])
            return {"step": {"type": kind, "phase": phase, "code": code, "key": key}}
        "text":
            if str(step.get("text", "")).is_empty():
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: text is empty." % index)
            return {"step": {"type": kind, "text": str(step["text"])}}
        "mouse_move", "mouse_button", "touch":
            if not step.has("x") or not step.has("y"):
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: %s needs x and y." % [index, kind])
            var button := str(step.get("button", "left"))
            if kind == "mouse_button" and not MOUSE_BUTTONS.has(button):
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: unknown mouse button '%s'." % [index, button])
            return {"step": {
                "type": kind, "phase": phase, "x": float(step["x"]), "y": float(step["y"]),
                "button": button, "index": int(step.get("index", 0)),
            }}
        "joy_button":
            var button_name := str(step.get("button", ""))
            if not JOY_BUTTONS.has(button_name):
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: unknown joypad button '%s'." % [index, button_name])
            return {"step": {"type": kind, "phase": phase, "button": button_name, "device": int(step.get("device", 0))}}
        "joy_axis":
            var axis := str(step.get("axis", ""))
            if not JOY_AXES.has(axis):
                return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: unknown joypad axis '%s'." % [index, axis])
            return {"step": {"type": kind, "axis": axis, "value": clampf(float(step.get("value", 0.0)), -1.0, 1.0), "device": int(step.get("device", 0))}}
        "wait":
            return {"step": {"type": kind, "frames": clampi(int(step.get("frames", 1)), 0, 600)}}
        _:
            return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "steps[%d]: unknown type '%s'." % [index, kind])


static func _run(rt: Node, step: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var kind := str(step["type"])
    match kind:
        "key":
            await _key(rt, int(step["code"]), str(step["phase"]))
        "text":
            for character in str(step["text"]):
                _unicode(character.unicode_at(0), true)
                Input.flush_buffered_events()
                await rt.get_tree().process_frame
                _unicode(character.unicode_at(0), false)
                Input.flush_buffered_events()
        "mouse_move":
            _mouse_motion(float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
            Input.flush_buffered_events()
        "mouse_button":
            await _mouse_button(rt, str(step["button"]), str(step["phase"]), float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
        "touch":
            await _touch(rt, int(step["index"]), str(step["phase"]), float(step["x"]), float(step["y"]))
        "joy_button":
            await _joy_button(rt, str(step["button"]), str(step["phase"]), int(step["device"]))
        "joy_axis":
            _joy_axis(str(step["axis"]), float(step["value"]), int(step["device"]))
            Input.flush_buffered_events()
        "wait":
            for _i in int(step["frames"]):
                await rt.get_tree().process_frame
    return {"step": {"type": kind, "ok": true}}


static func _key(rt: Node, code: int, phase: String) -> void:
    if phase == "release":
        _key_event(code, false)
        _keys.erase(code)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
        return
    _key_event(code, true)
    _keys[code] = true
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    if phase == "tap":
        await rt.get_tree().process_frame
        _key_event(code, false)
        _keys.erase(code)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()


static func _key_event(code: int, pressed: bool) -> void:
    var event := InputEventKey.new()
    event.keycode = code & KEY_CODE_MASK
    event.physical_keycode = event.keycode
    event.pressed = pressed
    event.echo = false
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _unicode(code: int, pressed: bool) -> void:
    var event := InputEventKey.new()
    event.unicode = code
    event.keycode = code
    event.pressed = pressed
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _mouse_motion(x: float, y: float) -> void:
    var event := InputEventMouseMotion.new()
    event.position = Vector2(x, y)
    event.global_position = event.position
    event.relative = event.position - _mouse_pos
    _mouse_pos = event.position
    Input.warp_mouse(event.position)
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _mouse_button(rt: Node, button: String, phase: String, x: float, y: float) -> void:
    _mouse_motion(x, y)
    var index := int(MOUSE_BUTTONS[button])
    if phase == "release":
        _mouse_event(index, false, x, y)
        _mouse.erase(button)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
        return
    _mouse_event(index, true, x, y)
    _mouse[button] = true
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    if phase == "tap":
        await rt.get_tree().process_frame
        _mouse_event(index, false, x, y)
        _mouse.erase(button)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()


static func _mouse_event(index: int, pressed: bool, x: float, y: float) -> void:
    var event := InputEventMouseButton.new()
    event.button_index = index
    event.pressed = pressed
    event.position = Vector2(x, y)
    event.global_position = event.position
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _touch(rt: Node, index: int, phase: String, x: float, y: float) -> void:
    if phase == "release":
        _touch_event(index, false, x, y)
        _touches.erase(index)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
        return
    _touch_event(index, true, x, y)
    _touches[index] = Vector2(x, y)
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    if phase == "tap":
        await rt.get_tree().process_frame
        _touch_event(index, false, x, y)
        _touches.erase(index)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()


static func _touch_event(index: int, pressed: bool, x: float, y: float) -> void:
    var event := InputEventScreenTouch.new()
    event.index = index
    event.pressed = pressed
    event.position = Vector2(x, y)
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _joy_button(rt: Node, button: String, phase: String, device: int) -> void:
    var index := int(JOY_BUTTONS[button])
    var key := "%d:%s" % [device, button]
    if phase == "release":
        _joy_button_event(device, index, false)
        _joy_buttons.erase(key)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()
        return
    _joy_button_event(device, index, true)
    _joy_buttons[key] = true
    Input.flush_buffered_events()
    if phase == "tap":
        await rt.get_tree().process_frame
        _joy_button_event(device, index, false)
        _joy_buttons.erase(key)
        Input.flush_buffered_events()


static func _joy_button_event(device: int, button: int, pressed: bool) -> void:
    var event := InputEventJoypadButton.new()
    event.device = device
    event.button_index = button
    event.pressed = pressed
    Input.parse_input_event(event)


static func _joy_axis(axis: String, value: float, device: int) -> void:
    var event := InputEventJoypadMotion.new()
    event.device = device
    event.axis = int(JOY_AXES[axis])
    event.axis_value = value
    Input.parse_input_event(event)
    var key := "%d:%s" % [device, axis]
    if is_zero_approx(value):
        _joy_axes.erase(key)
    else:
        _joy_axes[key] = value


static func _release_everything() -> Dictionary:
    var keys: Array = _keys.keys()
    for code: Variant in keys:
        _key_event(int(code), false)
    _keys.clear()
    var buttons: Array = _mouse.keys()
    for button: Variant in buttons:
        _mouse_event(int(MOUSE_BUTTONS[str(button)]), false, _mouse_pos.x, _mouse_pos.y)
    _mouse.clear()
    var joy: Array = _joy_buttons.keys()
    for key: Variant in joy:
        var parts := str(key).split(":")
        _joy_button_event(int(parts[0]), int(JOY_BUTTONS[parts[1]]), false)
    _joy_buttons.clear()
    var axes: Array = _joy_axes.keys()
    for key: Variant in axes:
        var parts := str(key).split(":")
        _joy_axis(parts[1], 0.0, int(parts[0]))
    _joy_axes.clear()
    var touches: Array = _touches.keys()
    for index: Variant in touches:
        var at: Vector2 = _touches[index]
        _touch_event(int(index), false, at.x, at.y)
    _touches.clear()
    return {"keys": keys.size(), "mouse": buttons.size(), "joy_buttons": joy.size(), "joy_axes": axes.size(), "touches": touches.size()}


static func _held_summary() -> Dictionary:
    return {
        "keys": _keys.size(),
        "mouse": _mouse.size(),
        "joy_buttons": _joy_buttons.size(),
        "joy_axes": _joy_axes.size(),
        "touches": _touches.size(),
    }
```

`addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_live.gd`:

```gd
extends RefCounted
## Live scene inspection. Property writes and method calls are intentionally narrow:
## `script` cannot be set, and a method runs only when the caller lists it in `permit`
## and it is not on the denylist (no eval, no quit, no trampoline through call/rpc).

const R := preload("rt_result.gd")
const DENY: Array[String] = [
    "quit", "set_script", "call", "callv", "call_deferred", "rpc", "rpc_id", "free",
]
const MAX_NODES := 400
const MAX_DEPTH := 32


static func methods() -> Dictionary:
    return {
        "game.tree": {"fn": tree},
        "game.get": {"fn": get_node},
        "game.set": {"fn": set_props},
        "game.call": {"fn": call_method},
        "game.signals": {"fn": signals_of},
        "game.observe": {"fn": observe},
        "game.snapshot": {"fn": snapshot},
    }


static func tree(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var root_path := str(params.get("root", ".")).strip_edges()
    var root: Node = rt.get_tree().root if root_path == "/root" else rt.find_node(root_path)
    if root == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "No node at '%s'." % root_path)
    var limit := clampi(int(params.get("max_nodes", 150)), 1, MAX_NODES)
    var depth_limit := int(params.get("depth", MAX_DEPTH))
    var name_filter := str(params.get("name", "")).to_lower()
    var class_filter := str(params.get("class", ""))
    var group_filter := str(params.get("group", ""))
    var text_filter := str(params.get("text", "")).to_lower()
    var nodes: Array = []
    _walk(rt, root, 0, depth_limit, limit, name_filter, class_filter, group_filter, text_filter, nodes)
    return R.ok({
        "scene": rt.scene_root().scene_file_path if rt.scene_root() != null else "",
        "count": nodes.size(),
        "truncated": nodes.size() >= limit,
        "nodes": nodes,
    })


static func _walk(rt: Node, node: Node, depth: int, depth_limit: int, limit: int, name_filter: String, class_filter: String, group_filter: String, text_filter: String, into: Array) -> void:
    if into.size() >= limit or depth > depth_limit:
        return
    var include := true
    if not name_filter.is_empty() and not String(node.name).to_lower().contains(name_filter):
        include = false
    if include and not class_filter.is_empty() and not node.is_class(class_filter) and node.get_class() != class_filter:
        include = false
    if include and not group_filter.is_empty() and not node.is_in_group(group_filter):
        include = false
    var visible_text := _visible_text(node)
    if include and not text_filter.is_empty() and not visible_text.to_lower().contains(text_filter):
        include = false
    if include:
        var item := {
            "path": rt.path_of(node),
            "name": String(node.name),
            "class": node.get_class(),
            "text": visible_text,
        }
        if node.get_script() != null:
            item["script"] = (node.get_script() as Script).resource_path
        if node is Node2D:
            item["position"] = [node.position.x, node.position.y]
        elif node is Node3D:
            item["position"] = [node.position.x, node.position.y, node.position.z]
        if node is CanvasItem:
            item["visible"] = node.visible
        into.append(item)
    for child: Node in node.get_children():
        _walk(rt, child, depth + 1, depth_limit, limit, name_filter, class_filter, group_filter, text_filter, into)


static func _visible_text(node: Node) -> String:
    if node is Button or node is Label or node is LineEdit or node is TextEdit or node is RichTextLabel:
        return str(node.text)
    return ""


static func get_node(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var node := _need(rt, params)
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node not found at '%s'." % str(params.get("path", ".")))
    var wanted: Variant = params.get("properties", [])
    var props := {}
    if wanted is Array and not (wanted as Array).is_empty():
        for key: Variant in wanted:
            var name := str(key)
            if name == "script":
                continue
            props[name] = rt.encode(node.get(name))
    else:
        for info: Dictionary in node.get_property_list():
            if int(info.get("usage", 0)) & PROPERTY_USAGE_SCRIPT_VARIABLE == 0 and int(info.get("usage", 0)) & PROPERTY_USAGE_EDITOR == 0:
                continue
            var name := str(info.get("name", ""))
            if name.is_empty() or name == "script" or name.begins_with("_"):
                continue
            var value: Variant = node.get(name)
            var fallback: Variant = node.property_get_revert(name) if node.property_can_revert(name) else null
            if value == fallback and not (wanted is Array and (wanted as Array).has("*")):
                continue
            props[name] = rt.encode(value)
    var result := {
        "path": rt.path_of(node),
        "class": node.get_class(),
        "properties": props,
        "groups": node.get_groups(),
    }
    if node.get_script() != null:
        result["script"] = (node.get_script() as Script).resource_path
    if bool(params.get("signals", false)):
        result["signals"] = _signal_list(node)
    if bool(params.get("methods", false)):
        var methods: Array = []
        for info: Dictionary in node.get_method_list():
            var name := str(info.get("name", ""))
            if not name.begins_with("_") and not name in DENY:
                methods.append(name)
        result["methods"] = methods
    return R.ok(result)


static func set_props(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var node := _need(rt, params)
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node not found at '%s'." % str(params.get("path", ".")))
    var properties: Variant = params.get("properties", {})
    if not properties is Dictionary or (properties as Dictionary).is_empty():
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "game.set: 'properties' must be a non-empty object.")
    var readback := {}
    for key: Variant in properties:
        var name := str(key)
        if name == "script" or name.begins_with("_") or name.contains(":"):
            return R.err(R.DENIED, "Refusing to set '%s'." % name)
        var current: Variant = node.get(name)
        var decoded: Variant = _coerce(properties[key], typeof(current) if current != null else TYPE_NIL)
        node.set(name, decoded)
        readback[name] = rt.encode(node.get(name))
    return R.ok({"path": rt.path_of(node), "properties": readback})


static func call_method(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var node := _need(rt, params)
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node not found at '%s'." % str(params.get("path", ".")))
    var method := str(params.get("method", ""))
    if not method.is_valid_identifier():
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "Method '%s' is not an identifier." % method)
    if method in DENY or method.begins_with("_"):
        return R.err(R.DENIED, "Method '%s' is not allowed." % method)
    var permit: Variant = params.get("permit", [])
    if not permit is Array or not (permit as Array).has(method):
        return R.err(R.DENIED, "Method '%s' was not explicitly permitted. Pass permit: [\"%s\"]." % [method, method])
    if not node.has_method(method):
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node '%s' has no method '%s'." % [rt.path_of(node), method])
    if method == "queue_free" and (node == rt.get_tree().root or node == rt or node == rt.scene_root()):
        return R.err(R.DENIED, "Refusing to free the scene root or the runtime.")
    var raw: Variant = params.get("args", [])
    if raw == null:
        raw = []
    if not raw is Array:
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "game.call: 'args' must be an array.")
    var arg_types: Array = []
    for info: Dictionary in node.get_method_list():
        if str(info.get("name", "")) == method:
            for arg_info: Dictionary in info.get("args", []):
                arg_types.append(int(arg_info.get("type", TYPE_NIL)))
            break
    var args: Array = []
    var index := 0
    for item: Variant in raw:
        var expected := TYPE_NIL
        if index < arg_types.size():
            expected = int(arg_types[index])
        var coerced: Variant = _coerce(item, expected)
        if expected != TYPE_NIL and typeof(coerced) != expected and not _type_compatible(coerced, expected):
            return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "Argument %d for '%s' is %s; expected %s." % [index, method, type_string(typeof(coerced)), type_string(expected)])
        args.append(coerced)
        index += 1
    var value: Variant = node.callv(method, args)
    return R.ok({"path": rt.path_of(node), "method": method, "value": rt.encode(value)})


static func signals_of(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var node := _need(rt, params)
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node not found at '%s'." % str(params.get("path", ".")))
    return R.ok({"path": rt.path_of(node), "signals": _signal_list(node)})


static func observe(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var node := _need(rt, params)
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node not found at '%s'." % str(params.get("path", ".")))
    var names: Variant = params.get("signals", [])
    if not names is Array or (names as Array).is_empty():
        return R.err(R.BAD_PARAMS, "game.observe: 'signals' must be a non-empty array of signal names.")
    var frames := clampi(int(params.get("frames", 1)), 0, 600)
    var events: Array = []
    var connected: Array = []
    for entry: Variant in names:
        var sig_name := str(entry)
        if not node.has_signal(sig_name):
            _disconnect_all(node, connected)
            return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "Node '%s' has no signal '%s'." % [rt.path_of(node), sig_name])
        var hook := func() -> void:
            events.append({"signal": sig_name, "frame": Engine.get_process_frames()})
        var err := node.connect(sig_name, hook)
        if err != OK:
            _disconnect_all(node, connected)
            return R.err(R.GODOT_ERROR, "Could not observe '%s': %s." % [sig_name, error_string(err)])
        connected.append({"signal": sig_name, "hook": hook})
    var state := {"left": frames, "node": node, "connected": connected, "events": events, "path": rt.path_of(node)}
    return {"_pending": true, "_tick": func() -> bool:
        if int(state["left"]) > 0:
            state["left"] = int(state["left"]) - 1
            return true
        _disconnect_all(state["node"], state["connected"])
        rt.finish_job(R.ok({"path": state["path"], "frames": frames, "events": state["events"]}))
        return false
    }


static func snapshot(rt: Node, _params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var root: Node = rt.scene_root() as Node
    var children: Array = []
    if root != null:
        for child: Node in root.get_children():
            children.append({"name": String(child.name), "class": child.get_class()})
    return R.ok({
        "scene": root.scene_file_path if root != null else "",
        "scene_name": String(root.name) if root != null else "",
        "frame": Engine.get_process_frames(),
        "paused": rt.get_tree().paused,
        "children": children,
    })


static func _need(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Node:
    return rt.find_node(str(params.get("path", ".")))


static func _signal_list(node: Node) -> Array:
    var out: Array = []
    for info: Dictionary in node.get_signal_list():
        var name := str(info.get("name", ""))
        var connections: Array = []
        for connection: Dictionary in node.get_signal_connection_list(name):
            var callable: Callable = connection.get("callable", Callable())
            connections.append({
                "method": callable.get_method() if callable.is_valid() else "",
                "flags": connection.get("flags", 0),
            })
        out.append({"name": name, "connections": connections})
    return out


static func _disconnect_all(node: Node, connected: Array) -> void:
    for entry: Dictionary in connected:
        var hook: Callable = entry["hook"]
        if is_instance_valid(node) and node.is_connected(entry["signal"], hook):
            node.disconnect(entry["signal"], hook)


static func _type_compatible(value: Variant, expected: int) -> bool:
    if expected == TYPE_FLOAT and (value is float or value is int):
        return true
    if expected == TYPE_INT and value is int:
        return true
    return typeof(value) == expected


static func _coerce(value: Variant, expected: int) -> Variant:
    if (expected == TYPE_INT or expected == TYPE_FLOAT) and (value is float or value is int):
        return int(value) if expected == TYPE_INT else float(value)
    if value is Array:
        var items: Array = value
        if expected == TYPE_VECTOR2 and items.size() >= 2:
            return Vector2(float(items[0]), float(items[1]))
        if expected == TYPE_VECTOR3 and items.size() >= 3:
            return Vector3(float(items[0]), float(items[1]), float(items[2]))
        if expected == TYPE_COLOR and items.size() >= 3:
            var alpha := float(items[3]) if items.size() > 3 else 1.0
            return Color(float(items[0]), float(items[1]), float(items[2]), alpha)
        if expected == TYPE_NIL and items.size() == 2 and (items[0] is float or items[0] is int):
            return Vector2(float(items[0]), float(items[1]))
    if value is String and (expected == TYPE_VECTOR2 or expected == TYPE_VECTOR3 or expected == TYPE_COLOR):
        var parsed: Variant = str_to_var(value)
        if parsed != null:
            return parsed
    return value

```

`addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_output.gd`:

```gd
extends RefCounted
## Captures print(), push_warning() and push_error() while a debugger is attached.
## Godot 4.5+ OS.add_logger. The buffer is capped so a chatty game cannot grow without bound.

const R := preload("rt_result.gd")
const MAX_LINES := 2000
const MAX_TEXT := 2000

static var _logger: OutputLogger = null


static func methods() -> Dictionary:
    return {"rt.output": {"fn": output}}


static func install() -> void:
    if _logger != null:
        return
    _logger = OutputLogger.new()
    OS.add_logger(_logger)


static func uninstall() -> void:
    if _logger == null:
        return
    OS.remove_logger(_logger)
    _logger = null


static func output(_rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    if _logger == null:
        return R.err(R.UNSUPPORTED, "Output capture is not active.")
    var since := int(params.get("since", 0))
    var limit := clampi(int(params.get("limit", 200)), 1, MAX_LINES)
    var level := str(params.get("level", "all"))
    var snap: Dictionary = _logger.snapshot()
    var page: Array = []
    for entry: Dictionary in snap["entries"]:
        if int(entry["seq"]) < since:
            continue
        if level == "error" and entry["level"] != "error":
            continue
        if level == "warning" and entry["level"] == "info":
            continue
        page.append(entry)
        if page.size() >= limit:
            break
    var next_seq := since
    if not page.is_empty():
        next_seq = int(page.back()["seq"]) + 1
    return R.ok({
        "lines": page,
        "next": next_seq,
        "error_count": snap["error_count"],
        "warning_count": snap["warning_count"],
    })


class OutputLogger extends Logger:
    var _mutex := Mutex.new()
    var _entries: Array = []
    var _seq := 0
    var _errors := 0
    var _warnings := 0

    func _log_message(message: String, error: bool) -> void:
        _add("info", ("[stderr] " if error else "") + message.trim_suffix("\n"), [])

    func _log_error(function: String, file: String, line: int, code: String, rationale: String, _editor_notify: bool, error_type: int, script_backtraces: Array[ScriptBacktrace]) -> void:
        var warning := error_type == ERROR_TYPE_WARNING
        var text := rationale if not rationale.is_empty() else code
        if not rationale.is_empty() and not code.is_empty() and code != rationale:
            text = "%s: %s" % [code, rationale]
        var frames: Array = []
        for backtrace: ScriptBacktrace in script_backtraces:
            for index in backtrace.get_frame_count():
                frames.append("%s:%d in %s()" % [backtrace.get_frame_file(index), backtrace.get_frame_line(index), backtrace.get_frame_function(index)])
        if frames.is_empty() and not file.is_empty():
            frames.append("%s:%d in %s()" % [file, line, function])
        _add("warning" if warning else "error", text, frames)

    func _add(level: String, text: String, frames: Array) -> void:
        if text.length() > MAX_TEXT:
            text = text.substr(0, MAX_TEXT)
        _mutex.lock()
        _entries.append({"seq": _seq, "level": level, "text": text, "stack": frames})
        _seq += 1
        if level == "error":
            _errors += 1
        elif level == "warning":
            _warnings += 1
        while _entries.size() > MAX_LINES:
            _entries.pop_front()
        _mutex.unlock()

    func snapshot() -> Dictionary:
        _mutex.lock()
        var copy := _entries.duplicate(true)
        var errors := _errors
        var warnings := _warnings
        _mutex.unlock()
        return {"entries": copy, "error_count": errors, "warning_count": warnings}

```

```gd
extends RefCounted
## Result helpers for the in-game runtime. Every method returns {"result"} or {"error"}.

const BAD_PARAMS := "BAD_PARAMS"
const NOT_FOUND := "NOT_FOUND"
const UNSUPPORTED := "UNSUPPORTED"
const GODOT_ERROR := "GODOT_ERROR"
const DENIED := "DENIED"


static func err(code: String, message: String, data: Variant = null) -> Dictionary:
    var body := {"code": code, "message": message}
    if data != null:
        body["data"] = data
    return {"error": body}


static func ok(result: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    return {"result": result}


static func is_err(value: Variant) -> bool:
    return value is Dictionary and (value as Dictionary).has("error")
```

`addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_view.gd`:

```gd
extends RefCounted
## Screenshots of the running game. The image is returned as base64 PNG inside the
## debugger reply. No file is written unless the caller passes save_to, and save_to is
## rejected: this runtime keeps captures in memory.

const R := preload("rt_result.gd")
const DEFAULT_MAX := 768
const MAX_MAX := 4096


static func methods() -> Dictionary:
    return {"game.screenshot": {"fn": screenshot}}


static func screenshot(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    if DisplayServer.get_name() == "headless":
        return R.err(R.UNSUPPORTED, "This game is headless, so there is no image to capture. Start it without headless mode.")
    var picked := _pick(rt, params)
    if R.is_err(picked):
        return picked
    var viewport: Viewport = picked["viewport"]
    var restore_camera: Camera3D = picked.get("restore_camera")
    var made_current: Camera3D = picked.get("made_current")
    var made_current_2d: Camera2D = picked.get("made_current_2d")
    var previous_2d: bool = bool(picked.get("previous_2d", false))
    var state := {
        "waited": false,
        "viewport": viewport,
        "restore_camera": restore_camera,
        "made_current": made_current,
        "made_current_2d": made_current_2d,
        "previous_2d": previous_2d,
        "params": params,
        "label": picked.get("label", "viewport"),
    }
    return {"_pending": true, "_tick": func() -> bool: return _finish_shot(rt, state)}


static func _finish_shot(rt: Node, state: Dictionary) -> bool:
    if not bool(state["waited"]):
        state["waited"] = true
        return true
    var viewport: Viewport = state["viewport"]
    var made_current: Camera3D = state.get("made_current")
    var restore_camera: Camera3D = state.get("restore_camera")
    var made_current_2d: Camera2D = state.get("made_current_2d")
    var previous_2d: bool = bool(state.get("previous_2d", false))
    if not is_instance_valid(viewport):
        rt.finish_job(R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "The viewport was freed before the capture."))
        return false
    var texture := viewport.get_texture()
    var image: Image = texture.get_image() if texture != null else null
    if made_current != null and is_instance_valid(made_current):
        made_current.current = false
    if restore_camera != null and is_instance_valid(restore_camera):
        restore_camera.current = true
    if made_current_2d != null and is_instance_valid(made_current_2d):
        made_current_2d.enabled = previous_2d
    if image == null or image.is_empty():
        rt.finish_job(R.err(R.GODOT_ERROR, "The viewport returned no image."))
        return false
    image.convert(Image.FORMAT_RGBA8)
    var params: Dictionary = state["params"]
    var max_side := clampi(int(params.get("max_dimension", DEFAULT_MAX)), 16, MAX_MAX)
    var size := image.get_size()
    var longest := maxi(size.x, size.y)
    if longest > max_side and longest > 0:
        var scale := float(max_side) / float(longest)
        image.resize(maxi(int(size.x * scale), 1), maxi(int(size.y * scale), 1), Image.INTERPOLATE_BILINEAR)
    var bytes := image.save_png_to_buffer()
    if bytes.is_empty() or bytes.size() < 8 or bytes[0] != 0x89:
        rt.finish_job(R.err(R.GODOT_ERROR, "PNG encoding produced no image."))
        return false
    rt.finish_job(R.ok({
        "mime_type": "image/png",
        "data": Marshalls.raw_to_base64(bytes),
        "width": image.get_width(),
        "height": image.get_height(),
        "source": state["label"],
    }))
    return false


static func _pick(rt: Node, params: Dictionary) -> Dictionary:
    var camera_path := str(params.get("camera", "")).strip_edges()
    var node_path := str(params.get("node", "")).strip_edges()
    var path := camera_path if not camera_path.is_empty() else node_path
    if path.is_empty():
        return {"viewport": rt.get_tree().root, "label": "main window"}
    var node: Node = rt.find_node(path) as Node
    if node == null:
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "No node at '%s' to capture." % path)
    if node is Camera3D:
        var camera := node as Camera3D
        var viewport := camera.get_viewport()
        var previous := viewport.get_camera_3d()
        if previous != camera:
            camera.current = true
        return {"viewport": viewport, "label": rt.path_of(camera), "made_current": camera if previous != camera else null, "restore_camera": previous if previous != camera else null}
    if node is Camera2D:
        var camera_2d := node as Camera2D
        var was_enabled := camera_2d.enabled
        if not was_enabled:
            camera_2d.enabled = true
            camera_2d.make_current()
        return {"viewport": camera_2d.get_viewport(), "label": rt.path_of(camera_2d), "made_current_2d": camera_2d if not was_enabled else null, "previous_2d": was_enabled}
    if node is Viewport:
        return {"viewport": node, "label": rt.path_of(node)}
    if node is SubViewportContainer:
        for child: Node in node.get_children():
            if child is SubViewport:
                return {"viewport": child, "label": rt.path_of(child)}
        return R.err(R.NOT_FOUND, "SubViewportContainer '%s' has no SubViewport." % path)
    return {"viewport": node.get_viewport(), "label": rt.path_of(node)}

```

```uid
uid://jqiv1ytodimr
```

`examples/fake_gh.rs`:

```rs
//! A stand-in for the GitHub command-line client, used by the feedback tests. It records how it
//! was called and answers as its environment says. It is not part of the server and is not built
//! with it.
//!
//! Variables (all optional):
//! - `FAKE_GH_LOG`: file that receives one `[call]` block per run, one argument per line.
//! - `FAKE_GH_BODY`: file that receives standard input of `issue create`, or the content of the
//!   file named by `--body-file` for `issue comment`; a file it cannot open fails the run, as gh does.
//! - `FAKE_GH_LIST_STDOUT`, `FAKE_GH_LIST_STDERR`, `FAKE_GH_LIST_EXIT`, `FAKE_GH_LIST_DELAY_MS`.
//! - `FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT`, `FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDERR`, `FAKE_GH_CREATE_EXIT`, `FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS`.
//! - `FAKE_GH_LIST_HIT_URL`: `issue list` answers with one hit at this address whose body holds the
//!   fingerprint it was asked to search for (unless `FAKE_GH_LIST_STDOUT` is set).
//!
//! An argument that contains `FAKE_GH_SLEEP_<milliseconds>` makes the run take that long, so a
//! test can slow down one call without touching the environment of the process under test.

use std::io::{Read, Write};
use std::time::Duration;

fn var(name: &str) -> Option<String> {
    std::env::var(name).ok()
}

fn main() {
    let args: Vec<String> = std::env::args().skip(1).collect();

    if let Some(log) = var("FAKE_GH_LOG") {
        let mut entry = String::from("[call]\n");
        for arg in &args {
            entry.push_str(arg);
            entry.push('\n');
        }
        if let Ok(mut file) = std::fs::OpenOptions::new()
            .create(true)
            .append(true)
            .open(log)
        {
            let _ = file.write_all(entry.as_bytes());
        }
    }

    let (prefix, delay, default_stdout) = match (
        args.first().map(String::as_str),
        args.get(1).map(String::as_str),
    ) {
        (Some("issue"), Some("list")) => ("FAKE_GH_LIST", "FAKE_GH_LIST_DELAY_MS", "[]"),
        (Some("issue"), Some("create")) => {
            let mut input = String::new();
            let _ = std::io::stdin().read_to_string(&mut input);
            if let Some(path) = var("FAKE_GH_BODY") {
                let _ = std::fs::write(path, input);
            }
            (
                "FAKE_GH_CREATE",
                "FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS",
                "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/1\n",
            )
        }
        (Some("issue"), Some("comment")) => {
            let file = args
                .iter()
                .position(|arg| arg == "--body-file")
                .and_then(|index| args.get(index + 1));
            if let Some(file) = file {
                match std::fs::read_to_string(file) {
                    Ok(text) => {
                        if let Some(path) = var("FAKE_GH_BODY") {
                            let _ = std::fs::write(path, text);
                        }
                    }
                    Err(err) => {
                        eprintln!("open {file}: {err}");
                        std::process::exit(1);
                    }
                }
            }
            (
                "FAKE_GH_COMMENT",
                "FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS",
                "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/1#issuecomment-1
",
            )
        }
        _ => {
            eprintln!("fake gh: unsupported arguments");
            std::process::exit(64);
        }
    };

    let marker = "FAKE_GH_SLEEP_";
    let requested = args.iter().find_map(|arg| {
        let digits: String = arg
            .split_once(marker)?
            .1
            .chars()
            .take_while(char::is_ascii_digit)
            .collect();
        digits.parse::<u64>().ok()
    });
    let configured = var(delay).and_then(|text| text.parse::<u64>().ok());
    if let Some(millis) = requested.or(configured) {
        std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(millis));
    }
    let stdout = match (
        var(&format!("{prefix}_STDOUT")),
        var("FAKE_GH_LIST_HIT_URL"),
    ) {
        (Some(text), _) => text,
        (None, Some(url)) if prefix == "FAKE_GH_LIST" => {
            let fingerprint = args
                .iter()
                .position(|arg| arg == "--search")
                .and_then(|index| args.get(index + 1))
                .and_then(|query| query.split_whitespace().next())
                .unwrap_or_default();
            format!(
                r#"[{{"number":7,"title":"existing","url":"{url}","body":"Fingerprint: {fingerprint}"}}]"#
            )
        }
        _ => default_stdout.to_string(),
    };
    print!("{stdout}");
    eprint!("{}", var(&format!("{prefix}_STDERR")).unwrap_or_default());
    let code = var(&format!("{prefix}_EXIT"))
        .and_then(|text| text.parse::<i32>().ok())
        .unwrap_or(0);
    let _ = std::io::stdout().flush();
    std::process::exit(code);
}

```

`src/bridge.rs`:

```rs
use crate::error::{McpError, Result};
use crate::util::lock;
use futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex as StdMutex};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::net::TcpStream;
use tokio::sync::{Mutex, mpsc, oneshot};
use tokio::time::timeout;
use tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::Message;
use tokio_tungstenite::{WebSocketStream, client_async};
use tracing::{debug, info, warn};
use uuid::Uuid;

/// The bridge only ever listens on loopback, so a closed port answers (or times out) fast.
const CONNECT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_millis(300);
const HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(2);
const DEFAULT_COMMAND_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(15);
/// After a failed connect, further attempts within this window fail immediately.
const RETRY_AFTER_FAILURE: Duration = Duration::from_millis(500);

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BridgeCommand {
    #[serde(rename = "commandId")]
    pub command_id: String,
    #[serde(rename = "type")]
    pub command_type: String,
    pub params: Value,
    #[serde(default, skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub token: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct BridgeResponse {
    #[serde(rename = "commandId")]
    pub command_id: String,
    pub status: String,
    pub result: Option<Value>,
    pub error: Option<String>,
}

type Pending = Arc<StdMutex<HashMap<String, oneshot::Sender<Result<Value>>>>>;
type EventSubs = Arc<StdMutex<Vec<mpsc::UnboundedSender<Value>>>>;

#[derive(Debug)]
struct Connection {
    outbound: mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>,
    pending: Pending,
    alive: Arc<AtomicBool>,
    token: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct State {
    connection: Option<Arc<Connection>>,
    last_failure: Option<(Instant, String)>,
}

/// Client for the editor plugin's WebSocket bridge.
///
/// Keeps one connection open, multiplexes concurrent commands over it by command id, and
/// reconnects transparently after the editor restarts.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct BridgeClient {
    port: u16,
    command_timeout: Duration,
    state: Arc<Mutex<State>>,
    events: EventSubs,
}

impl BridgeClient {
    pub fn new(port: u16) -> Self {
        Self {
            port,
            command_timeout: DEFAULT_COMMAND_TIMEOUT,
            state: Arc::default(),
            events: EventSubs::default(),
        }
    }

    /// Unsolicited editor messages (`{"type":"event",...}`), such as the game debugger relay.
    pub fn subscribe_events(&self) -> mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<Value> {
        let (tx, rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        lock(&self.events).push(tx);
        rx
    }

    /// Overrides how long a command may wait for the editor's answer.
    pub fn with_timeout(mut self, command_timeout: Duration) -> Self {
        self.command_timeout = command_timeout;
        self
    }

    /// The shared secret this client sends, so that text that may reach a public place can be
    /// cleaned of it.
    pub fn secret(&self) -> Option<String> {
        read_token(self.port)
    }

    /// Sends a command to the Godot Editor and waits for its response.
    pub async fn send_command(&self, cmd_type: &str, params: Value) -> Result<Value> {
        let connection = self.connection().await?;

        let command_id = Uuid::new_v4().to_string();
        let (reply_tx, reply_rx) = oneshot::channel();
        lock(&connection.pending).insert(command_id.clone(), reply_tx);
        let _cleanup = PendingGuard {
            pending: connection.pending.clone(),
            command_id: command_id.clone(),
        };
        if !connection.alive.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
            return Err(connection_closed());
        }

        let message = serde_json::to_string(&BridgeCommand {
            command_id,
            command_type: cmd_type.to_string(),
            params,
            token: connection.token.clone(),
        })?;
        connection
            .outbound
            .send(message)
            .map_err(|_| connection_closed())?;

        match timeout(self.command_timeout, reply_rx).await {
            Ok(Ok(outcome)) => outcome,
            Ok(Err(_)) => Err(McpError::Bridge(
                "The Godot Editor bridge dropped the request".into(),
            )),
            Err(_) => Err(McpError::Bridge(format!(
                "Timed out after {}s waiting for the Godot Editor to answer '{cmd_type}'",
                self.command_timeout.as_secs_f32()
            ))),
        }
    }

    async fn connection(&self) -> Result<Arc<Connection>> {
        let mut state = self.state.lock().await;

        if let Some(existing) = &state.connection {
            if existing.alive.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                return Ok(existing.clone());
            }
        }
        state.connection = None;

        if let Some((failed_at, message)) = &state.last_failure {
            if failed_at.elapsed() < RETRY_AFTER_FAILURE {
                return Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(message.clone()));
            }
        }

        match self.connect().await {
            Ok(connection) => {
                state.last_failure = None;
                state.connection = Some(connection.clone());
                Ok(connection)
            }
            Err(error) => {
                if let McpError::BridgeUnavailable(message) = &error {
                    state.last_failure = Some((Instant::now(), message.clone()));
                }
                Err(error)
            }
        }
    }

    async fn connect(&self) -> Result<Arc<Connection>> {
        let port = self.port;
        let address = format!("127.0.0.1:{port}");

        let tcp = match timeout(CONNECT_TIMEOUT, TcpStream::connect(&address)).await {
            Ok(Ok(tcp)) => tcp,
            Ok(Err(err)) => return Err(unavailable(port, &format!("connection failed: {err}"))),
            Err(_) => return Err(unavailable(port, "connection timed out")),
        };
        let url = format!("ws://{address}");
        let websocket = match timeout(HANDSHAKE_TIMEOUT, client_async(url.as_str(), tcp)).await {
            Ok(Ok((websocket, _))) => websocket,
            Ok(Err(err)) => return Err(unavailable(port, &format!("handshake failed: {err}"))),
            Err(_) => return Err(unavailable(port, "handshake timed out")),
        };

        let (outbound, queue) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let pending = Pending::default();
        let alive = Arc::new(AtomicBool::new(true));
        tokio::spawn(run_connection(
            websocket,
            queue,
            pending.clone(),
            self.events.clone(),
            alive.clone(),
        ));

        info!("Connected to the Godot Editor bridge on port {port}");
        Ok(Arc::new(Connection {
            outbound,
            pending,
            alive,
            token: read_token(port),
        }))
    }
}

/// Removes a command's reply slot when its caller stops waiting (timeout or cancellation).
struct PendingGuard {
    pending: Pending,
    command_id: String,
}

impl Drop for PendingGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        lock(&self.pending).remove(&self.command_id);
    }
}

fn unavailable(port: u16, detail: &str) -> McpError {
    McpError::BridgeUnavailable(format!("ws://127.0.0.1:{port}: {detail}"))
}

fn connection_closed() -> McpError {
    McpError::Bridge("The connection to the Godot Editor bridge was closed".into())
}

async fn run_connection(
    websocket: WebSocketStream<TcpStream>,
    mut queue: mpsc::UnboundedReceiver<String>,
    pending: Pending,
    events: EventSubs,
    alive: Arc<AtomicBool>,
) {
    let (mut sink, mut stream) = websocket.split();
    loop {
        tokio::select! {
            queued = queue.recv() => match queued {
                Some(text) => {
                    if sink.send(Message::Text(text.into())).await.is_err() {
                        break;
                    }
                }
                None => {
                    let _ = sink.close().await;
                    break;
                }
            },
            incoming = stream.next() => match incoming {
                Some(Ok(Message::Text(text))) => deliver(&pending, &events, text.as_str()),
                Some(Ok(Message::Ping(data))) => {
                    let _ = sink.send(Message::Pong(data)).await;
                }
                Some(Ok(Message::Close(_))) | Some(Err(_)) | None => break,
                Some(Ok(_)) => {}
            },
        }
    }

    alive.store(false, Ordering::Release);
    for (_, reply) in lock(&pending).drain() {
        let _ = reply.send(Err(connection_closed()));
    }
    debug!("Godot Editor bridge connection closed");
}

fn deliver(pending: &Pending, events: &EventSubs, text: &str) {
    if let Ok(value) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text) {
        if value.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) == Some("event") {
            lock(events).retain(|subscriber| subscriber.send(value.clone()).is_ok());
            return;
        }
    }
    let response: BridgeResponse = match serde_json::from_str(text) {
        Ok(response) => response,
        Err(err) => {
            warn!("Ignoring malformed bridge message: {err}");
            return;
        }
    };
    let Some(reply) = lock(pending).remove(&response.command_id) else {
        debug!("Bridge reply for unknown command {}", response.command_id);
        return;
    };
    let outcome = if response.status == "success" {
        Ok(response.result.unwrap_or(Value::Null))
    } else {
        Err(McpError::ToolExecution(
            response
                .error
                .unwrap_or_else(|| "Unknown engine error".into()),
        ))
    };
    let _ = reply.send(outcome);
}

/// Where the editor plugin publishes its per-port bridge token.
///
/// This mirrors Godot's `OS.get_data_dir()`, so the plugin and the server agree without
/// any shared configuration as long as both run as the same OS user.
pub fn token_path(port: u16) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    let data_dir = if cfg!(windows) {
        std::env::var_os("APPDATA").map(PathBuf::from)
    } else if cfg!(target_os = "macos") {
        std::env::var_os("HOME").map(|home| PathBuf::from(home).join("Library/Application Support"))
    } else {
        std::env::var_os("XDG_DATA_HOME")
            .filter(|dir| !dir.is_empty())
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .or_else(|| {
                std::env::var_os("HOME").map(|home| PathBuf::from(home).join(".local/share"))
            })
    }?;
    Some(
        data_dir
            .join("godot-mcp")
            .join(format!("bridge-{port}.token")),
    )
}

/// `GODOT_MCP_TOKEN` wins; otherwise the token file written by the plugin is used.
fn read_token(port: u16) -> Option<String> {
    let from_env = std::env::var("GODOT_MCP_TOKEN")
        .ok()
        .map(|value| value.trim().to_string())
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty());
    if from_env.is_some() {
        return from_env;
    }
    let contents = std::fs::read_to_string(token_path(port)?).ok()?;
    Some(contents.trim().to_string()).filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
}
```

`src/cli_runner.rs`:

```rs
use crate::error::{McpError, Result};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::process::Command;
use tokio::time::timeout;

const VERSION_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);
const VALIDATE_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(30);

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct CliRunner {
    godot_binary: PathBuf,
}

impl CliRunner {
    pub fn new(godot_binary: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self { godot_binary }
    }

    /// The Godot executable this runner was configured with.
    pub fn binary(&self) -> &Path {
        &self.godot_binary
    }

    /// Retrieve engine version directly via CLI
    pub async fn get_version(&self) -> Result<String> {
        let mut command = Command::new(&self.godot_binary);
        command
            .arg("--version")
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .kill_on_drop(true);
        let output = timeout(VERSION_TIMEOUT, command.output())
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                McpError::Process(format!(
                    "'{} --version' did not finish within {}s",
                    self.godot_binary.display(),
                    VERSION_TIMEOUT.as_secs()
                ))
            })?
            .map_err(|e| {
                McpError::Process(format!(
                    "Failed to invoke Godot executable at '{}': {e}",
                    self.godot_binary.display()
                ))
            })?;

        if output.status.success() {
            Ok(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim().to_string())
        } else {
            Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                "Godot --version exited with error: {}",
                String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr)
            )))
        }
    }

    /// Launch Godot Editor for a designated project directory
    pub async fn launch_editor(&self, project_path: &Path) -> Result<u32> {
        let mut cmd = Command::new(&self.godot_binary);
        cmd.arg("--editor").arg("--path").arg(project_path);
        spawn_detached(&mut cmd, "Godot Editor")
    }

    /// Run the game project or specific scene.
    ///
    /// The game's own output must never reach this process's stdout, which carries the MCP
    /// JSON-RPC stream, so all standard streams are detached.
    pub async fn run_project(&self, project_path: &Path, scene: Option<&str>) -> Result<u32> {
        let mut cmd = Command::new(&self.godot_binary);
        cmd.arg("--path").arg(project_path);
        if let Some(scene_file) = scene {
            cmd.arg(scene_file);
        }
        spawn_detached(&mut cmd, "Godot project")
    }

    /// Validate a GDScript file syntax using headless Godot check
    pub async fn validate_script(
        &self,
        project_path: Option<&Path>,
        script_path: &Path,
    ) -> Result<String> {
        let mut cmd = Command::new(&self.godot_binary);
        cmd.arg("--headless").arg("--check-only");

        if let Some(proj) = project_path {
            cmd.arg("--path").arg(proj);
        }

        cmd.arg("--script")
            .arg(script_path)
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .kill_on_drop(true);

        let output = timeout(VALIDATE_TIMEOUT, cmd.output())
            .await
            .map_err(|_| {
                McpError::Process(format!(
                    "Godot script validation did not finish within {}s",
                    VALIDATE_TIMEOUT.as_secs()
                ))
            })?
            .map_err(|e| {
                McpError::Process(format!("Failed to run Godot script validation: {e}"))
            })?;

        let stdout = strip_banner(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout));
        let stderr = strip_banner(&String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr));

        if output.status.success() && stderr.is_empty() {
            Ok("GDScript validation passed successfully with no errors.".into())
        } else {
            let mut report = Vec::new();
            if !stdout.is_empty() {
                report.push(format!("Stdout: {stdout}"));
            }
            if !stderr.is_empty() {
                report.push(format!("Stderr: {stderr}"));
            }
            if report.is_empty() {
                report.push(format!("Godot exited with {}", output.status));
            }
            Err(McpError::Validation(report.join("\n")))
        }
    }
}

fn spawn_detached(command: &mut Command, what: &str) -> Result<u32> {
    // Ctrl+C in the MCP client's terminal reaches every process attached to its console
    // (Windows) or in its foreground process group (Unix), which would close the editor with
    // unsaved work. No console and a process group of its own keep the child out of reach.
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        const DETACHED_PROCESS: u32 = 0x0000_0008;
        const CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP: u32 = 0x0000_0200;
        command.creation_flags(DETACHED_PROCESS | CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP);
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    command.process_group(0);
    #[cfg(windows)]
    make_std_handles_private();

    let child = command
        .stdin(Stdio::null())
        .stdout(Stdio::null())
        .stderr(Stdio::null())
        .spawn()
        .map_err(|e| McpError::Process(format!("Failed to spawn {what} process: {e}")))?;
    child
        .id()
        .ok_or_else(|| McpError::Process(format!("Failed to obtain {what} process ID")))
}

/// Windows lets a spawned process inherit every inheritable handle of its parent, whatever it
/// is given as its own standard streams. The server's stdin, stdout and stderr are pipes of the
/// MCP client, so a launched editor would keep them open for as long as it runs, and a client
/// that waits for the server's stderr to close would wait for the editor instead.
#[cfg(windows)]
pub(crate) fn make_std_handles_private() {
    use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::SetHandleInformation;
    use windows_sys::Win32::Foundation::{HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE};
    use windows_sys::Win32::System::Console::{
        GetStdHandle, STD_ERROR_HANDLE, STD_INPUT_HANDLE, STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE,
    };

    for which in [STD_INPUT_HANDLE, STD_OUTPUT_HANDLE, STD_ERROR_HANDLE] {
        // SAFETY: both calls only read or flag handles owned by this process. A missing or
        // invalid handle makes `SetHandleInformation` fail, which is harmless here: the
        // handle is then simply not protected, as before.
        unsafe {
            let handle = GetStdHandle(which);
            if !handle.is_null() && handle != INVALID_HANDLE_VALUE {
                SetHandleInformation(handle, HANDLE_FLAG_INHERIT, 0);
            }
        }
    }
}

/// Drops the engine's start-up banner ("Godot Engine v4.x ...") from captured output.
fn strip_banner(text: &str) -> String {
    text.lines()
        .filter(|line| !line.starts_with("Godot Engine v"))
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("\n")
        .trim()
        .to_string()
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::strip_banner;

    #[test]
    fn banner_is_removed_and_the_rest_is_kept() {
        let raw = "Godot Engine v4.8.dev7.official - https://godotengine.org\n\nSCRIPT ERROR: x\n";
        assert_eq!(strip_banner(raw), "SCRIPT ERROR: x");
        assert_eq!(strip_banner("Godot Engine v4.5\n"), "");
    }

    /// Ctrl+C in the MCP client's terminal signals its whole process group; a launched editor
    /// must not be part of it.
    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[tokio::test]
    async fn launched_processes_get_their_own_process_group() {
        use super::CliRunner;
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        use std::time::Duration;

        fn process_group_of(pid: &str) -> String {
            let output = std::process::Command::new("ps")
                .args(["-o", "pgid=", "-p", pid])
                .output()
                .expect("run ps");
            String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).trim().to_string()
        }

        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp dir");
        let report = dir.path().join("pgid.txt");
        let script = dir.path().join("fake_godot.sh");
        std::fs::write(
            &script,
            format!(
                "#!/bin/sh\nps -o pgid= -p $$ > '{0}.tmp' && mv '{0}.tmp' '{0}'\n",
                report.display()
            ),
        )
        .expect("write script");
        std::fs::set_permissions(&script, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755)).expect("chmod");

        let pid = CliRunner::new(script)
            .launch_editor(dir.path())
            .await
            .expect("spawn");

        for _ in 0..100 {
            if report.exists() {
                break;
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
        }
        let child_group = std::fs::read_to_string(&report).expect("child reported its group");
        assert_eq!(child_group.trim(), pid.to_string());
        assert_ne!(
            child_group.trim(),
            process_group_of(&std::process::id().to_string())
        );
    }
}
```

`src/config.rs`:

```rs
use clap::Parser;
use std::convert::Infallible;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use tracing::{debug, warn};

const DEFAULT_DEBUG_PORT: u16 = 9333;

#[derive(Parser, Debug, Clone)]
#[command(
    name = "godot-mcp",
    about = "High-performance Model Context Protocol (MCP) server for the Godot Game Engine",
    version
)]
pub struct Config {
    /// Path to the primary Godot executable
    #[arg(long, env = "GODOT_PATH", value_parser = parse_path)]
    pub godot_path: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// Path to the Godot console wrapper executable. The server does not use it yet; the
    /// editor end-to-end tests read it to show editor logs on Windows.
    #[arg(long, env = "GODOT_CONSOLE_PATH", value_parser = parse_path)]
    pub godot_console_path: Option<PathBuf>,

    /// WebSocket port for Godot Editor bridge communication
    #[arg(long, env = "GODOT_DEBUG_PORT", default_value = "9333", value_parser = parse_port)]
    pub debug_port: u16,

    /// Agent feedback channel: `off`, `draft` (notes and drafts only) or `full` (adds
    /// submission after the human's approval)
    #[arg(long = "feedback", env = "GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK", default_value = "full", value_parser = parse_feedback_mode)]
    pub feedback: FeedbackMode,

    /// Path of the GitHub command-line client used to submit feedback reports; `gh` on `PATH`
    /// when unset
    #[arg(long = "gh-path", env = "GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH", value_parser = parse_path)]
    pub gh_path: Option<PathBuf>,
}

/// How much of the agent feedback channel the human allows.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq, Default)]
pub enum FeedbackMode {
    /// No feedback tools and no guidance.
    Off,
    /// Notes and drafts; the agent can never publish.
    Draft,
    /// Adds submission, which the human still has to approve.
    #[default]
    Full,
}

impl FeedbackMode {
    /// Reads a mode name in any case; `None` for anything else.
    pub fn parse(raw: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match raw.trim().to_ascii_lowercase().as_str() {
            "off" => Some(Self::Off),
            "draft" => Some(Self::Draft),
            "full" => Some(Self::Full),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Off => "off",
            Self::Draft => "draft",
            Self::Full => "full",
        }
    }

    /// Whether the note and draft tools exist.
    pub fn enabled(self) -> bool {
        self != Self::Off
    }

    /// Whether the submit tool exists.
    pub fn submits(self) -> bool {
        self == Self::Full
    }
}

/// An empty value means "not set" and an unknown one is reported and ignored: a setting that
/// is wrong must never keep the server from starting.
fn parse_feedback_mode(raw: &str) -> Result<FeedbackMode, Infallible> {
    if raw.trim().is_empty() {
        return Ok(FeedbackMode::default());
    }
    Ok(FeedbackMode::parse(raw).unwrap_or_else(|| {
        warn!(
            "Ignoring GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK={raw:?}: use off, draft or full. Using {}.",
            FeedbackMode::default().as_str()
        );
        FeedbackMode::default()
    }))
}

/// Accepts any text, including an empty one: placeholders such as `GODOT_PATH=""` in a
/// template or an MCP client's `env` block mean "not configured", not "invalid".
/// (clap's built-in path parser rejects empty values and the server would not start.)
/// An empty path never exists, so the Godot binary lookup falls through to auto-detection.
fn parse_path(raw: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, Infallible> {
    Ok(PathBuf::from(raw))
}

/// Like `u16`, except that an empty value selects the default port.
fn parse_port(raw: &str) -> Result<u16, String> {
    let raw = raw.trim();
    if raw.is_empty() {
        return Ok(DEFAULT_DEBUG_PORT);
    }
    raw.parse::<u16>().map_err(|err| format!("{err}"))
}

/// Loads a `.env` file from the working directory (or a parent) if there is one.
///
/// A file that exists but cannot be parsed is reported instead of silently ignored, since
/// none of its settings would take effect. The usual cause on Windows is a path in double
/// quotes: `"C:\Godot\Godot.exe"` is read as escape sequences, `'C:\Godot\Godot.exe'` is not.
fn load_dotenv() {
    match dotenvy::dotenv() {
        Ok(path) => debug!("Loaded settings from {}", path.display()),
        Err(err) if err.not_found() => {}
        Err(err) => warn!(
            "Ignoring the .env file because it could not be parsed ({err}). \
             Put Windows paths in single quotes, e.g. GODOT_PATH='C:\\Godot\\Godot.exe'"
        ),
    }
}

impl Config {
    pub fn load() -> Self {
        load_dotenv();
        Config::parse()
    }

    /// Resolve the effective Godot binary path.
    ///
    /// `GODOT_PATH` should always be set explicitly. Official Godot builds
    /// for Windows are portable, version-named executables (e.g.
    /// `Godot_v4.8.2-stable_win64.exe`) that users extract wherever they
    /// like, so there is no single standard install location to hardcode.
    /// The scan below is a best-effort convenience for a handful of common
    /// locations — it is not a substitute for configuring `GODOT_PATH`.
    ///
    /// A configured path is always used as given, even when the file is missing: replacing it
    /// with another installed Godot could silently run a different engine version than the one
    /// the user chose. Tools that need the engine then fail naming that path. An empty value is
    /// treated as unset.
    pub fn resolve_godot_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        if let Some(path) = self
            .godot_path
            .as_ref()
            .filter(|p| !p.as_os_str().is_empty())
        {
            // A bare command name is looked up on PATH when it is run, so only a path with a
            // directory part can be judged missing here.
            let has_directory = path.parent().is_some_and(|dir| !dir.as_os_str().is_empty());
            if has_directory && !path.exists() {
                warn!(
                    "GODOT_PATH points to '{}', which does not exist. Tools that run Godot \
                     will fail until it is fixed.",
                    path.display()
                );
            }
            return path.clone();
        }

        if let Some(found) = Self::scan_for_godot_binary() {
            return found;
        }

        // Fallback to searching in PATH
        PathBuf::from("godot")
    }

    /// Scan a few common Windows install roots (Program Files, Local AppData)
    /// for an executable whose name starts with `Godot` and ends in `.exe`,
    /// looking one level into subdirectories to account for version-named
    /// folders produced by extracting the official zip release.
    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    fn scan_for_godot_binary() -> Option<PathBuf> {
        let roots = [
            std::env::var("ProgramFiles").ok(),
            std::env::var("ProgramFiles(x86)").ok(),
            std::env::var("LOCALAPPDATA")
                .ok()
                .map(|p| format!("{p}\\Programs")),
        ];

        roots
            .into_iter()
            .flatten()
            .map(PathBuf::from)
            .find_map(|root| Self::find_godot_exe_in(&root, 2))
    }

    #[cfg(target_os = "windows")]
    fn find_godot_exe_in(dir: &std::path::Path, depth: u8) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        let entries = std::fs::read_dir(dir).ok()?;
        let mut subdirs = Vec::new();
        let mut matches = Vec::new();

        for entry in entries.filter_map(|e| e.ok()) {
            let path = entry.path();
            if path.is_dir() {
                subdirs.push(path);
                continue;
            }
            let Some(name) = path.file_name().and_then(|n| n.to_str()) else {
                continue;
            };
            let lower = name.to_lowercase();
            if lower.starts_with("godot") && lower.ends_with(".exe") && !lower.contains("console") {
                matches.push(path);
            }
        }

        // Prefer the lexicographically last match: for versioned filenames
        // like `Godot_v4.8.2-stable_win64.exe` this tends to pick the newest.
        matches.sort();
        if let Some(found) = matches.pop() {
            return Some(found);
        }

        if depth == 0 {
            return None;
        }
        subdirs
            .into_iter()
            .find_map(|subdir| Self::find_godot_exe_in(&subdir, depth - 1))
    }

    /// macOS installs are conventionally a drag-and-dropped `.app` bundle,
    /// so a fixed path is a reasonable (if not guaranteed) default.
    #[cfg(target_os = "macos")]
    fn scan_for_godot_binary() -> Option<PathBuf> {
        [
            "/Applications/Godot.app/Contents/MacOS/Godot",
            "/Applications/Godot_mono.app/Contents/MacOS/Godot",
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .find(|p| p.exists())
    }

    /// Linux installs commonly land in one of these locations via a package
    /// manager or Flatpak.
    #[cfg(target_os = "linux")]
    fn scan_for_godot_binary() -> Option<PathBuf> {
        [
            "/usr/bin/godot",
            "/usr/local/bin/godot",
            "/var/lib/flatpak/exports/bin/org.godotengine.Godot",
        ]
        .into_iter()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .find(|p| p.exists())
    }

    #[cfg(not(any(target_os = "windows", target_os = "macos", target_os = "linux")))]
    fn scan_for_godot_binary() -> Option<PathBuf> {
        None
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{Config, FeedbackMode};
    use clap::Parser;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    #[test]
    fn the_feedback_mode_defaults_to_full_and_accepts_any_case() {
        assert_eq!(parse(&[]).feedback, FeedbackMode::Full);
        for (text, mode) in [
            ("off", FeedbackMode::Off),
            ("OFF", FeedbackMode::Off),
            ("Draft", FeedbackMode::Draft),
            (" full ", FeedbackMode::Full),
        ] {
            assert_eq!(parse(&["--feedback", text]).feedback, mode, "{text:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_empty_or_unknown_feedback_mode_selects_full_instead_of_stopping_the_server() {
        assert_eq!(parse(&["--feedback", ""]).feedback, FeedbackMode::Full);
        assert_eq!(parse(&["--feedback", "bogus"]).feedback, FeedbackMode::Full);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_feedback_modes_have_stable_names() {
        assert_eq!(FeedbackMode::Off.as_str(), "off");
        assert_eq!(FeedbackMode::Draft.as_str(), "draft");
        assert_eq!(FeedbackMode::Full.as_str(), "full");
        assert!(FeedbackMode::Full.submits() && !FeedbackMode::Draft.submits());
        assert!(FeedbackMode::Draft.enabled() && !FeedbackMode::Off.enabled());
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_gh_path_is_optional_and_an_empty_value_is_accepted() {
        assert_eq!(parse(&[]).gh_path, None);
        assert_eq!(
            parse(&["--gh-path", "/opt/gh"]).gh_path,
            Some(PathBuf::from("/opt/gh"))
        );
        assert_eq!(parse(&["--gh-path", ""]).gh_path, Some(PathBuf::from("")));
    }

    fn parse(args: &[&str]) -> Config {
        let mut full = vec!["godot-mcp"];
        full.extend_from_slice(args);
        Config::try_parse_from(full).expect("valid arguments")
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_configured_godot_path_is_used_even_when_the_file_is_missing() {
        // Silently substituting another installed Godot could run a different version than the
        // one the user chose; the tools report the missing file instead.
        let missing = std::env::temp_dir()
            .join("godot-mcp-test")
            .join("no-such-godot");
        let config = parse(&["--godot-path", missing.to_str().expect("utf-8 path")]);
        assert_eq!(config.resolve_godot_path(), missing);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_bare_command_name_is_kept_so_the_operating_system_can_look_it_up_on_path() {
        let config = parse(&["--godot-path", "godot-4.6"]);
        assert_eq!(config.resolve_godot_path(), PathBuf::from("godot-4.6"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_empty_godot_path_means_unset_and_still_resolves_to_something_runnable_by_name() {
        let config = parse(&["--godot-path", ""]);
        assert_ne!(config.resolve_godot_path(), PathBuf::from(""));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_unused_mcp_url_setting_is_gone() {
        assert!(Config::try_parse_from(["godot-mcp", "--mcp-url", "http://localhost:1"]).is_err());
        assert!(Config::try_parse_from(["godot-mcp", "--debug-port", "9333"]).is_ok());
    }
}
```

`src/error.rs`:
