```rs
use thiserror::Error;

#[derive(Error, Debug)]
pub enum McpError {
    /// The editor bridge cannot be reached at all (editor closed, plugin disabled, wrong port).
    #[error("Editor bridge unavailable: {0}")]
    BridgeUnavailable(String),

    /// The bridge connection broke or stalled while a command was in flight.
    #[error("Bridge connection error: {0}")]
    Bridge(String),

    #[error("Engine process error: {0}")]
    Process(String),

    #[error("Protocol error: {0}")]
    Protocol(String),

    /// The editor understood the command and reported a failure.
    #[error("Tool execution error: {0}")]
    ToolExecution(String),

    #[error("Validation error: {0}")]
    Validation(String),

    #[error("I/O error: {0}")]
    Io(#[from] std::io::Error),

    #[error("JSON serialization error: {0}")]
    Json(#[from] serde_json::Error),
}

pub type Result<T> = std::result::Result<T, McpError>;

```

`src/feedback.rs`:

```rs
//! The agent feedback channel: what an agent noticed while working, turned into one scrubbed
//! report for the maintainers. Everything here lives in memory for the life of the process and
//! nothing is sent anywhere by this module.

use std::path::PathBuf;

/// Largest prefilled new-issue link, in bytes. GitHub does not publish its limit (an over-long
/// link answers 414), so this stays well below the 8 KB most servers accept.
pub const LINK_BUDGET: usize = 6_000;

/// Ends a link whose body had to be cut.
pub const LINK_CUT_MARKER: &str =
    "\n\n... (shortened; paste the full report from the conversation)";

const REDACTED: &str = "{redacted}";
const PROJECT: &str = "{project}";
const PATH: &str = "{path}";
const HOME: &str = "~";
const ZERO_WIDTH_SPACE: char = '\u{200B}';

/// Prefixes of well-known credential formats, each followed by at least `MIN_AFTER_PREFIX`
/// characters when it is a credential.
const CREDENTIAL_PREFIXES: [&str; 8] = [
    "ghp_",
    "gho_",
    "ghs_",
    "github_pat_",
    "sk-",
    "xoxb-",
    "xoxp-",
    "AKIA",
];
const MIN_AFTER_PREFIX: usize = 8;
const LONG_RUN: usize = 32;
const UNIX_ROOTS: [&str; 7] = ["home", "Users", "root", "tmp", "var", "opt", "mnt"];

/// What the scrubber must remove besides the patterns it recognizes by shape.
#[derive(Debug, Default, Clone)]
pub struct ScrubContext {
    /// The bridge secret, removed wherever it appears.
    pub secret: Option<String>,
    /// The project directory, shown as `{project}`.
    pub project_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    /// The user's home directory, shown as `~`.
    pub home_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
}

impl ScrubContext {
    /// A context whose home directory comes from the environment.
    pub fn from_environment(secret: Option<String>, project_dir: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {
        let home_variable = if cfg!(windows) { "USERPROFILE" } else { "HOME" };
        let home_dir = std::env::var_os(home_variable)
            .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
            .map(PathBuf::from);
        Self {
            secret,
            project_dir,
            home_dir,
        }
    }
}

/// Removes everything from `text` that must not reach a public tracker: the bridge secret,
/// credentials, absolute filesystem paths and Markdown that notifies users or embeds content.
/// A second pass over the result changes nothing.
pub fn scrub(text: &str, context: &ScrubContext) -> String {
    let mut text = strip_control(text);
    if let Some(secret) = context
        .secret
        .as_deref()
        .filter(|secret| !secret.is_empty())
    {
        text = text.replace(secret, REDACTED);
    }
    if let Some(dir) = &context.project_dir {
        text = replace_directory(&text, &dir.to_string_lossy(), PROJECT, cfg!(windows));
    }
    // The home directory is shortened only once the project directory is known: a project that
    // lives under the home directory would otherwise show its own folder name as `~/Name/...`.
    // Without it, such paths are left to the absolute-path step, which hides them completely.
    if let (Some(_), Some(dir)) = (&context.project_dir, &context.home_dir) {
        text = replace_directory(&text, &dir.to_string_lossy(), HOME, cfg!(windows));
    }
    let text = redact_paths(&text);
    let text = redact_credentials(&text);
    neutralize_markdown(&text)
}

/// Drops control characters (newline and tab stay) and the bidirectional override characters
/// that can make text read differently from how it is stored.
fn strip_control(text: &str) -> String {
    text.chars()
        .filter(|&c| {
            matches!(c, '\n' | '\t')
                || !(c.is_control()
                    || matches!(c, '\u{202A}'..='\u{202E}' | '\u{2066}'..='\u{2069}'))
        })
        .collect()
}

fn is_name_char(c: char) -> bool {
    c.is_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-'
}

/// Replaces `dir`, written with either separator, by `placeholder`. A match that continues into
/// a longer name (`/game` inside `/game2`) is not a match.
fn replace_directory(text: &str, dir: &str, placeholder: &str, ignore_case: bool) -> String {
    let dir = dir.trim_end_matches(['/', '\\']);
    if !dir.contains(['/', '\\']) {
        return text.to_string();
    }
    let spellings = [
        dir.to_string(),
        dir.replace('\\', "/"),
        dir.replace('/', "\\"),
    ];
    spellings.iter().fold(text.to_string(), |text, spelling| {
        replace_bounded(&text, spelling, placeholder, ignore_case)
    })
}

fn replace_bounded(text: &str, needle: &str, replacement: &str, ignore_case: bool) -> String {
    // ASCII lowercasing keeps every byte offset, so positions found in the folded copy are
    // valid in the original.
    let (haystack, pattern) = if ignore_case {
        (text.to_ascii_lowercase(), needle.to_ascii_lowercase())
    } else {
        (text.to_string(), needle.to_string())
    };
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    let mut cursor = 0;
    while let Some(found) = haystack[cursor..].find(&pattern) {
        let start = cursor + found;
        let end = start + pattern.len();
        let separated_before = text[..start]
            .chars()
            .next_back()
            .is_none_or(|c| !is_name_char(c));
        let separated_after = text[end..].chars().next().is_none_or(|c| !is_name_char(c));
        out.push_str(&text[cursor..start]);
        if separated_before && separated_after {
            out.push_str(replacement);
        } else {
            out.push_str(&text[start..end]);
        }
        cursor = end;
    }
    out.push_str(&text[cursor..]);
    out
}

fn is_path_delimiter(c: char) -> bool {
    c.is_whitespace()
        || matches!(
            c,
            '"' | '\'' | '`' | '<' | '>' | '[' | ']' | ',' | ';' | '|' | '='
        )
}

/// Replaces absolute filesystem paths by `{path}`; `res://`, `user://` and relative paths stay.
fn redact_paths(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    let mut token_start: Option<usize> = None;
    for (index, c) in text.char_indices() {
        if is_path_delimiter(c) {
            if let Some(start) = token_start.take() {
                push_path_token(&mut out, &text[start..index]);
            }
            out.push(c);
        } else if token_start.is_none() {
            token_start = Some(index);
        }
    }
    if let Some(start) = token_start {
        push_path_token(&mut out, &text[start..]);
    }
    out
}

fn push_path_token(out: &mut String, token: &str) {
    let without_lead = token.trim_start_matches('(');
    let lead = &token[..token.len() - without_lead.len()];
    let core = without_lead.trim_end_matches(['.', ',', ':', ';', '!', '?', ')']);
    let tail = &without_lead[core.len()..];
    out.push_str(lead);
    out.push_str(if is_absolute_path(core) { PATH } else { core });
    out.push_str(tail);
}

fn is_absolute_path(core: &str) -> bool {
    if ["res://", "user://", "uid://"]
        .iter()
        .any(|scheme| core.starts_with(scheme))
    {
        return false;
    }
    let bytes = core.as_bytes();
    let drive_path = bytes.len() >= 3
        && bytes[0].is_ascii_alphabetic()
        && bytes[1] == b':'
        && (bytes[2] == b'\\' || (bytes[2] == b'/' && bytes.get(3) != Some(&b'/')));
    if drive_path || (core.starts_with("\\\\") && core.len() > 2) {
        return true;
    }
    if core.starts_with('/') {
        let segments: Vec<&str> = core.split('/').filter(|part| !part.is_empty()).collect();
        return segments.len() >= 2
            || segments
                .first()
                .is_some_and(|first| UNIX_ROOTS.contains(first));
    }
    false
}

/// Replaces credential-shaped text by `{redacted}`.
fn redact_credentials(text: &str) -> String {
    fn has_credential_prefix(word: &str) -> bool {
        CREDENTIAL_PREFIXES.iter().any(|prefix| {
            word.strip_prefix(prefix)
                .is_some_and(|rest| rest.chars().count() >= MIN_AFTER_PREFIX)
        })
    }
    fn is_long_mixed_run(run: &str) -> bool {
        run.len() >= LONG_RUN
            && run.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_alphabetic())
            && run.chars().any(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
    }
    let text = replace_runs(
        text,
        |c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_' || c == '-',
        has_credential_prefix,
    );
    let text = redact_bearer_values(&text);
    replace_runs(&text, |c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric(), is_long_mixed_run)
}

/// Rewrites every maximal run of characters accepted by `in_run` that `redact` selects.
fn replace_runs(text: &str, in_run: fn(char) -> bool, redact: fn(&str) -> bool) -> String {
    fn push_run(out: &mut String, run: &str, redact: fn(&str) -> bool) {
        out.push_str(if redact(run) { REDACTED } else { run });
    }
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    let mut run_start: Option<usize> = None;
    for (index, c) in text.char_indices() {
        if in_run(c) {
            run_start.get_or_insert(index);
        } else {
            if let Some(start) = run_start.take() {
                push_run(&mut out, &text[start..index], redact);
            }
            out.push(c);
        }
    }
    if let Some(start) = run_start {
        push_run(&mut out, &text[start..], redact);
    }
    out
}

/// Replaces the value that follows the word `Bearer`.
fn redact_bearer_values(text: &str) -> String {
    const WORD: &str = "bearer";
    let lower = text.to_ascii_lowercase();
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    let mut cursor = 0;
    while let Some(found) = lower[cursor..].find(WORD) {
        let word_end = cursor + found + WORD.len();
        let separated_before = text[..cursor + found]
            .chars()
            .next_back()
            .is_none_or(|c| !is_name_char(c));
        let rest = &text[word_end..];
        let spaces = rest.len() - rest.trim_start_matches([' ', '\t']).len();
        let value_len = rest[spaces..]
            .find(char::is_whitespace)
            .unwrap_or(rest.len() - spaces);
        let value = &rest[spaces..spaces + value_len];
        out.push_str(&text[cursor..word_end]);
        if separated_before
            && spaces > 0
            && value.chars().count() >= MIN_AFTER_PREFIX
            && value != REDACTED
        {
            out.push_str(&rest[..spaces]);
            out.push_str(REDACTED);
            cursor = word_end + spaces + value_len;
        } else {
            cursor = word_end;
        }
    }
    out.push_str(&text[cursor..]);
    out
}

/// Keeps text from notifying users (`@name`), cross-linking issues (`#123`), injecting markup
/// (`<`) or making the tracker fetch an image (`![`).
fn neutralize_markdown(text: &str) -> String {
    let chars: Vec<char> = text.chars().collect();
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    for (index, &c) in chars.iter().enumerate() {
        let next = chars.get(index + 1).copied();
        match c {
            '@' if next.is_some_and(|n| n.is_alphanumeric() || n == '_') => {
                out.push('@');
                out.push(ZERO_WIDTH_SPACE);
            }
            '#' if next.is_some_and(|n| n.is_ascii_digit()) => {
                out.push('#');
                out.push(ZERO_WIDTH_SPACE);
            }
            '<' => out.push_str("&lt;"),
            '!' if next == Some('[') => out.push_str("!\\"),
            other => out.push(other),
        }
    }
    out
}

/// What stays the same when the same failure is reported twice: lower case, digit runs as `#`,
/// anything path-like as `<path>`, single spaces, at most 120 characters.
pub fn shape(message: &str) -> String {
    let words: Vec<String> = message
        .split_whitespace()
        .map(|token| {
            if token.contains(['/', '\\']) {
                return "<path>".to_string();
            }
            let mut word = String::new();
            for c in token.to_lowercase().chars() {
                if c.is_ascii_digit() {
                    if !word.ends_with('#') {
                        word.push('#');
                    }
                } else {
                    word.push(c);
                }
            }
            word
        })
        .collect();
    words.join(" ").chars().take(120).collect()
}

/// 64-bit FNV-1a: small, stable across Rust versions and platforms, and good enough to tell
/// reports apart (it is not a security measure).
pub fn fnv1a64(bytes: &[u8]) -> u64 {
    bytes.iter().fold(0xcbf2_9ce4_8422_2325, |hash, &byte| {
        (hash ^ u64::from(byte)).wrapping_mul(0x0100_0000_01b3)
    })
}

/// One alphanumeric token, so the tracker's search treats it as a single term.
pub fn fingerprint(key: &str) -> String {
    let digits = format!("{:016x}", fnv1a64(key.as_bytes()));
    format!("gmfb{}", &digits[..12])
}

/// Percent-encodes everything except the unreserved characters `A-Z a-z 0-9 - . _ ~`.
pub fn percent_encode(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut out = String::with_capacity(text.len());
    for c in text.chars() {
        push_percent_encoded(&mut out, c);
    }
    out
}

fn push_percent_encoded(out: &mut String, c: char) {
    if c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '-' | '.' | '_' | '~') {
        out.push(c);
        return;
    }
    let mut buffer = [0u8; 4];
    for byte in c.encode_utf8(&mut buffer).bytes() {
        out.push_str(&format!("%{byte:02X}"));
    }
}

/// A new-issue link carrying only the title and the body (labels and the like need permissions
/// the reporter may lack). A body that does not fit is cut at a character boundary and ends with
/// `LINK_CUT_MARKER`; when even that does not fit, the link carries the title alone.
pub fn build_link(base: &str, title: &str, body: &str) -> Result<String, String> {
    let head = format!("{base}?title={}", percent_encode(title));
    if head.len() > LINK_BUDGET {
        return Err(format!(
            "the title is too long for a link of {LINK_BUDGET} bytes"
        ));
    }
    let full = format!("{head}&body={}", percent_encode(body));
    if full.len() <= LINK_BUDGET {
        return Ok(full);
    }
    let marker = percent_encode(LINK_CUT_MARKER);
    let fixed = head.len() + "&body=".len() + marker.len();
    if fixed > LINK_BUDGET {
        return Ok(head);
    }
    let mut kept = String::new();
    for c in body.chars() {
        let mut piece = String::new();
        push_percent_encoded(&mut piece, c);
        if fixed + kept.len() + piece.len() > LINK_BUDGET {
            break;
        }
        kept.push_str(&piece);
    }
    Ok(format!("{head}&body={kept}{marker}"))
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use std::path::PathBuf;

    fn context() -> ScrubContext {
        ScrubContext::default()
    }

    #[test]
    fn control_and_bidirectional_override_characters_are_removed() {
        let text = "a\u{0}b\u{7}c\r\nd\te\u{202E}f\u{2066}g\u{200B}h";
        assert_eq!(scrub(text, &context()), "abc\nd\tefg\u{200B}h");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_exact_secret_is_replaced_wherever_it_appears() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some("s3cr3t-value".into()),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("token s3cr3t-value and again s3cr3t-value.", &ctx),
            "token {redacted} and again {redacted}."
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_empty_secret_changes_nothing() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some(String::new()),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(scrub("plain text", &ctx), "plain text");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_project_directory_becomes_a_placeholder_in_both_separator_styles() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            project_dir: Some(PathBuf::from(r"C:\Users\Alice\game")),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            scrub(r"error in C:\Users\Alice\game\scripts\a.gd", &ctx),
            r"error in {project}\scripts\a.gd"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("error in C:/Users/Alice/game/scripts/a.gd", &ctx),
            "error in {project}/scripts/a.gd"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_unix_project_directory_with_a_trailing_slash_is_replaced() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            project_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice/game/")),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("/home/alice/game/scripts/a.gd", &ctx),
            "{project}/scripts/a.gd"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_directory_match_does_not_continue_into_a_longer_name() {
        assert_eq!(
            replace_directory(
                "/home/alice/game2/x",
                "/home/alice/game",
                "{project}",
                false
            ),
            "/home/alice/game2/x"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            replace_directory(
                "see /home/alice/game, ok",
                "/home/alice/game",
                "{project}",
                false
            ),
            "see {project}, ok"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn directory_matching_can_ignore_case() {
        assert_eq!(
            replace_directory(r"c:\users\alice\x", r"C:\Users\Alice", "~", true),
            r"~\x"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            replace_directory(r"c:\users\alice\x", r"C:\Users\Alice", "~", false),
            r"c:\users\alice\x"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_home_directory_becomes_a_tilde_after_the_project_directory() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            project_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice/game")),
            home_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice")),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("/home/alice/game/a.gd and /home/alice/.config/b", &ctx),
            "{project}/a.gd and ~/.config/b"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn without_a_known_project_a_path_under_home_is_hidden_completely() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            home_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice")),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("opened /home/alice/MyGame/scripts/a.gd", &ctx),
            "opened {path}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_root_directory_as_home_is_ignored_instead_of_eating_every_slash() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            home_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/")),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        assert_eq!(scrub("res://a/b and x/y", &ctx), "res://a/b and x/y");
    }

    #[test]
    fn absolute_paths_become_placeholders() {
        assert_eq!(
            redact_paths(r"failed to open D:\Games\Foo\bar.gd."),
            "failed to open {path}."
        );
        assert_eq!(
            redact_paths(r"read \\server\share\x.txt now"),
            "read {path} now"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            redact_paths("/usr/local/bin/godot not found"),
            "{path} not found"
        );
        assert_eq!(redact_paths("cwd is /tmp"), "cwd is {path}");
        assert_eq!(redact_paths("(see /home/bob/x.gd)"), "(see {path})");
        assert_eq!(redact_paths("--path=/srv/data/game"), "--path={path}");
        assert_eq!(
            redact_paths(r#"{"file":"/etc/hosts/x"}"#),
            r#"{"file":"{path}"}"#
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn project_relative_and_non_path_text_is_left_alone() {
        for text in [
            "res://scenes/Main.tscn",
            "user://save/slot1.dat",
            "uid://b3x4k2",
            "https://example.com/a/b/c",
            "Player/Sprite2D/Camera",
            "1/2 of /api",
            "{project}/scripts/a.gd",
            "~/.config/godot",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(redact_paths(text), text, "{text}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn credentials_are_replaced() {
        for secret in [
            "ghp_abcdefghijklmnop1234",
            "gho_abcdefghijklmnop1234",
            "ghs_abcdefghijklmnop1234",
            "github_pat_11ABCDEFG0abcdefghijkl_mnopqrstuv",
            "sk-abcdefgh12345678",
            "xoxb-1234567890-abcdef",
            "xoxp-1234567890-abcdef",
            "AKIAIOSFODNN7EXAMPLE",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(
                redact_credentials(&format!("key {secret} end")),
                "key {redacted} end",
                "{secret}"
            );
        }
        assert_eq!(
            redact_credentials("Authorization: Bearer abc.def-ghi_jkl and more"),
            "Authorization: Bearer {redacted} and more"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            redact_credentials("hash 0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef end"),
            "hash {redacted} end"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn ordinary_identifiers_and_ids_survive_the_credential_rule() {
        for text in [
            "abcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyzabcdef",
            "0123456789012345678901234567890",
            "123e4567-e89b-12d3-a456-426614174000",
            "res://levels/very_long_level_name_with_digits_1234567890_abcdefghijklmnop.tscn",
            "task-queue and mask-abcdefgh12345678",
            "sk-short",
            "godot_set_node_properties",
            "Bearer",
        ] {
            assert_eq!(redact_credentials(text), text, "{text}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn markdown_that_notifies_or_embeds_is_neutralized() {
        assert_eq!(
            neutralize_markdown("hi @alice and @everyone"),
            "hi @\u{200B}alice and @\u{200B}everyone"
        );
        assert_eq!(neutralize_markdown("see #123"), "see #\u{200B}123");
        assert_eq!(neutralize_markdown("# title and #tag"), "# title and #tag");
        assert_eq!(
            neutralize_markdown("<script>alert(1)</script>"),
            "&lt;script>alert(1)&lt;/script>"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            neutralize_markdown("![x](http://e/p.png)"),
            r"!\[x](http://e/p.png)"
        );
        assert_eq!(neutralize_markdown("a@b.com"), "a@\u{200B}b.com");
    }

    #[test]
    fn scrub_applies_every_step_to_one_text() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some("topsecretvalue".into()),
            project_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice/game")),
            home_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice")),
        };
        let text = "ping @bob see #12 at /home/alice/game/a.gd and /opt/x/y with topsecretvalue, \
                    ghp_abcdefghijklmnop1234, C:\\Tools\\godot.exe, \
                    0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef <b>";
        assert_eq!(
            scrub(text, &ctx),
            "ping @\u{200B}bob see #\u{200B}12 at {project}/a.gd and {path} with {redacted}, \
             {redacted}, {path}, {redacted} &lt;b>"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn scrubbing_twice_changes_nothing_more() {
        let ctx = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some("topsecretvalue".into()),
            project_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice/game")),
            home_dir: Some(PathBuf::from("/home/alice")),
        };
        let text = "@bob #12 <b> ![i](u) /home/alice/game/a.gd /opt/x/y topsecretvalue \
                    ghp_abcdefghijklmnop1234 C:\\Tools\\godot.exe 0123456789abcdef0123456789abcdef";
        let once = scrub(text, &ctx);
        assert_eq!(scrub(&once, &ctx), once);
        assert!(!once.contains("alice"));
        assert!(!once.contains("topsecretvalue"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn newlines_and_tabs_survive() {
        assert_eq!(
            scrub("line one\n\tline two", &context()),
            "line one\n\tline two"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn fnv1a_matches_the_published_vectors() {
        assert_eq!(fnv1a64(b""), 0xcbf2_9ce4_8422_2325);
        assert_eq!(fnv1a64(b"a"), 0xaf63_dc4c_8601_ec8c);
        assert_eq!(fnv1a64(b"foobar"), 0x8594_4171_f739_67e8);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_fingerprint_is_one_stable_alphanumeric_token() {
        let first = fingerprint("bug|godot_add_node");
        assert_eq!(first, fingerprint("bug|godot_add_node"));
        assert_ne!(first, fingerprint("bug|godot_remove_node"));
        assert!(first.starts_with("gmfb"));
        assert_eq!(first.len(), 16);
        assert!(
            first
                .chars()
                .all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_message_shape_ignores_numbers_paths_case_and_spacing() {
        assert_eq!(
            shape("Failed  at /home/u/a.gd line 12, col 7"),
            "failed at <path> line #, col #"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            shape("Failed at res://x/b.gd line 99, col 3"),
            "failed at <path> line #, col #"
        );
        assert_eq!(shape(&"x".repeat(500)).len(), 120);
    }

    #[test]
    fn percent_encoding_keeps_only_unreserved_characters() {
        assert_eq!(percent_encode("AZaz09-._~"), "AZaz09-._~");
        assert_eq!(percent_encode(" /?#&=+%\n"), "%20%2F%3F%23%26%3D%2B%25%0A");
        assert_eq!(percent_encode("é"), "%C3%A9");
    }

    const BASE: &str = "https://github.com/o/r/issues/new";

    #[test]
    fn a_short_report_becomes_a_complete_link() {
        assert_eq!(
            build_link(BASE, "A b", "Body").expect("link"),
            "https://github.com/o/r/issues/new?title=A%20b&body=Body"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_long_body_is_cut_to_the_budget_and_says_so() {
        let link = build_link(BASE, "T", &"a".repeat(20_000)).expect("link");
        assert!(link.len() <= LINK_BUDGET, "{}", link.len());
        assert!(link.ends_with(&percent_encode(LINK_CUT_MARKER)));
        assert!(link.contains("&body=aaaa"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_cut_never_splits_a_multibyte_character() {
        let link = build_link(BASE, "T", &"é".repeat(10_000)).expect("link");
        assert!(link.len() <= LINK_BUDGET);
        let body = link.split("&body=").nth(1).expect("body part");
        let decoded = decode_percent(body);
        assert!(String::from_utf8(decoded).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_title_that_leaves_no_room_for_the_marker_gives_a_title_only_link() {
        let title = "x".repeat(LINK_BUDGET - BASE.len() - "?title=".len() - 10);
        let link = build_link(BASE, &title, &"b".repeat(1_000)).expect("link");
        assert!(link.len() <= LINK_BUDGET);
        assert!(!link.contains("body="));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_title_that_cannot_fit_gives_no_link() {
        let error = build_link(BASE, &"x".repeat(7_000), "b").expect_err("no link");
        assert!(error.contains("too long"), "{error}");
    }

    fn decode_percent(text: &str) -> Vec<u8> {
        let bytes = text.as_bytes();
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        let mut i = 0;
        while i < bytes.len() {
            if bytes[i] == b'%' {
                let hex = std::str::from_utf8(&bytes[i + 1..i + 3]).expect("hex digits");
                out.push(u8::from_str_radix(hex, 16).expect("hex digits"));
                i += 3;
            } else {
                out.push(bytes[i]);
                i += 1;
            }
        }
        out
    }
}

```

`src/godot/debug_ctl.rs`:

```rs
//! Breakpoint control on top of Godot's remote debugger.
//!
//! Commands and payloads follow Godot 4.x `remote_debugger.cpp` (checked via the
//! shin2344234 server, which verified them on 4.7.2):
//! `core:breakpoint [source, line, enabled]`, `core:break`, and, only while stopped,
//! `continue` / `step` / `next` / `out` / `get_stack_dump` / `get_stack_frame_vars`.

use crate::error::{McpError, Result};
use crate::godot::debug_session::{DebugSession, GameMessage};
use crate::util::lock;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::collections::{BTreeMap, BTreeSet};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::sync::broadcast;

const QUERY_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct StopInfo {
    pub reason: String,
    pub error: Option<String>,
    pub can_step: bool,
    pub has_stack: bool,
    pub thread: i64,
    pub id: u64,
}

#[derive(Debug, Default)]
struct State {
    breakpoints: BTreeMap<String, BTreeSet<i64>>,
    stop: Option<StopInfo>,
    stepping: bool,
    stops: u64,
}

pub struct DebugCtl {
    session: Arc<DebugSession>,
    state: Mutex<State>,
}

impl DebugCtl {
    pub fn new(session: Arc<DebugSession>) -> Arc<Self> {
        let ctl = Arc::new(Self {
            session: Arc::clone(&session),
            state: Mutex::new(State::default()),
        });
        let watcher = Arc::clone(&ctl);
        let mut rx = session.subscribe();
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            loop {
                match rx.recv().await {
                    Ok(message) => watcher.on_message(&message),
                    Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                    Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => break,
                }
            }
        });
        ctl
    }

    fn on_message(&self, message: &GameMessage) {
        match message.message.as_str() {
            "debug_enter" => {
                let mut state = lock(&self.state);
                let can_continue = message
                    .data
                    .first()
                    .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                    .unwrap_or(true);
                let text = message.data.get(1).and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("");
                let has_stack = message
                    .data
                    .get(2)
                    .and_then(Value::as_bool)
                    .unwrap_or(false);
                let thread = message
                    .data
                    .get(3)
                    .and_then(Value::as_i64)
                    .filter(|thread| *thread > 0)
                    .unwrap_or(message.thread);
                let reason = if text == "Breakpoint" && state.stepping {
                    "step"
                } else if text == "Breakpoint" {
                    "breakpoint"
                } else if text == "Breakpoint Statement" {
                    "breakpoint_statement"
                } else if text.is_empty() {
                    "pause"
                } else {
                    "error"
                };
                state.stepping = false;
                state.stops += 1;
                state.stop = Some(StopInfo {
                    reason: reason.into(),
                    error: (reason == "error").then(|| text.to_string()),
                    can_step: can_continue && reason != "error",
                    has_stack,
                    thread,
                    id: state.stops,
                });
            }
            "debug_exit" => {
                lock(&self.state).stop = None;
            }
            _ => {}
        }
    }

    pub fn list_breakpoints(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        let state = lock(&self.state);
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        for (file, lines) in &state.breakpoints {
            for line in lines {
                out.push(format!("{file}:{line}"));
            }
        }
        out
    }

    pub fn remember(&self, file: &str, line: i64, enabled: bool) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        if enabled {
            state
                .breakpoints
                .entry(file.to_string())
                .or_default()
                .insert(line);
        } else if let Some(lines) = state.breakpoints.get_mut(file) {
            lines.remove(&line);
            if lines.is_empty() {
                state.breakpoints.remove(file);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn seed(&self, specs: &[String]) {
        for spec in specs {
            if let Some((file, line)) = split_breakpoint(spec) {
                self.remember(&file, line, true);
            }
        }
    }

    pub fn set_breakpoint(&self, file: &str, line: i64, enabled: bool) -> Result<()> {
        self.session.send(
            "core:breakpoint",
            vec![json!(file), json!(line), json!(enabled)],
            None,
        )?;
        self.remember(file, line, enabled);
        Ok(())
    }

    pub fn stop(&self) -> Option<StopInfo> {
        lock(&self.state).stop.clone()
    }

    pub async fn pause(&self, wait: Duration) -> Result<StopInfo> {
        if let Some(stop) = self.stop() {
            return Ok(stop);
        }
        self.session.send("core:break", Vec::new(), None)?;
        self.wait_stopped(wait).await
    }

    pub async fn resume(&self) -> Result<()> {
        let stop = self.require_stopped()?;
        self.session
            .send("continue", Vec::new(), Some(stop.thread))?;
        Ok(())
    }

    /// `step` is `into`, `over`, or `out` (Godot commands `step`, `next`, `out`).
    pub async fn step(&self, kind: &str, wait: Duration) -> Result<StopInfo> {
        let stop = self.require_stopped()?;
        if !stop.can_step {
            return Err(McpError::ToolExecution(
                "this stop cannot be stepped (Godot only allows continue after a script error)"
                    .into(),
            ));
        }
        let command = match kind {
            "into" => "step",
            "over" => "next",
            "out" => "out",
            other => {
                return Err(McpError::Validation(format!(
                    "unknown step kind '{other}' (use into, over, or out)"
                )));
            }
        };
        {
            let mut state = lock(&self.state);
            state.stepping = true;
            state.stop = None;
        }
        self.session.send(command, Vec::new(), Some(stop.thread))?;
        self.wait_stopped(wait).await
    }

    pub async fn stack(&self) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
        let stop = self.require_stopped()?;
        if !stop.has_stack {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let mut rx = self.session.subscribe();
        self.session
            .send("get_stack_dump", Vec::new(), Some(stop.thread))?;
        let message = recv_named(&mut rx, "stack_dump", QUERY_TIMEOUT).await?;
        Ok(parse_stack(&message.data))
    }

    pub async fn variables(&self, frame: i64) -> Result<Vec<Value>> {
        let stop = self.require_stopped()?;
        if !stop.has_stack {
            return Ok(Vec::new());
        }
        let mut rx = self.session.subscribe();
        self.session.send(
            "get_stack_frame_vars",
            vec![json!(frame)],
            Some(stop.thread),
        )?;
        let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + QUERY_TIMEOUT;
        let mut expected = -1_i64;
        let mut vars = Vec::new();
        loop {
            let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                    "timed out waiting for variables of frame {frame}"
                )));
            }
            let message = match tokio::time::timeout(left, rx.recv()).await {
                Ok(Ok(message)) => message,
                Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_))) => continue,
                Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed)) => {
                    return Err(McpError::Process("the game disconnected".into()));
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                        "timed out waiting for variables of frame {frame}"
                    )));
                }
            };
            if message.message == "stack_frame_vars" {
                expected = message.data.first().and_then(Value::as_i64).unwrap_or(0);
                if expected <= 0 {
                    return Ok(vars);
                }
            } else if message.message == "stack_frame_var" && expected >= 0 {
                vars.push(parse_variable(&message.data));
                if vars.len() as i64 >= expected {
                    return Ok(vars);
                }
            }
        }
    }

    fn require_stopped(&self) -> Result<StopInfo> {
        self.stop().ok_or_else(|| {
            McpError::ToolExecution(
                "the game is running, not stopped. Pause it or set a breakpoint first.".into(),
            )
        })
    }

    async fn wait_stopped(&self, wait: Duration) -> Result<StopInfo> {
        let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + wait;
        loop {
            if let Some(stop) = self.stop() {
                return Ok(stop);
            }
            if self.session.is_closed() {
                return Err(McpError::Process("the game disconnected".into()));
            }
            let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return Err(McpError::Process(
                    "timed out waiting for the game to stop".into(),
                ));
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20).min(left)).await;
        }
    }
}

pub fn split_breakpoint(spec: &str) -> Option<(String, i64)> {
    let (file, line) = spec.rsplit_once(':')?;
    let line = line.parse::<i64>().ok()?;
    if file.is_empty() || line < 1 {
        return None;
    }
    Some((file.to_string(), line))
}

fn parse_stack(data: &[Value]) -> Vec<Value> {
    let n = data.first().and_then(Value::as_i64).unwrap_or(0) / 3;
    let mut frames = Vec::new();
    for index in 0..n {
        let base = 1 + (index as usize) * 3;
        if base + 2 >= data.len() {
            break;
        }
        frames.push(json!({
            "file": data[base],
            "line": data[base + 1],
            "function": data[base + 2],
        }));
    }
    frames
}

fn parse_variable(data: &[Value]) -> Value {
    json!({
        "name": data.first().cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
        "scope": data.get(1).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
        "type": data.get(2).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
        "value": data.get(3).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null),
        "type_hint": data.get(4).cloned().unwrap_or(Value::String(String::new())),
    })
}

async fn recv_named(
    rx: &mut broadcast::Receiver<GameMessage>,
    name: &str,
    wait: Duration,
) -> Result<GameMessage> {
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + wait;
    loop {
        let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
        if left.is_zero() {
            return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                "timed out waiting for '{name}' from the game"
            )));
        }
        match tokio::time::timeout(left, rx.recv()).await {
            Ok(Ok(message)) if message.message == name => return Ok(message),
            Ok(Ok(_)) => {}
            Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_))) => {}
            Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed)) => {
                return Err(McpError::Process("the game disconnected".into()));
            }
            Err(_) => {
                return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                    "timed out waiting for '{name}' from the game"
                )));
            }
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn breakpoint_specs_split_on_the_last_colon() {
        assert_eq!(
            split_breakpoint("res://a.gd:12"),
            Some(("res://a.gd".into(), 12))
        );
        assert!(split_breakpoint("no-line").is_none());
        assert!(split_breakpoint("res://a.gd:0").is_none());
    }
}
```

`src/godot/debug_session.rs`:

```rs
//! TCP session for one game launched with `--remote-debug tcp://127.0.0.1:<port>`.
//!
//! Framing is a little-endian length plus one Variant (see `variant`). The listener
//! binds to loopback, accepts a single connection, then stops listening.

use crate::error::{McpError, Result};
use crate::godot::variant::{PacketReader, frame_packet};
use serde_json::Value;
use std::sync::Arc;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicI64, Ordering};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt};
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::sync::{broadcast, mpsc};
use tokio::time::timeout;

/// One debugger message from the game: `[message, thread_id, data]`.
#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct GameMessage {
    pub message: String,
    pub thread: i64,
    pub data: Vec<Value>,
}

pub struct DebugSession {
    port: u16,
    outbound: mpsc::UnboundedSender<Vec<u8>>,
    events: broadcast::Sender<GameMessage>,
    connected: AtomicBool,
    closed: AtomicBool,
    main_thread: AtomicI64,
    /// When true (the default), script errors are reported but do not freeze the game.
    ignore_error_breaks: AtomicBool,
}

impl DebugSession {
    /// Bind `127.0.0.1` on an ephemeral port and accept exactly one game.
    pub async fn listen() -> Result<Arc<Self>> {
        let listener = TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").await.map_err(|err| {
            McpError::Process(format!("failed to bind the debugger listener: {err}"))
        })?;
        let port = listener
            .local_addr()
            .map_err(|err| McpError::Process(format!("debugger listener has no address: {err}")))?
            .port();
        let (outbound, queue) = mpsc::unbounded_channel();
        let (events, _) = broadcast::channel(512);
        let session = Arc::new(Self {
            port,
            outbound,
            events,
            connected: AtomicBool::new(false),
            closed: AtomicBool::new(false),
            main_thread: AtomicI64::new(1),
            ignore_error_breaks: AtomicBool::new(true),
        });
        let reader_events = session.events.clone();
        let session_flag = Arc::clone(&session);
        tokio::spawn(async move {
            let Ok((socket, _)) = listener.accept().await else {
                session_flag.closed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
                return;
            };
            drop(listener);
            session_flag.connected.store(true, Ordering::Release);
            let _ = socket.set_nodelay(true);
            let (mut read, mut write) = socket.into_split();
            let writer = tokio::spawn(async move {
                let mut queue = queue;
                while let Some(bytes) = queue.recv().await {
                    if write.write_all(&bytes).await.is_err() {
                        break;
                    }
                }
            });
            // The game is already reading. Tell it not to freeze on script errors, and
            // clear SceneDebugger's default node-select mode, which drops injected input
            // after the first pause. Both are no-ops for a peer that is not a game.
            if session_flag.ignore_error_breaks.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                let _ = session_flag.send(
                    "core:set_ignore_error_breaks",
                    vec![serde_json::json!(true)],
                    None,
                );
            }
            let _ = session_flag.send(
                "scene:runtime_node_select_set_type",
                vec![serde_json::json!(0)],
                None,
            );
            let mut packets = PacketReader::default();
            let mut buf = vec![0_u8; 64 * 1024];
            loop {
                let n = match read.read(&mut buf).await {
                    Ok(0) | Err(_) => break,
                    Ok(n) => n,
                };
                match packets.push(&buf[..n]) {
                    Ok(values) => {
                        for value in values {
                            if let Some(message) = parse_message(&value) {
                                if message.message == "set_pid" && message.thread > 0 {
                                    session_flag
                                        .main_thread
                                        .store(message.thread, Ordering::Release);
                                }
                                let _ = reader_events.send(message);
                            }
                        }
                    }
                    Err(_) => break,
                }
            }
            writer.abort();
            session_flag.connected.store(false, Ordering::Release);
            session_flag.closed.store(true, Ordering::Release);
        });
        Ok(session)
    }

    pub fn port(&self) -> u16 {
        self.port
    }

    pub fn uri(&self) -> String {
        format!("tcp://127.0.0.1:{}", self.port)
    }

    pub fn is_connected(&self) -> bool {
        self.connected.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    pub fn is_closed(&self) -> bool {
        self.closed.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    /// `false` keeps Godot's default of stopping the game on a script error.
    pub fn set_ignore_error_breaks(&self, ignore: bool) {
        self.ignore_error_breaks.store(ignore, Ordering::Release);
    }

    pub fn subscribe(&self) -> broadcast::Receiver<GameMessage> {
        self.events.subscribe()
    }

    pub fn main_thread(&self) -> i64 {
        self.main_thread.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    /// Send a command to the game's main thread unless `thread` is set.
    pub fn send(&self, command: &str, data: Vec<Value>, thread: Option<i64>) -> Result<()> {
        if !self.is_connected() {
            return Err(McpError::Process(
                "the game's debugger is not connected".into(),
            ));
        }
        let thread = thread.unwrap_or_else(|| self.main_thread());
        let packet = frame_packet(&serde_json::json!([command, thread, data])).map_err(|err| {
            McpError::Protocol(format!(
                "failed to encode debugger command '{command}': {err}"
            ))
        })?;
        self.outbound
            .send(packet)
            .map_err(|_| McpError::Process("the debugger connection is closed".into()))
    }

    pub async fn wait_for(&self, name: &str, wait: Duration) -> Result<GameMessage> {
        let mut rx = self.subscribe();
        let result = timeout(wait, async {
            loop {
                match rx.recv().await {
                    Ok(message) if message.message == name => return Ok(message),
                    Ok(_) => {}
                    Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                    Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => {
                        return Err(McpError::Process("the game disconnected".into()));
                    }
                }
            }
        })
        .await;
        match result {
            Ok(outcome) => outcome,
            Err(_) => Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                "timed out after {}s waiting for '{name}' from the game",
                wait.as_secs()
            ))),
        }
    }
}

fn parse_message(value: &Value) -> Option<GameMessage> {
    let items = value.as_array()?;
    if items.len() < 3 {
        return None;
    }
    let message = items[0].as_str()?.to_string();
    let thread = items[1].as_i64().unwrap_or(1);
    let data = items[2].as_array()?.clone();
    Some(GameMessage {
        message,
        thread,
        data,
    })
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[tokio::test]
    async fn one_connection_roundtrips_a_command() {
        let session = DebugSession::listen().await.expect("listen");
        let mut client = tokio::net::TcpStream::connect(format!("127.0.0.1:{}", session.port()))
            .await
            .expect("connect");
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(20)).await;
        assert!(session.is_connected());

        let hello = frame_packet(&serde_json::json!(["set_pid", 1, [42]])).unwrap();
        client.write_all(&hello).await.unwrap();
        let incoming = session
            .wait_for("set_pid", Duration::from_secs(1))
            .await
            .unwrap();
        assert_eq!(incoming.data, vec![serde_json::json!(42)]);

        let mut rx = session.subscribe();
        session
            .send("mcp:request", vec![Value::String("{}".into())], None)
            .unwrap();
        let mut reader = PacketReader::default();
        let mut buf = vec![0_u8; 1024];
        let mut commands = Vec::new();
        let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(2);
        while commands.len() < 3 && tokio::time::Instant::now() < deadline {
            let n = tokio::time::timeout(Duration::from_secs(1), client.read(&mut buf))
                .await
                .expect("read timeout")
                .unwrap();
            commands.extend(reader.push(&buf[..n]).unwrap());
        }
        let names: Vec<_> = commands
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|packet| packet[0].as_str())
            .collect();
        assert!(names.contains(&"core:set_ignore_error_breaks"), "{names:?}");
        assert!(
            names.contains(&"scene:runtime_node_select_set_type"),
            "{names:?}"
        );
        assert!(names.contains(&"mcp:request"), "{names:?}");
        let _ = rx.try_recv();
    }
}
```

```rs
//! Godot remote-debugger client and the in-game runtime channel.

//! Process runs speak Godot's own debugger protocol (`--remote-debug`). The server

mod debug_ctl;
```

`src/godot/runs.rs`:

```rs
//! Managed Godot process runs.
//!
//! A run listens as the game's remote debugger, injects the runtime autoload through
//! `override.cfg` (never `project.godot`), and restores that file when the process exits.
//! The runtime script is copied into the project only when the addon does not already
//! contain it, and those copied files are removed when the run ends.

use crate::error::{McpError, Result};
use crate::godot::debug_ctl::DebugCtl;
use crate::godot::debug_session::{DebugSession, GameMessage};
use crate::util::lock;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::collections::VecDeque;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::sync::atomic::{AtomicBool, AtomicI64, AtomicU32, Ordering};
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncRead, BufReader};
use tokio::process::{Child, Command};
use tokio::sync::broadcast;
use uuid::Uuid;

const MARKER: &str = "; godot_mcp: temporary runtime autoload (removed automatically)";
const MAX_LOGS: usize = 2_000;
static NEXT_REQUEST: AtomicI64 = AtomicI64::new(1);

const RUNTIME_FILES: &[(&str, &str)] = &[
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/mcp_runtime.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/mcp_runtime.gd"),
    ),
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_result.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_result.gd"),
    ),
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_live.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_live.gd"),
    ),
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_input.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_input.gd"),
    ),
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_view.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_view.gd"),
    ),
    (
        "addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_output.gd",
        include_str!("../../addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_output.gd"),
    ),
];

#[derive(Clone, Debug)]
pub struct RunInfo {
    pub id: String,
    pub pid: u32,
    pub project: PathBuf,
}

struct LogLine {
    seq: u64,
    level: String,
    text: String,
    stack: Vec<String>,
}

struct Logs {
    seq: u64,
    lines: VecDeque<LogLine>,
    errors: VecDeque<Value>,
}

struct OverrideState {
    path: PathBuf,
    backup_path: PathBuf,
    previous: Option<Vec<u8>>,
    planted: Vec<PathBuf>,
}

pub struct Run {
    id: String,
    project: PathBuf,
    scene: String,
    headless: bool,
    pid: AtomicU32,
    session: Arc<DebugSession>,
    debug: Arc<DebugCtl>,
    logs: Mutex<Logs>,
    child: Mutex<Option<Child>>,
    hello: Mutex<Option<Value>>,
    running: AtomicBool,
    exit_code: Mutex<Option<i32>>,
    override_state: OverrideState,
    restored: AtomicBool,
}

pub struct GameHub {
    godot: PathBuf,
    runs: Mutex<Vec<Arc<Run>>>,
}

impl GameHub {
    pub fn new(godot: PathBuf) -> Self {
        Self {
            godot,
            runs: Mutex::new(Vec::new()),
        }
    }

    pub async fn start(
        &self,
        project: &Path,
        scene: Option<&str>,
        breakpoints: &[String],
        headless: bool,
        break_on_errors: bool,
    ) -> Result<RunInfo> {
        let planted = ensure_runtime(project)?;
        let override_state = inject_override(project, planted)?;
        let session = match DebugSession::listen().await {
            Ok(session) => session,
            Err(err) => {
                restore_override(&override_state);
                return Err(err);
            }
        };
        session.set_ignore_error_breaks(!break_on_errors);
        let debug = DebugCtl::new(Arc::clone(&session));
        debug.seed(breakpoints);
        let id = Uuid::new_v4().simple().to_string();
        let id = id[..8].to_string();
        let run = Arc::new(Run {
            id: id.clone(),
            project: project.to_path_buf(),
            scene: scene.unwrap_or("").to_string(),
            headless,
            pid: AtomicU32::new(0),
            session: Arc::clone(&session),
            debug,
            logs: Mutex::new(Logs {
                seq: 0,
                lines: VecDeque::new(),
                errors: VecDeque::new(),
            }),
            child: Mutex::new(None),
            hello: Mutex::new(None),
            running: AtomicBool::new(true),
            exit_code: Mutex::new(None),
            override_state,
            restored: AtomicBool::new(false),
        });
        spawn_log_task(Arc::clone(&run));

        let mut command = Command::new(&self.godot);
        if headless {
            command.arg("--headless");
        }
        command.arg("--path").arg(project);
        command.arg("--remote-debug").arg(session.uri());
        if !breakpoints.is_empty() {
            command.arg("--breakpoints").arg(breakpoints.join(","));
        }
        if let Some(scene) = scene {
            command.arg("--scene").arg(scene);
        }
        command
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped())
            .kill_on_drop(true);
        #[cfg(unix)]
        command.process_group(0);
        #[cfg(windows)]
        {
            const DETACHED_PROCESS: u32 = 0x0000_0008;
            const CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP: u32 = 0x0000_0200;
            command.creation_flags(DETACHED_PROCESS | CREATE_NEW_PROCESS_GROUP);
        }

        let mut child = match command.spawn() {
            Ok(child) => child,
            Err(err) => {
                run.restore();
                return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                    "failed to spawn Godot at '{}': {err}",
                    self.godot.display()
                )));
            }
        };
        let pid = child.id().unwrap_or(0);
        run.pid.store(pid, Ordering::Release);
        let stdout = child.stdout.take();
        let stderr = child.stderr.take();
        *lock(&run.child) = Some(child);
        spawn_pipe(Arc::clone(&run), stdout, "stdout");
        spawn_pipe(Arc::clone(&run), stderr, "stderr");
        spawn_wait(Arc::clone(&run));

        let mut runs = lock(&self.runs);
        runs.push(Arc::clone(&run));
        if runs.len() > 8 {
            if let Some(old) = runs.first().cloned() {
                if !old.running.load(Ordering::Acquire) {
                    runs.remove(0);
                }
            }
        }
        Ok(RunInfo {
            id,
            pid,
            project: project.to_path_buf(),
        })
    }

    pub fn status(&self) -> Vec<Value> {
        lock(&self.runs).iter().map(|run| run.summary()).collect()
    }

    pub async fn stop(&self, run_id: Option<&str>) -> Result<Value> {
        let run = self.select(run_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
            McpError::ToolExecution("No managed Godot process is running.".into())
        })?;
        run.kill().await;
        Ok(run.summary())
    }

    pub fn select(&self, run_id: Option<&str>) -> Result<Option<Arc<Run>>> {
        let runs = lock(&self.runs);
        if let Some(id) = run_id {
            if id == "editor" {
                return Ok(None);
            }
            return runs
                .iter()
                .rev()
                .find(|run| run.id == id)
                .cloned()
                .map(Some)
                .ok_or_else(|| McpError::ToolExecution(format!("No run with id '{id}'.")));
        }
        Ok(runs.iter().rev().find(|run| run.is_alive()).cloned())
    }

    pub async fn runtime(
        &self,
        run_id: Option<&str>,
        method: &str,
        params: Value,
        wait: Duration,
    ) -> Result<Value> {
        let run = self.select(run_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
            McpError::ToolExecution(
                "No managed game is running. Start one with godot_run_project (mode \"process\")."
                    .into(),
            )
        })?;
        run.runtime(method, params, wait).await
    }

    pub fn logs(&self, run_id: Option<&str>, since: u64, level: &str) -> Result<Value> {
        let run = self
            .select(run_id)?
            .ok_or_else(|| McpError::ToolExecution("No managed game to read logs from.".into()))?;
        Ok(run.page_logs(since, level))
    }

    pub fn debug(&self, run_id: Option<&str>) -> Result<Arc<Run>> {
        self.select(run_id)?.ok_or_else(|| {
            McpError::ToolExecution(
                "Breakpoint debugging needs a process run (godot_run_project mode \"process\")."
                    .into(),
            )
        })
    }
}

impl Run {
    pub fn id(&self) -> &str {
        &self.id
    }

    pub fn debug_ctl(&self) -> &DebugCtl {
        &self.debug
    }

    pub fn session(&self) -> &DebugSession {
        &self.session
    }

    fn is_alive(&self) -> bool {
        self.running.load(Ordering::Acquire)
    }

    fn summary(&self) -> Value {
        json!({
            "run_id": self.id,
            "pid": self.pid.load(Ordering::Acquire),
            "project": self.project.display().to_string(),
            "scene": self.scene,
            "headless": self.headless,
            "running": self.is_alive(),
            "debugger_connected": self.session.is_connected(),
            "exit_code": lock(&self.exit_code).clone(),
            "stopped": self.debug.stop().map(|stop| json!({
                "reason": stop.reason,
                "error": stop.error,
                "can_step": stop.can_step,
                "has_stack": stop.has_stack,
            })),
        })
    }

    async fn kill(&self) {
        let child = lock(&self.child).take();
        if let Some(mut child) = child {
            let _ = child.start_kill();
            let _ = child.wait().await;
        }
        self.running.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        self.restore();
    }

    fn restore(&self) {
        if self.restored.swap(true, Ordering::AcqRel) {
            return;
        }
        restore_override(&self.override_state);
    }

    pub fn page_logs(&self, since: u64, level: &str) -> Value {
        let logs = lock(&self.logs);
        let lines: Vec<Value> = logs
            .lines
            .iter()
            .filter(|line| line.seq >= since)
            .filter(|line| match level {
                "error" => line.level == "error",
                "warning" => line.level != "info",
                _ => true,
            })
            .take(200)
            .map(|line| {
                json!({
                    "seq": line.seq,
                    "level": line.level,
                    "text": line.text,
                    "stack": line.stack,
                })
            })
            .collect();
        let next = lines
            .last()
            .and_then(|line| line.get("seq"))
            .and_then(Value::as_u64)
            .map(|seq| seq + 1)
            .unwrap_or(since);
        json!({
            "run_id": self.id,
            "running": self.is_alive(),
            "lines": lines,
            "errors": logs.errors.iter().cloned().collect::<Vec<_>>(),
            "next": next,
        })
    }

    pub async fn runtime(&self, method: &str, params: Value, wait: Duration) -> Result<Value> {
        self.wait_hello(Duration::from_secs(8).min(wait)).await?;
        if !self.session.is_connected() {
            return Err(McpError::Process(
                "the game exited before the runtime answered".into(),
            ));
        }
        let id = NEXT_REQUEST.fetch_add(1, Ordering::Relaxed);
        let mut rx = self.session.subscribe();
        let payload = serde_json::to_string(&json!({
            "id": id,
            "method": method,
            "params": params,
        }))?;
        self.session
            .send("mcp:request", vec![Value::String(payload)], None)?;
        let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + wait;
        loop {
            let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                    "timed out waiting for the game runtime to answer '{method}'"
                )));
            }
            let message = match tokio::time::timeout(left, rx.recv()).await {
                Ok(Ok(message)) => message,
                Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_))) => continue,
                Ok(Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed)) => {
                    return Err(McpError::Process("the game disconnected".into()));
                }
                Err(_) => {
                    return Err(McpError::Process(format!(
                        "timed out waiting for the game runtime to answer '{method}'"
                    )));
                }
            };
            if message.message != "mcp:reply" {
                continue;
            }
            let Some(text) = message.data.first().and_then(Value::as_str) else {
                continue;
            };
            let parsed: Value = serde_json::from_str(text)?;
            if !json_id_eq(parsed.get("id"), id) {
                continue;
            }
            if let Some(err) = parsed.get("error") {
                return Err(McpError::ToolExecution(err.to_string()));
            }
            return Ok(parsed.get("result").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null));
        }
    }

    async fn wait_hello(&self, wait: Duration) -> Result<Value> {
        let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + wait;
        loop {
            if let Some(hello) = lock(&self.hello).clone() {
                return Ok(hello);
            }
            if !self.is_alive() && self.session.is_closed() {
                return Err(McpError::Process(
                    "the game exited before the runtime said hello. Read godot_run_output.".into(),
                ));
            }
            let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
            if left.is_zero() {
                return Err(McpError::Process(
                    "the game did not load the MCP runtime. Install addons/godot_mcp in the project and start it with godot_run_project."
                        .into(),
                ));
            }
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50).min(left)).await;
        }
    }

    fn push_line(&self, level: &str, text: &str, stack: Vec<String>) {
        if text.is_empty() {
            return;
        }
        let mut logs = lock(&self.logs);
        let seq = logs.seq;
        logs.seq += 1;
        if level != "info" {
            logs.errors.push_back(json!({
                "seq": seq,
                "level": level,
                "message": text,
                "stack": stack,
            }));
            while logs.errors.len() > 200 {
                logs.errors.pop_front();
            }
        }
        logs.lines.push_back(LogLine {
            seq,
            level: level.into(),
            text: text.chars().take(2_000).collect(),
            stack,
        });
        while logs.lines.len() > MAX_LOGS {
            logs.lines.pop_front();
        }
    }

    fn note_hello(&self, payload: Value) {
        *lock(&self.hello) = Some(payload);
    }

    fn note_exit(&self, code: Option<i32>) {
        self.running.store(false, Ordering::Release);
        *lock(&self.exit_code) = Some(code.unwrap_or(-1));
        self.restore();
    }
}

fn spawn_log_task(run: Arc<Run>) {
    let mut rx = run.session.subscribe();
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            match rx.recv().await {
                Ok(message) => ingest(&run, &message),
                Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Lagged(_)) => {}
                Err(broadcast::error::RecvError::Closed) => break,
            }
        }
    });
}

pub fn json_id_eq(value: Option<&Value>, id: i64) -> bool {
    let Some(value) = value else {
        return false;
    };
    if value.as_i64() == Some(id) {
        return true;
    }
    // Godot's JSON parser stores every number as a float, so a reply echoes 1.0.
    value
        .as_f64()
        .is_some_and(|number| number == id as f64 && number.fract() == 0.0)
}

fn ingest(run: &Run, message: &GameMessage) {
    match message.message.as_str() {
        "mcp:hello" => {
            if let Some(text) = message.data.first().and_then(Value::as_str) {
                if let Ok(payload) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text) {
                    run.note_hello(payload);
                }
            }
        }
        "output" => {
            let strings = message.data.first().and_then(Value::as_array);
            if let Some(strings) = strings {
                for item in strings {
                    if let Some(text) = item.as_str() {
                        let level = if text.contains("ERROR") {
                            "error"
                        } else if text.contains("WARNING") {
                            "warning"
                        } else {
                            "info"
                        };
                        run.push_line(level, text, Vec::new());
                    }
                }
            }
        }
        "error" => {
            if let Some((level, text, stack)) = parse_error(&message.data) {
                run.push_line(&level, &text, stack);
            }
        }
        _ => {}
    }
}

fn parse_error(data: &[Value]) -> Option<(String, String, Vec<String>)> {
    if data.len() < 11 {
        return None;
    }
    let file = data.get(4)?.as_str().unwrap_or("");
    let function = data.get(5)?.as_str().unwrap_or("");
    let line = data.get(6)?.as_i64().unwrap_or(0);
    let error = data.get(7).and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    let detail = data.get(8).and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("");
    let warning = data.get(9).and_then(Value::as_bool).unwrap_or(false);
    let stack_size = data.get(10).and_then(Value::as_i64).unwrap_or(0) / 3;
    let mut stack = Vec::new();
    for index in 0..stack_size {
        let base = 11 + (index as usize) * 3;
        if base + 2 >= data.len() {
            break;
        }
        stack.push(format!(
            "{}:{} in {}()",
            data[base].as_str().unwrap_or(""),
            data[base + 2].as_i64().unwrap_or(0),
            data[base + 1].as_str().unwrap_or("")
        ));
    }
    if stack.is_empty() && !file.is_empty() {
        stack.push(format!("{file}:{line} in {function}()"));
    }
    let text = if !error.is_empty() { error } else { detail };
    Some((
        if warning { "warning" } else { "error" }.into(),
        text.into(),
        stack,
    ))
}

fn spawn_pipe(run: Arc<Run>, pipe: Option<impl AsyncRead + Unpin + Send + 'static>, _name: &str) {
    let Some(pipe) = pipe else {
        return;
    };
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        let mut lines = BufReader::new(pipe).lines();
        while let Ok(Some(line)) = lines.next_line().await {
            let level = if line.contains("ERROR") {
                "error"
            } else if line.contains("WARNING") {
                "warning"
            } else {
                "info"
            };
            run.push_line(level, &line, Vec::new());
        }
    });
}

fn spawn_wait(run: Arc<Run>) {
    tokio::spawn(async move {
        loop {
            tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
            let status = {
                let mut child = lock(&run.child);
                match child.as_mut() {
                    Some(child) => child.try_wait(),
                    None => break,
                }
            };
            match status {
                Ok(Some(code)) => {
                    run.note_exit(code.code());
                    break;
                }
                Ok(None) => {}
                Err(_) => {
                    run.note_exit(None);
                    break;
                }
            }
        }
    });
}

fn ensure_runtime(project: &Path) -> Result<Vec<PathBuf>> {
    let mut planted = Vec::new();
    for (relative, contents) in RUNTIME_FILES {
        let path = project.join(relative);
        if path.is_file() {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(parent) = path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        std::fs::write(&path, contents)?;
        planted.push(path);
    }
    Ok(planted)
}

fn remove_planted(files: &[PathBuf]) {
    for path in files {
        let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
        let mut current = path.parent().map(Path::to_path_buf);
        while let Some(dir) = current {
            if dir.join("project.godot").is_file() || std::fs::remove_dir(&dir).is_err() {
                break;
            }
            current = dir.parent().map(Path::to_path_buf);
        }
    }
}

struct PlantedGuard(Vec<PathBuf>);

impl Drop for PlantedGuard {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        remove_planted(&self.0);
    }
}

fn inject_override(project: &Path, planted: Vec<PathBuf>) -> Result<OverrideState> {
    let mut planted = PlantedGuard(planted);
    let state = write_override(project, &planted.0)?;
    planted.0.clear();
    Ok(state)
}

fn write_override(project: &Path, planted: &[PathBuf]) -> Result<OverrideState> {
    let path = project.join("override.cfg");
    let backup_path = project.join(".godot/godot_mcp/override.cfg.user-backup");
    if path.is_file() {
        let current = std::fs::read(&path)?;
        if String::from_utf8_lossy(&current).contains(MARKER) {
            if backup_path.is_file() {
                std::fs::rename(&backup_path, &path)?;
            } else {
                std::fs::remove_file(&path)?;
            }
        }
    }
    let previous = if path.is_file() {
        let bytes = std::fs::read(&path)?;
        if let Some(parent) = backup_path.parent() {
            std::fs::create_dir_all(parent)?;
        }
        std::fs::write(&backup_path, &bytes)?;
        Some(bytes)
    } else {
        None
    };
    let autoload = format!(
        "{MARKER}\n[autoload]\n\nGodotMcpRuntime=\"res://addons/godot_mcp/runtime/mcp_runtime.gd\"\n"
    );
    let body = match &previous {
        Some(bytes) => format!(
            "{}\n\n{autoload}",
            String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes).trim_end()
        ),
        None => autoload,
    };
    std::fs::write(&path, body)?;
    Ok(OverrideState {
        path,
        backup_path,
        previous,
        planted: planted.to_vec(),
    })
}

fn restore_override(state: &OverrideState) {
    match &state.previous {
        Some(bytes) => {
            let _ = std::fs::write(&state.path, bytes);
        }
        None => {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&state.path);
        }
    }
    let _ = std::fs::remove_file(&state.backup_path);
    remove_planted(&state.planted);
}

impl Drop for GameHub {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        for run in lock(&self.runs).iter() {
            if let Some(child) = lock(&run.child).as_mut() {
                let _ = child.start_kill();
            }
            run.restore();
        }
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::{ensure_runtime, inject_override, restore_override};

    #[test]
    fn a_run_removes_only_the_runtime_files_it_created() {
        let project = tempfile::tempdir().expect("project");
        std::fs::write(project.path().join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").unwrap();
        let kept = project
            .path()
            .join("addons/godot_mcp/runtime/mcp_runtime.gd");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(kept.parent().unwrap()).unwrap();
        std::fs::write(&kept, "extends Node\n").unwrap();

        let planted = ensure_runtime(project.path()).unwrap();
        assert!(planted.iter().all(|path| path != &kept));
        assert!(
            project
                .path()
                .join("addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops/rt_input.gd")
                .is_file()
        );

        let state = inject_override(project.path(), planted).unwrap();
        assert!(
            std::fs::read_to_string(project.path().join("override.cfg"))
                .unwrap()
                .contains("temporary runtime autoload")
        );
        restore_override(&state);

        assert!(!project.path().join("override.cfg").exists());
        assert!(kept.is_file(), "a file the project already had was removed");
        assert!(!project.path().join("addons/godot_mcp/runtime/ops").exists());

        let fresh = tempfile::tempdir().expect("fresh project");
        std::fs::write(fresh.path().join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").unwrap();
        let planted = ensure_runtime(fresh.path()).unwrap();
        let state = inject_override(fresh.path(), planted).unwrap();
        restore_override(&state);
        assert!(
            !fresh.path().join("addons").exists(),
            "a project without the addon must not keep the copied runtime"
        );
    }
}

```

`src/godot/variant.rs`:

```rs
//! Godot's binary `encode_variant` / `decode_variant`, as used by the remote debugger.
//!
//! Layout matches Godot 4.x `core/io/marshalls.cpp` and was checked against the
//! shin2344234/godot-mcp encoder (`server/src/game/variant.ts`), which was verified on
//! Godot 4.7.2. Numbers are little-endian. Every value starts with a 32-bit header:
//! the low 8 bits are `Variant::Type`, bit 16 selects 64-bit numbers (and "object as id").
//! Payloads are padded to 4 bytes.
//!
//! Encoding covers what this server sends: null, bool, integers, floats, strings, arrays
//! and string-keyed objects. Decoding also accepts the math and packed types a game may
//! send back (stack variables, error payloads).

// The decoder reads bytes from another process, so a panic here would take the server down:
// no unwrap or expect outside the tests.
#![deny(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]

use serde_json::{Map, Number, Value};

const NIL: u32 = 0;
const BOOL: u32 = 1;
const INT: u32 = 2;
const FLOAT: u32 = 3;
const STRING: u32 = 4;
const VECTOR2: u32 = 5;
const VECTOR2I: u32 = 6;
const RECT2: u32 = 7;
const RECT2I: u32 = 8;
const VECTOR3: u32 = 9;
const VECTOR3I: u32 = 10;
const TRANSFORM2D: u32 = 11;
const VECTOR4: u32 = 12;
const VECTOR4I: u32 = 13;
const PLANE: u32 = 14;
const QUATERNION: u32 = 15;
const AABB: u32 = 16;
const BASIS: u32 = 17;
const TRANSFORM3D: u32 = 18;
const PROJECTION: u32 = 19;
const COLOR: u32 = 20;
const STRING_NAME: u32 = 21;
const NODE_PATH: u32 = 22;
const RID: u32 = 23;
const OBJECT: u32 = 24;
const CALLABLE: u32 = 25;
const SIGNAL: u32 = 26;
const DICTIONARY: u32 = 27;
const ARRAY: u32 = 28;
const PACKED_BYTE_ARRAY: u32 = 29;
const PACKED_INT32_ARRAY: u32 = 30;
const PACKED_INT64_ARRAY: u32 = 31;
const PACKED_FLOAT32_ARRAY: u32 = 32;
const PACKED_FLOAT64_ARRAY: u32 = 33;
const PACKED_STRING_ARRAY: u32 = 34;
const PACKED_VECTOR2_ARRAY: u32 = 35;
const PACKED_VECTOR3_ARRAY: u32 = 36;
const PACKED_COLOR_ARRAY: u32 = 37;
const PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY: u32 = 38;

const FLAG_64: u32 = 1 << 16;
const TYPE_MASK: u32 = 0xff;
const MAX_DEPTH: usize = 64;
const MAX_PACKET: usize = 8 << 20;

const KIND_NONE: u32 = 0;
const KIND_BUILTIN: u32 = 1;
const KIND_CLASS: u32 = 2;
const KIND_SCRIPT: u32 = 3;

#[derive(Debug)]
pub struct VariantError(pub String);

impl std::fmt::Display for VariantError {
    fn fmt(&self, f: &mut std::fmt::Formatter<'_>) -> std::fmt::Result {
        f.write_str(&self.0)
    }
}

struct Reader<'a> {
    buf: &'a [u8],
    pos: usize,
}

impl<'a> Reader<'a> {
    fn need(&self, n: usize) -> Result<(), VariantError> {
        if self.pos + n > self.buf.len() {
            Err(VariantError(format!(
                "variant data ends early (need {n} bytes at {} of {})",
                self.pos,
                self.buf.len()
            )))
        } else {
            Ok(())
        }
    }

    /// Bytes left after the current position.
    fn remaining(&self) -> usize {
        self.buf.len().saturating_sub(self.pos)
    }

    /// How many elements to reserve room for. The count comes from the peer, so it is capped by
    /// what the data can hold (every element takes at least four bytes); a count that promises
    /// more fails later on the missing data instead of reserving memory for it.
    fn capacity_for(&self, count: usize) -> usize {
        count.min(self.remaining() / 4)
    }

    fn take<const N: usize>(&mut self) -> Result<[u8; N], VariantError> {
        self.need(N)?;
        let mut out = [0_u8; N];
        out.copy_from_slice(&self.buf[self.pos..self.pos + N]);
        self.pos += N;
        Ok(out)
    }

    fn u32(&mut self) -> Result<u32, VariantError> {
        Ok(u32::from_le_bytes(self.take()?))
    }

    fn i32(&mut self) -> Result<i32, VariantError> {
        Ok(self.u32()? as i32)
    }

    fn i64(&mut self) -> Result<i64, VariantError> {
        Ok(i64::from_le_bytes(self.take()?))
    }

    fn u64(&mut self) -> Result<u64, VariantError> {
        Ok(u64::from_le_bytes(self.take()?))
    }

    fn f32(&mut self) -> Result<f32, VariantError> {
        Ok(f32::from_le_bytes(self.u32()?.to_le_bytes()))
    }

    fn f64(&mut self) -> Result<f64, VariantError> {
        Ok(f64::from_le_bytes(self.u64()?.to_le_bytes()))
    }

    fn bytes(&mut self, n: usize) -> Result<&'a [u8], VariantError> {
        self.need(n)?;
        let slice = &self.buf[self.pos..self.pos + n];
        self.pos += n;
        Ok(slice)
    }

    fn str(&mut self) -> Result<String, VariantError> {
        let len = self.u32()? as usize;
        if len > self.buf.len() {
            return Err(VariantError(format!(
                "string length {len} exceeds the packet"
            )));
        }
        let bytes = self.bytes(len)?;
        let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(bytes)
            .trim_end_matches('\0')
            .to_string();
        self.pos += (4 - (len % 4)) % 4;
        Ok(text)
    }
}

/// Decode one Variant. Returns the JSON value and the number of bytes consumed.
pub fn decode_variant(buf: &[u8]) -> Result<(Value, usize), VariantError> {
```
