    let mut reader = Reader { buf, pos: 0 };
    let value = read_value(&mut reader, 0)?;
    Ok((value, reader.pos))
}

fn read_container_type(reader: &mut Reader<'_>, kind: u32) -> Result<(), VariantError> {
    match kind {
        KIND_NONE => Ok(()),
        KIND_BUILTIN => {
            reader.u32()?;
            Ok(())
        }
        KIND_CLASS | KIND_SCRIPT => {
            reader.str()?;
            Ok(())
        }
        other => Err(VariantError(format!("invalid container type kind {other}"))),
    }
}

fn num(n: f64) -> String {
    if n.is_finite() {
        let rounded = (n * 1_000_000.0).round() / 1_000_000.0;
        if rounded.fract() == 0.0 {
            format!("{}", rounded as i64)
        } else {
            format!("{rounded}")
        }
    } else if n.is_nan() {
        "nan".into()
    } else if n.is_sign_positive() {
        "inf".into()
    } else {
        "-inf".into()
    }
}

fn math_spec(type_id: u32) -> Option<(&'static str, usize, bool)> {
    Some(match type_id {
        VECTOR2 => ("Vector2", 2, false),
        VECTOR2I => ("Vector2i", 2, true),
        RECT2 => ("Rect2", 4, false),
        RECT2I => ("Rect2i", 4, true),
        VECTOR3 => ("Vector3", 3, false),
        VECTOR3I => ("Vector3i", 3, true),
        TRANSFORM2D => ("Transform2D", 6, false),
        VECTOR4 => ("Vector4", 4, false),
        VECTOR4I => ("Vector4i", 4, true),
        PLANE => ("Plane", 4, false),
        QUATERNION => ("Quaternion", 4, false),
        AABB => ("AABB", 6, false),
        BASIS => ("Basis", 9, false),
        TRANSFORM3D => ("Transform3D", 12, false),
        PROJECTION => ("Projection", 16, false),
        _ => return None,
    })
}

fn read_value(reader: &mut Reader<'_>, depth: usize) -> Result<Value, VariantError> {
    if depth > MAX_DEPTH {
        return Err(VariantError("variant is nested too deeply".into()));
    }
    let header = reader.u32()?;
    let type_id = header & TYPE_MASK;
    let wide = header & FLAG_64 != 0;
    let real = |reader: &mut Reader<'_>, wide: bool| -> Result<f64, VariantError> {
        if wide {
            reader.f64()
        } else {
            Ok(f64::from(reader.f32()?))
        }
    };

    if let Some((name, count, ints)) = math_spec(type_id) {
        let mut parts = Vec::with_capacity(count);
        for _ in 0..count {
            if ints {
                parts.push(num(f64::from(reader.i32()?)));
            } else {
                parts.push(num(real(reader, wide)?));
            }
        }
        return Ok(Value::String(format!("{name}({})", parts.join(", "))));
    }

    match type_id {
        NIL => Ok(Value::Null),
        BOOL => Ok(Value::Bool(reader.u32()? != 0)),
        INT => {
            if wide {
                Ok(Value::Number(reader.i64()?.into()))
            } else {
                Ok(Value::Number(reader.i32()?.into()))
            }
        }
        FLOAT => {
            let n = if wide {
                reader.f64()?
            } else {
                f64::from(reader.f32()?)
            };
            Ok(Number::from_f64(n)
                .map(Value::Number)
                .unwrap_or(Value::Null))
        }
        STRING | STRING_NAME => Ok(Value::String(reader.str()?)),
        COLOR => {
            let parts = [
                num(f64::from(reader.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(reader.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(reader.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(reader.f32()?)),
            ];
            Ok(Value::String(format!("Color({})", parts.join(", "))))
        }
        NODE_PATH => {
            let first = reader.u32()?;
            if first & 0x8000_0000 == 0 {
                reader.pos -= 4;
                return Ok(Value::String(reader.str()?));
            }
            let names = (first & 0x7fff_ffff) as usize;
            let mut subnames = reader.u32()? as usize;
            let flags = reader.u32()?;
            if flags & 2 != 0 {
                subnames += 1;
            }
            let mut parts = Vec::with_capacity(reader.capacity_for(names + subnames));
            for _ in 0..names + subnames {
                parts.push(reader.str()?);
            }
            let path = parts[..names].join("/");
            let sub = if subnames == 0 {
                String::new()
            } else {
                format!(":{}", parts[names..].join(":"))
            };
            let absolute = if flags & 1 != 0 { "/" } else { "" };
            Ok(Value::String(format!("{absolute}{path}{sub}")))
        }
        RID => Ok(Value::String(format!("RID({})", reader.u64()?))),
        OBJECT => {
            if wide {
                let id = reader.u64()?;
                if id == 0 {
                    Ok(Value::Null)
                } else {
                    Ok(serde_json::json!({ "object_id": id.to_string() }))
                }
            } else {
                let class = reader.str()?;
                if class.is_empty() {
                    return Ok(Value::Null);
                }
                let count = reader.u32()? as usize;
                let mut properties = Map::new();
                for _ in 0..count {
                    let name = reader.str()?;
                    properties.insert(name, read_value(reader, depth + 1)?);
                }
                Ok(serde_json::json!({ "object": class, "properties": properties }))
            }
        }
        CALLABLE => Ok(Value::String("Callable()".into())),
        SIGNAL => {
            let name = reader.str()?;
            let id = reader.u64()?;
            Ok(serde_json::json!({ "signal": name, "object_id": id.to_string() }))
        }
        DICTIONARY => {
            read_container_type(reader, (header >> 16) & 3)?;
            read_container_type(reader, (header >> 18) & 3)?;
            let count = (reader.u32()? & 0x7fff_ffff) as usize;
            let mut map = Map::new();
            for _ in 0..count {
                let key = read_value(reader, depth + 1)?;
                let value = read_value(reader, depth + 1)?;
                let key = match key {
                    Value::String(text) => text,
                    other => other.to_string(),
                };
                map.insert(key, value);
            }
            Ok(Value::Object(map))
        }
        ARRAY => {
            read_container_type(reader, (header >> 16) & 3)?;
            let count = (reader.u32()? & 0x7fff_ffff) as usize;
            let mut items = Vec::with_capacity(reader.capacity_for(count));
            for _ in 0..count {
                items.push(read_value(reader, depth + 1)?);
            }
            Ok(Value::Array(items))
        }
        PACKED_BYTE_ARRAY => {
            let n = reader.u32()? as usize;
            let bytes = reader.bytes(n)?;
            let values: Vec<Value> = bytes
                .iter()
                .map(|byte| Value::Number((*byte).into()))
                .collect();
            reader.pos += (4 - (n % 4)) % 4;
            Ok(Value::Array(values))
        }
        PACKED_INT32_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| Ok(Value::Number(r.i32()?.into()))),
        PACKED_INT64_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| Ok(Value::Number(r.i64()?.into()))),
        PACKED_FLOAT32_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Number::from_f64(f64::from(r.f32()?))
                .map(Value::Number)
                .unwrap_or(Value::Null))
        }),
        PACKED_FLOAT64_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Number::from_f64(r.f64()?)
                .map(Value::Number)
                .unwrap_or(Value::Null))
        }),
        PACKED_STRING_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| Ok(Value::String(r.str()?))),
        PACKED_VECTOR2_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Value::String(format!(
                "Vector2({}, {})",
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?))
            )))
        }),
        PACKED_VECTOR3_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Value::String(format!(
                "Vector3({}, {}, {})",
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?))
            )))
        }),
        PACKED_VECTOR4_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Value::String(format!(
                "Vector4({}, {}, {}, {})",
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?))
            )))
        }),
        PACKED_COLOR_ARRAY => read_packed(reader, |r| {
            Ok(Value::String(format!(
                "Color({}, {}, {}, {})",
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?)),
                num(f64::from(r.f32()?))
            )))
        }),
        other => Err(VariantError(format!("unknown Variant type {other}"))),
    }
}

fn read_packed(
    reader: &mut Reader<'_>,
    mut item: impl FnMut(&mut Reader<'_>) -> Result<Value, VariantError>,
) -> Result<Value, VariantError> {
    let count = reader.u32()? as usize;
    let mut items = Vec::with_capacity(reader.capacity_for(count));
    for _ in 0..count {
        items.push(item(reader)?);
    }
    Ok(Value::Array(items))
}

/// Encode a JSON value as a Godot Variant.
pub fn encode_variant(value: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>, VariantError> {
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    write_value(value, &mut out, 0)?;
    Ok(out)
}

fn write_u32(out: &mut Vec<u8>, n: u32) {
    out.extend_from_slice(&n.to_le_bytes());
}

fn write_string(out: &mut Vec<u8>, text: &str) {
    let bytes = text.as_bytes();
    write_u32(out, bytes.len() as u32);
    out.extend_from_slice(bytes);
    out.resize(out.len() + (4 - (bytes.len() % 4)) % 4, 0);
}

fn write_value(value: &Value, out: &mut Vec<u8>, depth: usize) -> Result<(), VariantError> {
    if depth > MAX_DEPTH {
        return Err(VariantError("value is nested too deeply to encode".into()));
    }
    match value {
        Value::Null => write_u32(out, NIL),
        Value::Bool(flag) => {
            write_u32(out, BOOL);
            write_u32(out, u32::from(*flag));
        }
        Value::Number(number) => write_number(number, out),
        Value::String(text) => {
            write_u32(out, STRING);
            write_string(out, text);
        }
        Value::Array(items) => {
            write_u32(out, ARRAY);
            write_u32(out, items.len() as u32);
            for item in items {
                write_value(item, out, depth + 1)?;
            }
        }
        Value::Object(map) => {
            write_u32(out, DICTIONARY);
            write_u32(out, map.len() as u32);
            for (key, item) in map {
                write_value(&Value::String(key.clone()), out, depth + 1)?;
                write_value(item, out, depth + 1)?;
            }
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn write_number(number: &Number, out: &mut Vec<u8>) {
    if let Some(n) = number.as_i64() {
        if i64::from(i32::MIN) <= n && n <= i64::from(i32::MAX) {
            write_u32(out, INT);
            write_u32(out, n as u32);
            return;
        }
        write_u32(out, INT | FLAG_64);
        out.extend_from_slice(&n.to_le_bytes());
        return;
    }
    if let Some(n) = number.as_u64() {
        if i32::try_from(n).is_ok() {
            write_u32(out, INT);
            write_u32(out, n as u32);
            return;
        }
        write_u32(out, INT | FLAG_64);
        out.extend_from_slice(&(n as i64).to_le_bytes());
        return;
    }
    let n = number.as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0);
    write_u32(out, FLOAT | FLAG_64);
    out.extend_from_slice(&n.to_le_bytes());
}

/// Debugger packet: little-endian byte length, then one Variant.
pub fn frame_packet(value: &Value) -> Result<Vec<u8>, VariantError> {
    let body = encode_variant(value)?;
    if body.len() > MAX_PACKET {
        return Err(VariantError(format!(
            "debugger packet is too large ({} bytes)",
            body.len()
        )));
    }
    let mut framed = Vec::with_capacity(4 + body.len());
    write_u32(&mut framed, body.len() as u32);
    framed.extend_from_slice(&body);
    Ok(framed)
}

/// Splits a TCP byte stream into decoded debugger packets.
#[derive(Debug, Default)]
pub struct PacketReader {
    pending: Vec<u8>,
}

impl PacketReader {
    pub fn push(&mut self, chunk: &[u8]) -> Result<Vec<Value>, VariantError> {
        self.pending.extend_from_slice(chunk);
        let mut out = Vec::new();
        while let Some(prefix) = self.pending.first_chunk::<4>() {
            let size = u32::from_le_bytes(*prefix) as usize;
            if size > MAX_PACKET {
                return Err(VariantError(format!(
                    "debugger packet too large ({size} bytes)"
                )));
            }
            if self.pending.len() < 4 + size {
                break;
            }
            let body = self.pending[4..4 + size].to_vec();
            self.pending.drain(..4 + size);
            let (value, used) = decode_variant(&body)?;
            if used != body.len() {
                return Err(VariantError(format!(
                    "variant used {used} bytes of a {size}-byte packet"
                )));
            }
            out.push(value);
        }
        Ok(out)
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
#[allow(clippy::unwrap_used, clippy::expect_used)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn roundtrip(value: Value) {
        let encoded = encode_variant(&value).expect("encode");
        let (decoded, used) = decode_variant(&encoded).expect("decode");
        assert_eq!(used, encoded.len());
        assert_eq!(decoded, value);
    }

    #[test]
    fn scalars_and_containers_roundtrip() {
        roundtrip(Value::Null);
        roundtrip(json!(true));
        roundtrip(json!(false));
        roundtrip(json!(0));
        roundtrip(json!(-1));
        roundtrip(json!(42));
        roundtrip(json!(i64::from(i32::MAX)));
        roundtrip(json!(i64::from(i32::MIN)));
        roundtrip(json!(""));
        roundtrip(json!("hi"));
        roundtrip(json!("héllo"));
        roundtrip(json!(["a", 1, true, null]));
        roundtrip(json!({"cmd": "mcp:request", "n": 7}));
    }

    #[test]
    fn wide_integers_roundtrip() {
        roundtrip(json!(i64::from(i32::MAX) + 1));
        roundtrip(json!(i64::from(i32::MIN) - 1));
    }

    #[test]
    fn framed_packets_split_across_reads() {
        let first = frame_packet(&json!(["set_pid", 1, [7]])).unwrap();
        let second = frame_packet(&json!("ok")).unwrap();
        let mut reader = PacketReader::default();
        assert!(reader.push(&first[..3]).unwrap().is_empty());
        let mut rest = first[3..].to_vec();
        rest.extend_from_slice(&second[..1]);
        let one = reader.push(&rest).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(one, vec![json!(["set_pid", 1, [7]])]);
        let two = reader.push(&second[1..]).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(two, vec![json!("ok")]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn oversized_packet_is_rejected() {
        let mut reader = PacketReader::default();
        let huge = ((MAX_PACKET as u32) + 1).to_le_bytes();
        let err = reader.push(&huge).expect_err("too large");
        assert!(err.0.contains("too large"), "{err}");
    }

    /// A peer chooses the element counts. A twelve-byte packet must not make the decoder
    /// reserve memory for billions of elements; it has to fail on the missing data instead.
    #[test]
    fn counts_larger_than_the_data_fail_without_reserving_memory() {
        for (type_id, count) in [
            (ARRAY, 0x7fff_ffff_u32),
            (PACKED_INT64_ARRAY, 0xffff_ffff),
            (PACKED_STRING_ARRAY, 0xffff_ffff),
            (PACKED_COLOR_ARRAY, 0xffff_ffff),
        ] {
            let mut buf = Vec::new();
            write_u32(&mut buf, type_id);
            write_u32(&mut buf, count);
            let err = decode_variant(&buf).expect_err("the data ends right after the count");
            assert!(err.0.contains("ends early"), "type {type_id}: {err}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn node_path_name_counts_larger_than_the_data_fail_without_reserving_memory() {
        let mut buf = Vec::new();
        write_u32(&mut buf, NODE_PATH);
        write_u32(&mut buf, 0x8000_0000 | 0x7fff_ffff);
        write_u32(&mut buf, 0xffff_ffff);
        write_u32(&mut buf, 0);
        let err = decode_variant(&buf).expect_err("no names follow");
        assert!(err.0.contains("ends early"), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn color_decodes_as_text() {
        let mut buf = Vec::new();
        write_u32(&mut buf, COLOR);
        for component in [1.0f32, 0.0, 0.0, 1.0] {
            buf.extend_from_slice(&component.to_le_bytes());
        }
        let (value, _) = decode_variant(&buf).unwrap();
        assert_eq!(value, json!("Color(1, 0, 0, 1)"));
    }
}

```

`src/issues.rs`:

```rs
//! Submission of a feedback draft to the project's issue tracker through the human's own
//! signed-in GitHub command-line client. The server stores no credentials and calls no web API
//! itself; whatever `gh` is signed in as is what publishes the issue.

use crate::feedback::{ScrubContext, scrub};
use crate::journal::Outcome;
use std::ffi::OsStr;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;
use tokio::process::Command;
use tokio::time::timeout;

const LOOKUP_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(20);
const CREATE_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(45);
const QUOTED_LINE_LIMIT: usize = 200;
const BY_HAND: &str =
    "Or file it by hand: give the human the report text, or the prefilled link from the draft";

/// The repository the product is published in, as named by the package manifest.
#[derive(Debug, Clone, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub struct RepoAddress {
    owner: String,
    name: String,
}

impl RepoAddress {
    /// Reads `https://github.com/{owner}/{name}` (also with a trailing `/` or `.git`).
    pub fn parse(url: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        let rest = url.trim().strip_prefix("https://github.com/")?;
        let rest = rest.trim_end_matches('/');
        let rest = rest.strip_suffix(".git").unwrap_or(rest);
        let (owner, name) = rest.split_once('/')?;
        let valid = |part: &str| {
            !part.is_empty()
                && !part.starts_with('-')
                && part
                    .chars()
                    .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '-' | '_' | '.'))
        };
        (valid(owner) && valid(name)).then(|| Self {
            owner: owner.to_string(),
            name: name.to_string(),
        })
    }

    /// The repository named by this crate's own manifest.
    pub fn manifest() -> Option<Self> {
        Self::parse(env!("CARGO_PKG_REPOSITORY"))
    }

    pub fn slug(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}/{}", self.owner, self.name)
    }

    pub fn new_issue_url(&self) -> String {
        format!("{}new", self.issue_prefix())
    }

    /// What every issue address in this repository starts with.
    pub fn issue_prefix(&self) -> String {
        format!("https://github.com/{}/{}/issues/", self.owner, self.name)
    }
}

/// Where the GitHub client is: the configured path when set (it must exist), else `gh` on the
/// `PATH`. Looking does not start anything.
pub fn locate_gh(configured: Option<&Path>) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    locate_gh_in(configured, std::env::var_os("PATH").as_deref())
}

fn locate_gh_in(configured: Option<&Path>, path_var: Option<&OsStr>) -> Option<PathBuf> {
    if let Some(configured) = configured.filter(|path| !path.as_os_str().is_empty()) {
        return configured.is_file().then(|| configured.to_path_buf());
    }
    let name = if cfg!(windows) { "gh.exe" } else { "gh" };
    std::env::split_paths(path_var?)
        .map(|dir| dir.join(name))
        .find(|candidate| candidate.is_file())
}

fn list_args(repo: &RepoAddress, fingerprint: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    [
        "issue",
        "list",
        "--repo",
        &repo.slug(),
        "--state",
        "all",
        "--search",
        &format!("{fingerprint} in:body"),
        "--json",
        "number,title,url,body",
        "--limit",
        "5",
    ]
    .map(String::from)
    .to_vec()
}

/// The title is one `--title=` argument, so a title that starts with `-` can never be read as an
/// option. The body goes through standard input, never through the command line.
fn create_args(repo: &RepoAddress, title: &str) -> Vec<String> {
    [
        "issue",
        "create",
        "--repo",
        &repo.slug(),
        &format!("--title={title}"),
        "--body-file",
        "-",
    ]
    .map(String::from)
    .to_vec()
}

/// Finds the issue, among the search hits, whose body carries the fingerprint. A hit counts only
/// when its address is an issue of this repository.
fn parse_duplicates(
    json: &str,
    fingerprint: &str,
    repo: &RepoAddress,
) -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
    let value: serde_json::Value = serde_json::from_str(json)
        .map_err(|err| format!("the issue search answered with something unreadable ({err})"))?;
    let hits = value
        .as_array()
        .ok_or_else(|| "the issue search did not answer with a list".to_string())?;
    let prefix = repo.issue_prefix();
    Ok(hits.iter().find_map(|hit| {
        let body = hit.get("body")?.as_str()?;
        let url = hit.get("url")?.as_str()?;
        (body.contains(fingerprint) && url.starts_with(&prefix)).then(|| url.to_string())
    }))
}

/// Maps what `gh issue create` did to one of the outcomes the channel reports.
fn interpret_create(success: bool, stdout: &str, stderr: &str, repo: &RepoAddress) -> Outcome {
    if success {
        let prefix = repo.issue_prefix();
        let address = stdout
            .lines()
            .map(str::trim)
            .rfind(|line| !line.is_empty())
            .filter(|line| {
                line.strip_prefix(&prefix).is_some_and(|number| {
                    !number.is_empty() && number.chars().all(|c| c.is_ascii_digit())
                })
            });
        return match address {
            Some(address) => Outcome::Created(address.to_string()),
            None => Outcome::Unknown(
                "the GitHub CLI reported success but printed no issue address".to_string(),
            ),
        };
    }

    let shown = if stderr.trim().is_empty() {
        stdout
    } else {
        stderr
    };
    let lower = shown.to_lowercase();
    let any = |needles: &[&str]| needles.iter().any(|needle| lower.contains(needle));
    let (reason, first_step) = if any(&[
        "gh auth login",
        "not logged in",
        "gh_token",
        "authentication",
    ]) {
        (
            "the GitHub CLI is not signed in".to_string(),
            "Run `gh auth login` in a terminal, then submit again",
        )
    } else if any(&["rate limit"]) {
        (
            "GitHub is limiting requests".to_string(),
            "Wait a few minutes, then submit again",
        )
    } else if any(&[
        "could not resolve host",
        "dial tcp",
        "no such host",
        "connection refused",
        "network is unreachable",
        "i/o timeout",
        "tls handshake",
    ]) {
        (
            "the GitHub CLI could not reach GitHub".to_string(),
            "Check the network connection, then submit again",
        )
    } else {
        (
            format!("the GitHub CLI reported: {}", first_line(shown)),
            "Fix the cause named above, then submit again",
        )
    };
    Outcome::NotCreated {
        reason,
        steps: vec![first_step.to_string(), BY_HAND.to_string()],
    }
}

/// The first non-empty line of `text`, scrubbed and cut, for quoting in an answer.
fn first_line(text: &str) -> String {
    let line = text
        .lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .find(|line| !line.is_empty())
        .unwrap_or("no message");
    scrub(line, &ScrubContext::default())
        .chars()
        .take(QUOTED_LINE_LIMIT)
        .collect()
}

enum RunFailure {
    Spawn(String),
    TimedOut,
    Io(String),
}

struct Finished {
    success: bool,
    stdout: String,
    stderr: String,
}

/// Runs the GitHub client for one feedback submission.
pub struct IssueClient {
    gh: PathBuf,
    repo: RepoAddress,
    lookup_limit: Duration,
    create_limit: Duration,
}

impl IssueClient {
    pub fn new(gh: PathBuf, repo: RepoAddress) -> Self {
        Self {
            gh,
            repo,
            lookup_limit: LOOKUP_TIMEOUT,
            create_limit: CREATE_TIMEOUT,
        }
    }

    /// Overrides how long the look-up and the creation may take.
    pub fn with_limits(mut self, lookup: Duration, create: Duration) -> Self {
        self.lookup_limit = lookup;
        self.create_limit = create;
        self
    }

    /// The address of an issue that already carries `fingerprint`, if there is one. An error is
    /// a failed look-up, which the caller reports and then carries on.
    pub async fn find_duplicate(&self, fingerprint: &str) -> Result<Option<String>, String> {
        let args = list_args(&self.repo, fingerprint);
        match self.run(&args, None, self.lookup_limit).await {
            Ok(finished) if finished.success => {
                parse_duplicates(&finished.stdout, fingerprint, &self.repo)
            }
            Ok(finished) => Err(first_line(if finished.stderr.trim().is_empty() {
                &finished.stdout
            } else {
                &finished.stderr
            })),
            Err(RunFailure::Spawn(detail) | RunFailure::Io(detail)) => Err(detail),
            Err(RunFailure::TimedOut) => Err(format!(
                "the issue search did not finish within {} s",
                self.lookup_limit.as_secs()
            )),
        }
    }

    /// Creates the issue. A client that could not even start created nothing; one that did not
    /// answer in time may have, so that case is reported as unknown.
    pub async fn create(&self, title: &str, body: &str) -> Outcome {
        let args = create_args(&self.repo, title);
        match self.run(&args, Some(body), self.create_limit).await {
            Ok(finished) => {
                interpret_create(finished.success, &finished.stdout, &finished.stderr, &self.repo)
            }
            Err(RunFailure::Spawn(detail)) => Outcome::NotCreated {
                reason: format!("the GitHub CLI could not be started: {}", first_line(&detail)),
                steps: vec![
                    "Install the GitHub CLI (https://cli.github.com) and sign in with `gh auth login`"
                        .to_string(),
                    "Or set GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH to the full path of the gh executable".to_string(),
                    BY_HAND.to_string(),
                ],
            },
            Err(RunFailure::TimedOut) => Outcome::Unknown(format!(
                "the GitHub CLI did not answer within {} s; the issue may or may not exist",
                self.create_limit.as_secs()
            )),
            Err(RunFailure::Io(detail)) => Outcome::Unknown(format!(
                "talking to the GitHub CLI failed ({})",
                first_line(&detail)
            )),
        }
    }

    async fn run(
        &self,
        args: &[String],
        stdin: Option<&str>,
        limit: Duration,
    ) -> Result<Finished, RunFailure> {
        let mut command = Command::new(&self.gh);
        command
            .args(args)
            .env("GH_PROMPT_DISABLED", "1")
            .env("NO_COLOR", "1")
            .env("GH_NO_UPDATE_NOTIFIER", "1")
            .stdin(if stdin.is_some() {
                Stdio::piped()
            } else {
                Stdio::null()
            })
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::piped())
            .kill_on_drop(true);
        // The server's own standard handles carry the protocol; the client must not inherit them.
        #[cfg(windows)]
        crate::cli_runner::make_std_handles_private();

        let mut child = command
            .spawn()
            .map_err(|err| RunFailure::Spawn(err.to_string()))?;
        if let (Some(body), Some(mut input)) = (stdin, child.stdin.take()) {
            let body = body.to_owned();
            // A client that exits before reading its input is judged by its exit status alone.
            tokio::spawn(async move {
                let _ = input.write_all(body.as_bytes()).await;
                let _ = input.shutdown().await;
            });
        }
        let output = timeout(limit, child.wait_with_output())
            .await
            .map_err(|_| RunFailure::TimedOut)?
            .map_err(|err| RunFailure::Io(err.to_string()))?;
        Ok(Finished {
            success: output.status.success(),
            stdout: String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout).into_owned(),
            stderr: String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr).into_owned(),
        })
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn repo() -> RepoAddress {
        RepoAddress::parse("https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp").expect("repository")
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_repository_address_is_read_from_the_manifest_url() {
        for url in [
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp",
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp.git",
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/",
            "  https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp  ",
        ] {
            let parsed = RepoAddress::parse(url).unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{url}"));
            assert_eq!(parsed.slug(), "zeegzoog/godot-mcp", "{url}");
        }
        let parsed = repo();
        assert_eq!(
            parsed.new_issue_url(),
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/new"
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parsed.issue_prefix(),
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn anything_that_is_not_a_github_repository_url_is_rejected() {
        for url in [
            "",
            "https://gitlab.com/o/r",
            "http://github.com/o/r",
            "https://github.com/onlyowner",
            "https://github.com/o/r/tree/main",
            "https://github.com//r",
            "https://github.com/o/ r",
            "https://github.com/-o/r",
        ] {
            assert!(RepoAddress::parse(url).is_none(), "{url}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_package_manifest_names_a_usable_repository() {
        let manifest = RepoAddress::manifest().expect("the manifest repository parses");
        assert!(manifest.slug().contains('/'));
        assert!(manifest.new_issue_url().ends_with("/issues/new"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_look_up_arguments_are_fixed_and_carry_no_free_text() {
        assert_eq!(
            list_args(&repo(), "gmfb0123456789ab"),
            [
                "issue",
                "list",
                "--repo",
                "zeegzoog/godot-mcp",
                "--state",
                "all",
                "--search",
                "gmfb0123456789ab in:body",
                "--json",
                "number,title,url,body",
                "--limit",
                "5",
            ]
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_title_travels_as_one_argument_that_cannot_be_read_as_an_option() {
        let args = create_args(&repo(), "--web is not an option");
        assert_eq!(
            args,
            [
                "issue",
                "create",
                "--repo",
                "zeegzoog/godot-mcp",
                "--title=--web is not an option",
                "--body-file",
                "-",
            ]
        );
        assert!(
            !args.iter().any(|arg| arg.starts_with("--label")),
            "no label option"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_created_issue_is_recognized_by_its_address_on_the_last_line() {
        let outcome = interpret_create(
            true,
            "\nCreating issue in zeegzoog/godot-mcp\n\nhttps://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/42\n\n",
            "",
            &repo(),
        );
        assert!(
            matches!(&outcome, Outcome::Created(address) if address == "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/42"),
            "{outcome:?}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn success_without_an_issue_address_is_unknown_not_created() {
        for stdout in [
            "",
            "Created!",
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/",
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/abc",
            "https://github.com/other/repo/issues/7",
            "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/pull/7",
        ] {
            let outcome = interpret_create(true, stdout, "", &repo());
            assert!(
                matches!(outcome, Outcome::Unknown(_)),
                "{stdout:?}: {outcome:?}"
            );
        }
    }

    fn not_created(stderr: &str) -> (String, Vec<String>) {
        match interpret_create(false, "", stderr, &repo()) {
            Outcome::NotCreated { reason, steps } => (reason, steps),
            other => panic!("expected NotCreated, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_failure_names_what_went_wrong_and_what_to_do() {
        let (reason, steps) =
            not_created("To get started with GitHub CLI, please run:  gh auth login");
        assert!(reason.contains("not signed in"), "{reason}");
        assert!(steps[0].contains("gh auth login"), "{steps:?}");

        let (reason, _) = not_created(
            "gh: To use GitHub CLI in automation, set the GH_TOKEN environment variable.",
        );
        assert!(reason.contains("not signed in"), "{reason}");

        let (reason, steps) = not_created(
            "error connecting to api.github.com: dial tcp: lookup api.github.com: no such host",
        );
        assert!(reason.contains("reach GitHub"), "{reason}");
        assert!(steps[0].contains("network"), "{steps:?}");

        let (reason, _) = not_created("API rate limit exceeded for user");
        assert!(reason.contains("limiting"), "{reason}");

        let (reason, steps) = not_created("\nGraphQL: Something unexpected (createIssue)\nmore");
        assert!(reason.contains("Something unexpected"), "{reason}");
        assert!(
            !reason.contains("more"),
            "only the first line is quoted: {reason}"
        );
        assert!(!steps.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_failure_also_points_to_the_hand_filing_path() {
        for stderr in ["gh auth login", "no such host", "rate limit", "boom"] {
            let (_, steps) = not_created(stderr);
            assert!(
                steps.iter().any(|step| step.contains("by hand")),
                "{stderr}: {steps:?}"
            );
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_quoted_failure_line_is_scrubbed() {
        let (reason, _) = not_created("cannot read /home/alice/.config/gh/hosts.yml");
        assert!(
            reason.contains("{path}") && !reason.contains("alice"),
            "{reason}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_duplicate_counts_only_when_the_body_holds_the_fingerprint() {
        let json = r#"[
            {"number": 3, "title": "other", "url": "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/3", "body": "unrelated"},
            {"number": 9, "title": "same", "url": "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/9", "body": "text\n- Fingerprint: gmfb0123456789ab\n"}
        ]"#;
        assert_eq!(
            parse_duplicates(json, "gmfb0123456789ab", &repo()).expect("parsed"),
            Some("https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/9".to_string())
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse_duplicates(json, "gmfbffffffffffff", &repo()).expect("parsed"),
            None
        );
        assert_eq!(
            parse_duplicates("[]", "gmfb0123456789ab", &repo()).expect("parsed"),
            None
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_duplicate_with_a_foreign_address_is_ignored_and_bad_json_is_an_error() {
        let foreign = r#"[{"number": 1, "title": "t", "url": "https://evil.example/issues/1", "body": "gmfb0123456789ab"}]"#;
        assert_eq!(
            parse_duplicates(foreign, "gmfb0123456789ab", &repo()).expect("parsed"),
            None
        );
        assert!(parse_duplicates("not json", "gmfb0123456789ab", &repo()).is_err());
        assert!(parse_duplicates("{}", "gmfb0123456789ab", &repo()).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_client_is_found_where_it_is_configured_or_on_the_path() {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp dir");
        let name = if cfg!(windows) { "gh.exe" } else { "gh" };
        let file = dir.path().join(name);
        std::fs::write(&file, "").expect("write");

        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(Some(&file), None), Some(file.clone()));
        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(Some(&dir.path().join("missing")), None), None);
        let path_var = std::env::join_paths([dir.path()]).expect("path var");
        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(None, Some(path_var.as_os_str())), Some(file));
        let empty = tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp dir");
        let other = std::env::join_paths([empty.path()]).expect("path var");
        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(None, Some(other.as_os_str())), None);
        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(None, None), None);
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_empty_configured_path_counts_as_not_set() {
        assert_eq!(locate_gh_in(Some(std::path::Path::new("")), None), None);
    }
}

```

`src/journal.rs`:

```rs
//! The journal of the agent feedback channel: the notes an agent recorded, what the server
//! observed itself, and the drafts built from both. It lives in memory for the life of the
//! process; the text helpers (scrubbing, fingerprint, link) are in `feedback`.

use crate::feedback::{ScrubContext, build_link, fingerprint, scrub, shape};
use crate::util::lock;
use std::collections::BTreeMap;
use std::sync::Mutex;

pub const MAX_NOTES: usize = 20;
pub const MAX_DRAFTS: usize = 10;
pub const MAX_EVIDENCE: usize = 50;
pub const SUMMARY_LIMIT: usize = 120;
pub const DETAILS_LIMIT: usize = 2_000;
pub const SUGGESTION_LIMIT: usize = 500;
pub const TOOL_LIMIT: usize = 64;
pub const TITLE_LIMIT: usize = 100;
pub const EVIDENCE_MESSAGE_LIMIT: usize = 200;
pub const REPORT_LIMIT: usize = 24_000;
pub const CLIENT_FIELD_LIMIT: usize = 64;
pub const TITLE_PREFIX: &str = "[agent-feedback] ";

const INVALID_NAME: &str = "<invalid name>";
const MISSING_TOOLS_SHOWN: usize = 10;
const FAILURES_PER_TOOL: usize = 5;
const CELL_LIMIT: usize = 100;
const SHORTENED_NOTE: &str = "(details shortened)";

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Kind {
    Bug,
    MissingFeature,
    Improvement,
    Docs,
}

impl Kind {
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match text {
            "bug" => Some(Self::Bug),
            "missing_feature" => Some(Self::MissingFeature),
            "improvement" => Some(Self::Improvement),
            "docs" => Some(Self::Docs),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    pub fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Bug => "bug",
            Self::MissingFeature => "missing_feature",
            Self::Improvement => "improvement",
            Self::Docs => "docs",
        }
    }

    /// The kind of a draft that mixes kinds is the one with the lowest rank.
    fn rank(self) -> u8 {
        match self {
            Self::Bug => 0,
            Self::MissingFeature => 1,
            Self::Improvement => 2,
            Self::Docs => 3,
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum Impact {
    Blocked,
    Workaround,
    Minor,
}

impl Impact {
    pub fn parse(text: &str) -> Option<Self> {
        match text {
            "blocked" => Some(Self::Blocked),
            "workaround" => Some(Self::Workaround),
            "minor" => Some(Self::Minor),
            _ => None,
        }
    }

    fn as_str(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Blocked => "blocked",
            Self::Workaround => "workaround",
            Self::Minor => "minor",
        }
    }

    fn severity(self) -> u8 {
        match self {
            Self::Blocked => 2,
            Self::Workaround => 1,
            Self::Minor => 0,
        }
    }
}

/// Why a call to the channel was refused, with the step that fixes it.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Refusal {
    pub message: String,
    pub hint: String,
}

impl Refusal {
    fn new(message: impl Into<String>, hint: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            message: message.into(),
            hint: hint.into(),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct NewNote {
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub summary: String,
    pub details: String,
    pub tool: Option<String>,
    pub impact: Option<Impact>,
    pub suggestion: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
struct Note {
    id: String,
    kind: Kind,
    summary: String,
    details: String,
    tool: Option<String>,
    impact: Option<Impact>,
    suggestion: Option<String>,
    drafted: bool,
}

/// A note that is not in any draft yet.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct PendingNote {
    pub id: String,
    pub kind: Kind,
    pub summary: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Copy, PartialEq, Eq)]
pub enum EvidenceKind {
    Error,
    InvalidArguments,
    UnknownTool,
}

impl EvidenceKind {
    fn label(self) -> &'static str {
        match self {
            Self::Error => "error",
            Self::InvalidArguments => "invalid arguments",
            Self::UnknownTool => "unknown tool",
        }
    }
}

/// Something the server observed: the same failure of the same tool is one entry with a count.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Evidence {
    pub kind: EvidenceKind,
    pub tool: String,
    pub message: String,
    pub count: u32,
    last_seen: u64,
}

/// The facts of the running setup that a report quotes. Values are free text from other
/// processes, so the report builder sanitizes every one.
#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Environment {
    pub server_version: String,
    pub addon_version: String,
    pub engine_version: String,
    pub platform: String,
    pub bridge_state: String,
    pub feedback_mode: String,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone, Default)]
pub struct DraftRequest {
    pub note_ids: Option<Vec<String>>,
    pub title: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum Outcome {
    Created(String),
    Duplicate(String),
    NotCreated { reason: String, steps: Vec<String> },
    Unknown(String),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub enum DraftState {
    Pending,
    Submitting,
    Submitted(Outcome),
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct Draft {
    pub id: String,
    pub title: String,
    pub body: String,
    pub fingerprint: String,
    /// The prefilled new-issue link, or the reason there is none.
    pub link: Result<String, String>,
    pub note_ids: Vec<String>,
    pub state: DraftState,
}

#[derive(Debug, Clone)]
pub struct BuiltDraft {
    pub draft: Draft,
    /// Notes that are still in no draft.
    pub left_out: Vec<PendingNote>,
}

#[derive(Debug)]
pub enum SubmitRefusal {
    UnknownDraft { known: Vec<String> },
    InProgress,
    AlreadyDone(Outcome),
}

#[derive(Default)]
struct State {
    notes: Vec<Note>,
    next_note: u32,
    evidence: Vec<Evidence>,
    sequence: u64,
    /// Per tool: calls, failures.
    counters: BTreeMap<String, (u32, u32)>,
    drafts: Vec<Draft>,
    next_draft: u32,
    client_name: Option<String>,
    client_version: Option<String>,
}

#[derive(Default)]
pub struct Journal {
    state: Mutex<State>,
}

impl Journal {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self::default()
    }

    /// Remembers which MCP client the session talks to (free text, sanitized).
    pub fn set_client(&self, name: Option<&str>, version: Option<&str>, context: &ScrubContext) {
        let clean = |value: Option<&str>| {
            value
                .map(|text| one_line(&scrub(text, context)))
                .map(|text| text.chars().take(CLIENT_FIELD_LIMIT).collect::<String>())
                .filter(|text| !text.is_empty())
        };
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        state.client_name = clean(name);
        state.client_version = clean(version);
    }

    /// Records one observation. Text is scrubbed first, then checked against its limit.
    pub fn add_note(
        &self,
        note: NewNote,
        context: &ScrubContext,
    ) -> Result<(String, Vec<PendingNote>), Refusal> {
        let summary = one_line(&scrub(&note.summary, context));
        if summary.is_empty() {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                "summary is empty",
                "Describe the observation in one sentence of at most 120 characters",
            ));
        }
        check_length(
            "summary",
            &summary,
            SUMMARY_LIMIT,
            "Shorten it and put the rest in details",
        )?;
        let details = scrub(note.details.trim(), context);
        if details.is_empty() {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                "details is empty",
                "Say what you tried, what came back and what you expected",
            ));
        }
        check_length(
            "details",
            &details,
            DETAILS_LIMIT,
            "Keep the essentials: what you tried, what came back, what you expected",
        )?;
        let suggestion = note
            .suggestion
            .as_deref()
            .map(|text| scrub(text.trim(), context))
            .filter(|text| !text.is_empty());
        if let Some(text) = &suggestion {
            check_length(
                "suggestion",
                text,
                SUGGESTION_LIMIT,
                "State the suggestion in a few sentences",
            )?;
        }
        if let Some(tool) = &note.tool {
            if !is_tool_name(tool) {
                return Err(Refusal::new(
                    format!(
                        "tool {tool:?} is not a tool name: use 1 to {TOOL_LIMIT} characters of a-z, 0-9 and underscore"
                    ),
                    "Pass the tool's name such as godot_add_node, or leave tool out",
                ));
            }
        }

        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        if state.notes.len() >= MAX_NOTES {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                format!("the session already holds {MAX_NOTES} notes, which is the limit"),
                "Draft the notes with godot_draft_feedback and show the report to the human",
            ));
        }
        state.next_note += 1;
        let id = format!("n{}", state.next_note);
        state.notes.push(Note {
            id: id.clone(),
            kind: note.kind,
            summary,
            details,
            tool: note.tool,
            impact: note.impact,
            suggestion,
            drafted: false,
        });
        Ok((id, pending_of(&state)))
    }

    pub fn pending_notes(&self) -> Vec<PendingNote> {
        pending_of(&lock(&self.state))
    }

    /// Counts one finished call of a catalog tool.
    pub fn record_call(&self, tool: &str, failed: bool) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        let counter = state.counters.entry(tool.to_string()).or_insert((0, 0));
        counter.0 = counter.0.saturating_add(1);
        if failed {
            counter.1 = counter.1.saturating_add(1);
        }
    }

    /// Records something the server observed. Only the first line of `message` is kept, scrubbed
    /// and cut; a tool name that is not a plain name is not stored verbatim.
    pub fn record_evidence(
        &self,
        kind: EvidenceKind,
        tool: &str,
        message: &str,
        context: &ScrubContext,
    ) {
        let tool = if kind == EvidenceKind::UnknownTool && !is_plain_name(tool) {
            INVALID_NAME.to_string()
        } else {
            tool.to_string()
        };
        let scrubbed = scrub(message, context);
        let message: String = scrubbed
            .lines()
            .next()
            .unwrap_or("")
            .trim()
            .chars()
            .take(EVIDENCE_MESSAGE_LIMIT)
            .collect();

        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        state.sequence += 1;
        let sequence = state.sequence;
        if let Some(event) = state
            .evidence
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|event| event.kind == kind && event.tool == tool && event.message == message)
        {
            event.count = event.count.saturating_add(1);
            event.last_seen = sequence;
            return;
        }
        if state.evidence.len() >= MAX_EVIDENCE {
            if let Some(oldest) = state
                .evidence
                .iter()
                .enumerate()
                .min_by_key(|(_, event)| event.last_seen)
                .map(|(index, _)| index)
            {
                state.evidence.remove(oldest);
            }
        }
        state.evidence.push(Evidence {
            kind,
            tool,
            message,
            count: 1,
            last_seen: sequence,
        });
    }

    /// Builds a draft from the chosen notes (default: every note not yet drafted) and the
    /// evidence that belongs to them, and stores it.
    pub fn build_draft(
        &self,
        request: DraftRequest,
        environment: &Environment,
        link_base: &str,
        context: &ScrubContext,
    ) -> Result<BuiltDraft, Refusal> {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        if state.drafts.len() >= MAX_DRAFTS {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                format!("the session already holds {MAX_DRAFTS} drafts, which is the limit"),
                "Submit or hand over an existing draft instead of drafting again",
            ));
        }
        let known_ids = || {
            state
                .notes
                .iter()
                .map(|note| note.id.as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join(", ")
        };
        let ids: Vec<String> = match request.note_ids {
            Some(ids) if ids.is_empty() => {
                return Err(Refusal::new(
                    "note_ids is empty",
                    "Omit note_ids to draft every pending note, or list the ids to include",
                ));
            }
            Some(ids) => {
                let mut unique: Vec<String> = Vec::new();
                for id in ids {
                    if !unique.contains(&id) {
                        unique.push(id);
                    }
                }
                unique
            }
            None => state
                .notes
                .iter()
                .filter(|note| !note.drafted)
                .map(|note| note.id.clone())
                .collect(),
        };
        if ids.is_empty() {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                "there are no notes to report",
                "Record an observation with godot_note_feedback first",
            ));
        }
        let unknown: Vec<&str> = ids
            .iter()
            .filter(|id| !state.notes.iter().any(|note| &note.id == *id))
            .map(String::as_str)
            .collect();
        if !unknown.is_empty() {
            return Err(Refusal::new(
                format!("unknown note id(s): {}", unknown.join(", ")),
                format!("Known note ids: {}", known_ids()),
            ));
        }
        let chosen: Vec<Note> = ids
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|id| state.notes.iter().find(|note| &note.id == id).cloned())
            .collect();

        let kind = chosen
            .iter()
            .map(|note| note.kind)
            .min_by_key(|kind| kind.rank())
            .unwrap_or(Kind::Bug);
        let mut tools: Vec<String> = chosen.iter().filter_map(|note| note.tool.clone()).collect();
        tools.sort();
        tools.dedup();

        let missing = if kind == Kind::MissingFeature {
            let mut events: Vec<&Evidence> = state
                .evidence
                .iter()
                .filter(|event| event.kind == EvidenceKind::UnknownTool)
                .collect();
            events.sort_by_key(|event| std::cmp::Reverse((event.count, event.last_seen)));
            events.truncate(MISSING_TOOLS_SHOWN);
            events.into_iter().cloned().collect()
        } else {
            Vec::new()
        };
        let mut failures: Vec<Evidence> = Vec::new();
        for tool in &tools {
            let mut events: Vec<&Evidence> = state
                .evidence
                .iter()
                .filter(|event| event.kind != EvidenceKind::UnknownTool && &event.tool == tool)
                .collect();
            events.sort_by_key(|event| std::cmp::Reverse(event.last_seen));
            events.truncate(FAILURES_PER_TOOL);
            failures.extend(events.into_iter().cloned());
        }
        let counters: Vec<(String, (u32, u32))> = tools
            .iter()
            .filter_map(|tool| {
                state
                    .counters
                    .get(tool)
                    .map(|counts| (tool.clone(), *counts))
            })
            .collect();

        let fingerprint = fingerprint_of(kind, &tools, &failures, &missing, &chosen[0].summary);
        let title = draft_title(request.title.as_deref(), kind, &chosen[0].summary, context);
        let input = ReportInput {
            notes: &chosen,
            missing: &missing,
            failures: &failures,
            counters: &counters,
            environment,
            client: client_label(&state),
            fingerprint: &fingerprint,
            context,
        };
        let body = scrub(&render_within_limit(&input), context);
        let link = if link_base.is_empty() {
            Err("the project's repository address is unknown".to_string())
        } else {
            build_link(link_base, &title, &body)
        };

        state.next_draft += 1;
        let id = format!("d{}", state.next_draft);
        for note in state.notes.iter_mut().filter(|note| ids.contains(&note.id)) {
            note.drafted = true;
        }
        let draft = Draft {
            id,
            title,
            body,
            fingerprint,
            link,
            note_ids: ids,
            state: DraftState::Pending,
        };
        state.drafts.push(draft.clone());
        Ok(BuiltDraft {
            draft,
            left_out: pending_of(&state),
        })
    }

    pub fn draft_ids(&self) -> Vec<String> {
        lock(&self.state)
            .drafts
            .iter()
            .map(|draft| draft.id.clone())
            .collect()
    }

    /// Claims a draft for submission: `Pending` becomes `Submitting`. A draft that is already
    /// being submitted, or already has a final outcome, is refused.
    pub fn begin_submit(&self, id: &str) -> Result<Draft, SubmitRefusal> {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        let known: Vec<String> = state.drafts.iter().map(|draft| draft.id.clone()).collect();
        let Some(draft) = state.drafts.iter_mut().find(|draft| draft.id == id) else {
            return Err(SubmitRefusal::UnknownDraft { known });
        };
        match &draft.state {
            DraftState::Pending => {
                draft.state = DraftState::Submitting;
                Ok(draft.clone())
            }
            DraftState::Submitting => Err(SubmitRefusal::InProgress),
            DraftState::Submitted(outcome) => Err(SubmitRefusal::AlreadyDone(outcome.clone())),
        }
    }

    /// Stores the result of a submission. Only a created issue, or one whose creation cannot be
    /// confirmed, is final; a duplicate or a failure leaves the draft ready to submit again.
    pub fn finish_submit(&self, id: &str, outcome: Outcome) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        if let Some(draft) = state
            .drafts
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|draft| draft.id == id && matches!(draft.state, DraftState::Submitting))
        {
            draft.state = match outcome {
                Outcome::Created(_) | Outcome::Unknown(_) => DraftState::Submitted(outcome),
                Outcome::Duplicate(_) | Outcome::NotCreated { .. } => DraftState::Pending,
            };
        }
    }

    /// Returns a draft that was being submitted to `Pending` (the call was cancelled).
    pub fn abort_submit(&self, id: &str) {
        let mut state = lock(&self.state);
        if let Some(draft) = state
            .drafts
            .iter_mut()
            .find(|draft| draft.id == id && matches!(draft.state, DraftState::Submitting))
        {
            draft.state = DraftState::Pending;
        }
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    fn evidence_for_tests(&self) -> Vec<Evidence> {
        lock(&self.state).evidence.clone()
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    fn counter_for_tests(&self, tool: &str) -> (u32, u32) {
        lock(&self.state)
            .counters
            .get(tool)
            .copied()
            .unwrap_or((0, 0))
    }

    #[cfg(test)]
    fn panic_while_locked_for_tests(&self) {
        let _guard = lock(&self.state);
        panic!("poisoning the journal on purpose");
    }
}

fn pending_of(state: &State) -> Vec<PendingNote> {
    state
        .notes
        .iter()
        .filter(|note| !note.drafted)
        .map(|note| PendingNote {
            id: note.id.clone(),
            kind: note.kind,
            summary: note.summary.clone(),
        })
        .collect()
}

fn client_label(state: &State) -> String {
    match (&state.client_name, &state.client_version) {
        (Some(name), Some(version)) => format!("{name} {version}"),
        (Some(name), None) => name.clone(),
        (None, Some(version)) => version.clone(),
        (None, None) => "unknown".to_string(),
    }
}

fn check_length(field: &str, text: &str, limit: usize, hint: &str) -> Result<(), Refusal> {
    let length = text.chars().count();
    if length > limit {
        return Err(Refusal::new(
            format!("{field} is {length} characters; the limit is {limit}"),
            hint,
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// `[a-z0-9_]{1,64}`: the shape of a tool name given in a note.
fn is_tool_name(text: &str) -> bool {
    (1..=TOOL_LIMIT).contains(&text.chars().count())
        && text
            .chars()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_lowercase() || c.is_ascii_digit() || c == '_')
}

/// `[A-Za-z0-9_.-]{1,64}`: the shape of a requested tool name worth quoting.
fn is_plain_name(text: &str) -> bool {
    (1..=TOOL_LIMIT).contains(&text.chars().count())
        && text
            .chars()
            .all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || matches!(c, '_' | '.' | '-'))
}

fn one_line(text: &str) -> String {
    text.split_whitespace().collect::<Vec<_>>().join(" ")
}

fn draft_title(
    requested: Option<&str>,
    kind: Kind,
    first_summary: &str,
    context: &ScrubContext,
) -> String {
    let custom = requested
        .map(|title| one_line(&scrub(title, context)))
        .filter(|title| !title.is_empty());
    let core = custom.unwrap_or_else(|| format!("{}: {first_summary}", kind.as_str()));
    format!("{TITLE_PREFIX}{core}")
        .chars()
        .take(TITLE_LIMIT)
        .collect()
}

/// Derived from facts that stay the same when two people hit the same problem: the kind, the
/// named tools, the shape of the observed failures and, for a missing feature, the names of the
/// tools that were requested. Free wording counts only when none of those exist.
fn fingerprint_of(
    kind: Kind,
    tools: &[String],
    failures: &[Evidence],
    missing: &[Evidence],
    first_summary: &str,
) -> String {
    let mut shapes: Vec<String> = failures.iter().map(|event| shape(&event.message)).collect();
    shapes.sort();
    shapes.dedup();
    let mut missing_names: Vec<&str> = missing.iter().map(|event| event.tool.as_str()).collect();
    missing_names.sort_unstable();
    let fallback = if tools.is_empty() && shapes.is_empty() && missing_names.is_empty() {
        shape(first_summary)
    } else {
        String::new()
    };
    fingerprint(&format!(
        "v1\n{}\n{}\n{}\n{}\n{}",
        kind.as_str(),
        tools.join(","),
        shapes.join("\u{1f}"),
        missing_names.join(","),
        fallback
    ))
}

struct ReportInput<'a> {
    notes: &'a [Note],
    missing: &'a [Evidence],
    failures: &'a [Evidence],
    counters: &'a [(String, (u32, u32))],
    environment: &'a Environment,
    client: String,
    fingerprint: &'a str,
    context: &'a ScrubContext,
}

/// Renders the report; while it is over the size limit, halves the longest note details.
fn render_within_limit(input: &ReportInput<'_>) -> String {
    let mut details: Vec<String> = input
        .notes
        .iter()
        .map(|note| note.details.clone())
        .collect();
    let mut shortened = false;
    loop {
        let body = render_body(input, &details, shortened);
        if body.chars().count() <= REPORT_LIMIT {
            return body;
        }
        let longest = details
            .iter()
            .enumerate()
            .map(|(index, text)| (index, text.chars().count()))
            .max_by_key(|(_, length)| *length);
        match longest {
            Some((index, length)) if length > 0 => {
                details[index] = details[index].chars().take(length / 2).collect();
                shortened = true;
            }
            _ => return body.chars().take(REPORT_LIMIT).collect(),
        }
    }
}

const AGENT: &str = "*(written by the agent)*";
const SERVER: &str = "*(observed by the server)*";

fn render_body(input: &ReportInput<'_>, details: &[String], shortened: bool) -> String {
    let single = input.notes.len() == 1;
    let mut out = String::new();

    out.push_str(&format!("## Summary {AGENT}\n\n"));
    let summaries: Vec<String> = input
        .notes
        .iter()
        .map(|note| {
            if single {
                note.summary.clone()
            } else {
                format!("{}: {}", note.id, note.summary)
            }
        })
        .collect();
    out.push_str(&fenced(&summaries.join("\n")));

    out.push_str(&format!("\n\n## What happened {AGENT}\n\n"));
    if single {
        out.push_str(&fenced(&details[0]));
    } else {
        for (note, text) in input.notes.iter().zip(details) {
            let tool = note
                .tool
                .as_deref()
                .map(|tool| format!(" · {tool}"))
                .unwrap_or_default();
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "### {} · {}{tool}\n\n",
                note.id,
                note.kind.as_str()
            ));
            out.push_str(&fenced(text));
            out.push_str("\n\n");
        }
        while out.ends_with("\n\n") {
            out.pop();
        }
    }

    let suggestions: Vec<String> = input
        .notes
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|note| {
            note.suggestion.as_ref().map(|text| {
                if single {
                    text.clone()
                } else {
                    format!("{}: {text}", note.id)
                }
            })
        })
        .collect();
    if !suggestions.is_empty() {
        out.push_str(&format!("\n\n## Expected / suggestion {AGENT}\n\n"));
        out.push_str(&fenced(&suggestions.join("\n")));
    }

    let impact = input
        .notes
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|note| note.impact)
        .max_by_key(|impact| impact.severity())
        .map_or("not stated", Impact::as_str);
    out.push_str(&format!("\n\n## Impact {AGENT}\n\n{impact}\n"));

    let cell = |value: &str| cell(value, input.context);
    out.push_str(&format!("\n## Environment {SERVER}\n\n"));
    out.push_str("| Item | Value |\n| --- | --- |\n");
    for (label, value) in [
        ("godot-mcp", input.environment.server_version.as_str()),
        ("Addon", input.environment.addon_version.as_str()),
        ("Godot", input.environment.engine_version.as_str()),
        ("Platform", input.environment.platform.as_str()),
        ("Editor bridge", input.environment.bridge_state.as_str()),
        ("MCP client", input.client.as_str()),
        ("Feedback mode", input.environment.feedback_mode.as_str()),
    ] {
        out.push_str(&format!("| {label} | {} |\n", cell(value)));
    }

    out.push_str(&format!("\n## Server-observed context {SERVER}\n\n"));
    let mut observed = false;
    if !input.missing.is_empty() {
        observed = true;
        out.push_str("Requested tools that do not exist:\n\n");
        for event in input.missing {
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "- `{}`: requested {} time{}\n",
                event.tool,
                event.count,
                plural(event.count)
            ));
        }
        out.push('\n');
    }
    if !input.failures.is_empty() {
        observed = true;
        out.push_str("Recent failures of the tools named in the notes:\n\n");
        let lines: Vec<String> = input
            .failures
            .iter()
            .map(|event| {
                let times = if event.count > 1 {
                    format!(" ×{}", event.count)
                } else {
                    String::new()
                };
                format!(
                    "{} [{}{times}]: {}",
                    event.tool,
                    event.kind.label(),
                    event.message
                )
            })
            .collect();
        out.push_str(&fenced(&lines.join("\n")));
        out.push_str("\n\n");
    }
    if !input.counters.is_empty() {
        observed = true;
        out.push_str("Calls in this session:\n\n");
        for (tool, (calls, failures)) in input.counters {
            out.push_str(&format!(
                "- {tool}: {calls} call{}, {failures} failure{}\n",
                plural(*calls),
                plural(*failures)
            ));
        }
        out.push('\n');
    }
    if !observed {
        out.push_str(
            "No related failures or requests for missing tools were observed in this session.\n\n",
        );
    }

    out.push_str("## Report details\n\n");
    out.push_str(&format!("- Fingerprint: {}\n", input.fingerprint));
    out.push_str("- Format: godot-mcp feedback report v1\n");
    out.push_str("- Drafted by an AI agent through the godot-mcp feedback channel.\n");
    if shortened {
        out.push_str(&format!("- {SHORTENED_NOTE}\n"));
    }
    out
}

fn plural(count: u32) -> &'static str {
    if count == 1 { "" } else { "s" }
}

/// One table cell: sanitized free text on a single line, with the pipe escaped.
fn cell(value: &str, context: &ScrubContext) -> String {
    one_line(&scrub(value, context))
        .replace('|', "\\|")
        .chars()
        .take(CELL_LIMIT)
        .collect()
}

/// Puts text in a fenced code block whose fence is longer than any run of backticks inside it,
/// so the text can neither close the block nor pass for a heading of the report.
fn fenced(text: &str) -> String {
    fenced_as(text, "text")
}

/// Same, with the given language tag after the opening fence.
pub(crate) fn fenced_as(text: &str, language: &str) -> String {
    let mut longest = 0;
    let mut run = 0;
    for c in text.chars() {
        if c == '`' {
            run += 1;
            longest = longest.max(run);
        } else {
            run = 0;
        }
    }
    let fence = "`".repeat(longest.max(2) + 1);
    format!("{fence}{language}\n{text}\n{fence}")
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use crate::feedback::{LINK_BUDGET, ScrubContext, scrub};

    const LINK_BASE: &str = "https://github.com/o/r/issues/new";

    fn ctx() -> ScrubContext {
        ScrubContext::default()
    }

    fn environment() -> Environment {
        Environment {
            server_version: "0.3.0".into(),
            addon_version: "0.3.0".into(),
            engine_version: "4.5.stable".into(),
            platform: "linux x86_64".into(),
            bridge_state: "connected".into(),
            feedback_mode: "full".into(),
        }
    }

    fn note(kind: Kind, summary: &str) -> NewNote {
        NewNote {
            kind,
            summary: summary.into(),
            details: "Tried it and it failed.".into(),
            tool: None,
            impact: None,
            suggestion: None,
        }
    }

    fn add(journal: &Journal, kind: Kind, summary: &str) -> String {
        journal
            .add_note(note(kind, summary), &ctx())
            .expect("note")
            .0
    }

    fn draft(journal: &Journal) -> Draft {
        journal
            .build_draft(DraftRequest::default(), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect("draft")
            .draft
    }

    fn request(note_ids: &[&str]) -> DraftRequest {
        DraftRequest {
            note_ids: Some(note_ids.iter().map(|id| id.to_string()).collect()),
            title: None,
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn notes_get_sequential_ids_and_are_listed_as_pending() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let (first, _) = journal
            .add_note(note(Kind::Bug, "one"), &ctx())
            .expect("note");
        let (second, pending) = journal
            .add_note(note(Kind::Docs, "two"), &ctx())
            .expect("note");
        assert_eq!((first.as_str(), second.as_str()), ("n1", "n2"));
        assert_eq!(pending.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(pending[1].kind, Kind::Docs);
        assert_eq!(pending[1].summary, "two");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_twenty_first_note_is_refused_naming_the_limit() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        for index in 0..MAX_NOTES {
            add(&journal, Kind::Bug, &format!("note {index}"));
        }
        let refusal = journal
            .add_note(note(Kind::Bug, "one too many"), &ctx())
            .expect_err("limit");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("20"), "{}", refusal.message);
        assert!(!refusal.hint.is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn every_text_field_has_a_named_limit() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let mut long_summary = note(Kind::Bug, &"s".repeat(SUMMARY_LIMIT + 1));
        let refusal = journal
            .add_note(long_summary.clone(), &ctx())
            .expect_err("summary");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("summary") && refusal.message.contains("120"));
        long_summary.summary = "s".repeat(SUMMARY_LIMIT);
        assert!(journal.add_note(long_summary, &ctx()).is_ok());

        let mut long_details = note(Kind::Bug, "ok");
        long_details.details = "d".repeat(DETAILS_LIMIT + 1);
        let refusal = journal.add_note(long_details, &ctx()).expect_err("details");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("details") && refusal.message.contains("2000"));

        let mut long_suggestion = note(Kind::Bug, "ok");
        long_suggestion.suggestion = Some("g".repeat(SUGGESTION_LIMIT + 1));
        let refusal = journal
            .add_note(long_suggestion, &ctx())
            .expect_err("suggestion");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("suggestion") && refusal.message.contains("500"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_summary_or_details_and_odd_tool_names_are_refused() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        assert!(journal.add_note(note(Kind::Bug, "   "), &ctx()).is_err());
        let mut no_details = note(Kind::Bug, "ok");
        no_details.details = "  ".into();
        assert!(journal.add_note(no_details, &ctx()).is_err());
        let too_long = "t".repeat(TOOL_LIMIT + 1);
        for bad in [
            "Godot_Add",
            "has space",
            "semi;colon",
            "",
            too_long.as_str(),
        ] {
            let mut with_tool = note(Kind::Bug, "ok");
            with_tool.tool = Some(bad.to_string());
            let refusal = journal.add_note(with_tool, &ctx()).expect_err(bad);
            assert!(
                refusal.message.contains("tool"),
                "{bad}: {}",
                refusal.message
            );
        }
        let mut good = note(Kind::Bug, "ok");
        good.tool = Some("godot_add_node".into());
        assert!(journal.add_note(good, &ctx()).is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn text_is_scrubbed_before_it_is_stored_and_summaries_stay_on_one_line() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let secret = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some("hunter2hunter2".into()),
            ..ScrubContext::default()
        };
        let mut sensitive = note(Kind::Bug, "first line\nsecond hunter2hunter2");
        sensitive.details = "see @maintainer and /home/bob/game/a.gd".into();
        let (_, pending) = journal.add_note(sensitive, &secret).expect("note");
        assert_eq!(pending[0].summary, "first line second {redacted}");
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        assert!(body.contains("@\u{200B}maintainer"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("{path}") && !body.contains("bob"), "{body}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_same_failure_is_stored_once_with_a_count() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        for _ in 0..3 {
            journal.record_evidence(
                EvidenceKind::Error,
                "godot_add_node",
                "Node not found: A",
                &ctx(),
            );
        }
        journal.record_evidence(
            EvidenceKind::Error,
            "godot_add_node",
            "Other problem",
            &ctx(),
        );
        let events = journal.evidence_for_tests();
        assert_eq!(events.len(), 2);
        assert_eq!(events[0].count, 3);
    }

    #[test]
    fn evidence_beyond_the_limit_replaces_the_oldest_and_messages_are_cut() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        for index in 0..=MAX_EVIDENCE {
            journal.record_evidence(
                EvidenceKind::Error,
                "godot_x",
                &format!("failure number {index}"),
                &ctx(),
            );
        }
        let events = journal.evidence_for_tests();
        assert_eq!(events.len(), MAX_EVIDENCE);
        assert!(
            events
                .iter()
                .all(|event| event.message != "failure number 0")
        );

        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.record_evidence(
            EvidenceKind::Error,
            "godot_x",
            &format!("first line {}\nsecond line", "z".repeat(500)),
            &ctx(),
        );
        let event = &journal.evidence_for_tests()[0];
        assert_eq!(event.message.chars().count(), EVIDENCE_MESSAGE_LIMIT);
        assert!(!event.message.contains("second"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_tool_name_that_is_not_a_plain_name_is_not_stored_verbatim() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::UnknownTool, "ok.name-1_x", "", &ctx());
        journal.record_evidence(
            EvidenceKind::UnknownTool,
            "bad`name\n## Heading",
            "",
            &ctx(),
        );
        let names: Vec<String> = journal
            .evidence_for_tests()
            .into_iter()
            .map(|event| event.tool)
            .collect();
        assert_eq!(names, ["ok.name-1_x", "<invalid name>"]);
    }

    #[test]
    fn calls_and_failures_are_counted_per_tool() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.record_call("godot_add_node", false);
        journal.record_call("godot_add_node", true);
        journal.record_call("godot_add_node", true);
        journal.record_call("godot_get_version", false);
        assert_eq!(journal.counter_for_tests("godot_add_node"), (3, 2));
        assert_eq!(journal.counter_for_tests("godot_get_version"), (1, 0));
        assert_eq!(journal.counter_for_tests("never_called"), (0, 0));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_draft_needs_at_least_one_note() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let refusal = journal
            .build_draft(DraftRequest::default(), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect_err("nothing to draft");
        assert!(
            refusal.hint.contains("godot_note_feedback"),
            "{}",
            refusal.hint
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_draft_takes_every_undrafted_note_by_default_and_marks_them() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Docs, "readme");
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "crash");
        let built = journal
            .build_draft(DraftRequest::default(), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect("draft");
        assert_eq!(built.draft.id, "d1");
        assert_eq!(built.draft.note_ids, ["n1", "n2"]);
        assert!(built.left_out.is_empty());
        assert!(journal.pending_notes().is_empty());
    }

    #[test]
    fn explicit_notes_are_drafted_and_the_rest_are_reported_as_left_out() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Docs, "readme");
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "crash");
        let built = journal
            .build_draft(request(&["n2"]), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect("draft");
        assert_eq!(built.draft.note_ids, ["n2"]);
        assert_eq!(built.left_out.len(), 1);
        assert_eq!(built.left_out[0].id, "n1");
        // An already drafted note may be selected again.
        let again = journal
            .build_draft(request(&["n2"]), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect("redraft");
        assert_eq!(again.draft.id, "d2");
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_and_empty_selections_are_refused_with_the_known_ids() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "crash");
        let refusal = journal
            .build_draft(request(&["n9"]), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect_err("unknown id");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("n9") && refusal.hint.contains("n1"));
        let refusal = journal
            .build_draft(request(&[]), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect_err("empty selection");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("note_ids"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_eleventh_draft_is_refused_naming_the_limit() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "crash");
        for _ in 0..MAX_DRAFTS {
            journal
                .build_draft(request(&["n1"]), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
                .expect("draft within the limit");
        }
        let refusal = journal
            .build_draft(DraftRequest::default(), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &ctx())
            .expect_err("limit");
        assert!(refusal.message.contains("10"), "{}", refusal.message);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_title_is_prefixed_single_line_scrubbed_and_shortened() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::MissingFeature, "no scene creation");
        assert_eq!(
            draft(&journal).title,
            "[agent-feedback] missing_feature: no scene creation"
        );

        let titled = |title: &str| {
            let journal = Journal::new();
            add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
            journal
                .build_draft(
                    DraftRequest {
                        note_ids: None,
                        title: Some(title.into()),
                    },
                    &environment(),
                    LINK_BASE,
                    &ctx(),
                )
                .expect("draft")
                .draft
                .title
        };
        assert_eq!(
            titled("Custom\ntitle @bob"),
            "[agent-feedback] Custom title @\u{200B}bob"
        );
        let long = titled(&"w".repeat(500));
        assert_eq!(long.chars().count(), TITLE_LIMIT);
        assert!(long.starts_with(TITLE_PREFIX));
        assert_eq!(titled("   "), "[agent-feedback] bug: x");
