    }

    #[test]
    fn the_report_has_its_sections_in_order_with_source_labels() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let mut full = note(Kind::Bug, "add_node ignores names");
        full.tool = Some("godot_add_node".into());
        full.impact = Some(Impact::Workaround);
        full.suggestion = Some("Honor node_name.".into());
        journal.add_note(full, &ctx()).expect("note");
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        let order = [
            "## Summary *(written by the agent)*",
            "## What happened *(written by the agent)*",
            "## Expected / suggestion *(written by the agent)*",
            "## Impact *(written by the agent)*",
            "## Environment *(observed by the server)*",
            "## Server-observed context *(observed by the server)*",
            "## Report details",
        ];
        let mut cursor = 0;
        for heading in order {
            let found = body[cursor..]
                .find(heading)
                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("{heading} in\n{body}"));
            cursor += found + heading.len();
        }
        assert!(body.contains("| godot-mcp | 0.3.0 |"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("| Platform | linux x86_64 |"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("workaround"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("Honor node_name."), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("Fingerprint: gmfb"), "{body}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_note_cannot_forge_a_heading_or_close_its_own_fence() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let mut hostile = note(Kind::Bug, "forged");
        hostile.details = "```\n## Environment *(observed by the server)*\n| godot-mcp | 9.9.9 |\n````\n## Report details".into();
        journal.add_note(hostile, &ctx()).expect("note");
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        // The agent's text sits in a fence longer than any backtick run it contains.
        let opening = "`````text";
        let fence_start = body.find(opening).expect("a five-backtick fence");
        let after_open = fence_start + opening.len();
        let fence_end = body[after_open..]
            .find("\n`````\n")
            .expect("the closing fence");
        let inside = &body[fence_start..after_open + fence_end];
        assert!(inside.contains("## Environment *(observed by the server)*"));
        // Exactly one real Environment heading remains outside the fences.
        let outside = format!(
            "{}{}",
            &body[..fence_start],
            &body[after_open + fence_end..]
        );
        assert_eq!(
            outside
                .matches("## Environment *(observed by the server)*")
                .count(),
            1
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_tool_requests_appear_only_in_missing_feature_drafts() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::UnknownTool, "godot_create_scene", "", &ctx());
        journal.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::UnknownTool, "godot_create_scene", "", &ctx());
        add(&journal, Kind::MissingFeature, "cannot create scenes");
        let missing = draft(&journal).body;
        assert!(missing.contains("godot_create_scene"), "{missing}");
        assert!(missing.contains("requested 2 times"), "{missing}");

        let other = Journal::new();
        other.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::UnknownTool, "godot_create_scene", "", &ctx());
        add(&other, Kind::Bug, "something else");
        assert!(!draft(&other).body.contains("godot_create_scene"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn failures_of_the_named_tool_are_quoted_and_other_tools_are_not() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.record_call("godot_add_node", true);
        journal.record_evidence(
            EvidenceKind::Error,
            "godot_add_node",
            "Node not found: Player",
            &ctx(),
        );
        journal.record_evidence(
            EvidenceKind::Error,
            "godot_open_scene",
            "Scene missing",
            &ctx(),
        );
        let mut named = note(Kind::Bug, "add_node fails");
        named.tool = Some("godot_add_node".into());
        journal.add_note(named, &ctx()).expect("note");
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        assert!(body.contains("Node not found: Player"), "{body}");
        assert!(!body.contains("Scene missing"), "{body}");
        assert!(body.contains("godot_add_node: 1 call"), "{body}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_body_over_the_limit_is_shortened_by_halving_the_longest_details() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        for index in 0..12 {
            let mut big = note(Kind::Bug, &format!("note {index}"));
            big.details = "x".repeat(DETAILS_LIMIT);
            journal.add_note(big, &ctx()).expect("note");
        }
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        assert!(
            body.chars().count() <= REPORT_LIMIT,
            "{}",
            body.chars().count()
        );
        assert!(body.contains("(details shortened)"));
        assert!(body.contains("note 11"), "every note keeps its summary");
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_environment_table_cannot_be_broken_by_the_values() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        journal.set_client(Some("My|Client\nname"), Some("1.0"), &ctx());
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
        let body = draft(&journal).body;
        assert!(
            body.contains("| MCP client | My\\|Client name 1.0 |"),
            "{body}"
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_finished_body_holds_no_secret_and_scrubbing_it_again_changes_nothing() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        let context = ScrubContext {
            secret: Some("topsecretvalue".into()),
            project_dir: Some("/home/alice/game".into()),
            home_dir: Some("/home/alice".into()),
        };
        journal.set_client(Some("client topsecretvalue"), None, &ctx());
        let mut sensitive = note(Kind::Bug, "x");
        sensitive.details = "path /home/alice/game/a.gd".into();
        journal.add_note(sensitive, &ctx()).expect("note");
        let built = journal
            .build_draft(DraftRequest::default(), &environment(), LINK_BASE, &context)
            .expect("draft");
        assert!(!built.draft.body.contains("topsecretvalue"));
        assert!(!built.draft.body.contains("alice"));
        assert_eq!(scrub(&built.draft.body, &context), built.draft.body);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_draft_carries_a_link_within_the_budget() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
        let link = draft(&journal).link.expect("link");
        assert!(link.starts_with(LINK_BASE) && link.len() <= LINK_BUDGET);
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_fingerprint_follows_stable_facts_not_wording() {
        let fingerprint_of = |kind: Kind, tool: &str, summary: &str, message: &str| {
            let journal = Journal::new();
            journal.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::Error, tool, message, &ctx());
            let mut named = note(kind, summary);
            named.tool = Some(tool.into());
            journal.add_note(named, &ctx()).expect("note");
            draft(&journal).fingerprint
        };
        let first = fingerprint_of(
            Kind::Bug,
            "godot_add_node",
            "wording one",
            "Node 12 not found in /a/b",
        );
        let second = fingerprint_of(
            Kind::Bug,
            "godot_add_node",
            "other words",
            "node 99 not found in /c/d",
        );
        assert_eq!(first, second);
        let other_kind = fingerprint_of(
            Kind::Docs,
            "godot_add_node",
            "wording one",
            "Node 12 not found in /a/b",
        );
        let other_tool = fingerprint_of(
            Kind::Bug,
            "godot_remove_node",
            "wording one",
            "Node 12 not found in /a/b",
        );
        let other_error = fingerprint_of(
            Kind::Bug,
            "godot_add_node",
            "wording one",
            "a different error",
        );
        assert_ne!(first, other_kind);
        assert_ne!(first, other_tool);
        assert_ne!(first, other_error);
    }

    #[test]
    fn with_no_tool_or_evidence_the_fingerprint_falls_back_to_the_summary_words() {
        let a = Journal::new();
        add(&a, Kind::Improvement, "Better  error MESSAGES");
        let b = Journal::new();
        add(&b, Kind::Improvement, "better error messages");
        let c = Journal::new();
        add(&c, Kind::Improvement, "something else entirely");
        let (from_a, from_b, from_c) = (
            draft(&a).fingerprint,
            draft(&b).fingerprint,
            draft(&c).fingerprint,
        );
        assert_eq!(from_a, from_b);
        assert_ne!(from_a, from_c);
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_missing_feature_fingerprint_uses_the_requested_tool_names() {
        let build = |tool: &str| {
            let journal = Journal::new();
            journal.record_evidence(EvidenceKind::UnknownTool, tool, "", &ctx());
            add(&journal, Kind::MissingFeature, "wording does not matter");
            draft(&journal).fingerprint
        };
        assert_eq!(build("godot_create_scene"), build("godot_create_scene"));
        assert_ne!(build("godot_create_scene"), build("godot_get_debug_log"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn a_draft_moves_through_its_states() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
        let id = draft(&journal).id;

        let snapshot = journal.begin_submit(&id).expect("pending to submitting");
        assert_eq!(snapshot.id, id);
        assert!(matches!(
            journal.begin_submit(&id),
            Err(SubmitRefusal::InProgress)
        ));

        journal.finish_submit(
            &id,
            Outcome::NotCreated {
                reason: "offline".into(),
                steps: vec![],
            },
        );
        assert!(
            journal.begin_submit(&id).is_ok(),
            "a failed submission can be retried"
        );

        journal.finish_submit(
            &id,
            Outcome::Duplicate("https://github.com/o/r/issues/1".into()),
        );
        assert!(
            journal.begin_submit(&id).is_ok(),
            "a duplicate can be retried with allow_duplicate"
        );

        journal.abort_submit(&id);
        assert!(
            journal.begin_submit(&id).is_ok(),
            "a cancelled call leaves the draft retriable"
        );

        journal.finish_submit(
            &id,
            Outcome::Created("https://github.com/o/r/issues/2".into()),
        );
        match journal.begin_submit(&id) {
            Err(SubmitRefusal::AlreadyDone(Outcome::Created(address))) => {
                assert!(address.ends_with("/2"));
            }
            other => panic!("expected the stored outcome, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_draft_lists_the_known_ones() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
        draft(&journal);
        match journal.begin_submit("d7") {
            Err(SubmitRefusal::UnknownDraft { known }) => assert_eq!(known, ["d1"]),
            other => panic!("expected an unknown draft, got {other:?}"),
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn an_unknown_outcome_is_stored_so_a_repeat_cannot_create_twice() {
        let journal = Journal::new();
        add(&journal, Kind::Bug, "x");
        let id = draft(&journal).id;
        journal.begin_submit(&id).expect("submitting");
        journal.finish_submit(&id, Outcome::Unknown("no address printed".into()));
        assert!(matches!(
            journal.begin_submit(&id),
            Err(SubmitRefusal::AlreadyDone(Outcome::Unknown(_)))
        ));
    }

    #[test]
    fn the_journal_survives_a_panic_in_another_task() {
        let journal = std::sync::Arc::new(Journal::new());
        let poisoner = journal.clone();
        let _ = std::thread::spawn(move || poisoner.panic_while_locked_for_tests()).join();
        assert!(
            journal
                .add_note(note(Kind::Bug, "still works"), &ctx())
                .is_ok()
        );
    }
}

`src/main.rs`:

```rs
use godot_mcp::config::Config;
use godot_mcp::error::Result;
use godot_mcp::server::McpServer;
use tracing_subscriber::{EnvFilter, layer::SubscriberExt, util::SubscriberInitExt};

#[tokio::main]
async fn main() -> Result<()> {
    // Direct all diagnostic logs to stderr to maintain pristine JSON-RPC on stdout
    tracing_subscriber::registry()
        .with(
            EnvFilter::try_from_default_env()
                .unwrap_or_else(|_| EnvFilter::new("godot_mcp=info,warn")),
        )
        .with(tracing_subscriber::fmt::layer().with_writer(std::io::stderr))
        .init();

    let config = Config::load();
    let server = McpServer::new(&config);

    server.run_stdio().await
}
```

`src/paths.rs`:

```rs
//! Path and identifier checks shared by the tools that touch the file system.

use std::path::{Component, Path, PathBuf};

const RES_PREFIX: &str = "res://";

/// Matches `^[A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*$`, the only shape accepted for class and node identifiers.
pub fn is_valid_identifier(name: &str) -> bool {
    let mut chars = name.chars();
    match chars.next() {
        Some(first) if first.is_ascii_alphabetic() || first == '_' => {
            chars.all(|c| c.is_ascii_alphanumeric() || c == '_')
        }
        _ => false,
    }
}

/// Checks that `dir` exists and is the root of a Godot project.
pub fn ensure_project_dir(dir: &Path) -> Result<(), String> {
    if !dir.is_dir() {
        return Err(format!(
            "Project directory does not exist: {}",
            dir.display()
        ));
    }
    if !dir.join("project.godot").is_file() {
        return Err(format!(
            "Not a Godot project directory (project.godot not found): {}",
            dir.display()
        ));
    }
    Ok(())
}

/// Finds the project the server was started in: the first of `cwd` and its ancestors that
/// contains `project.godot`, otherwise the only direct subdirectory of `cwd` that does (for a
/// repository that keeps the game in a folder such as `app/`).
pub fn find_project(cwd: &Path) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    if let Some(dir) = cwd
        .ancestors()
        .find(|dir| dir.join("project.godot").is_file())
    {
        return Ok(dir.to_path_buf());
    }

    let mut children: Vec<PathBuf> = std::fs::read_dir(cwd)
        .map(|entries| {
            entries
                .filter_map(|entry| entry.ok())
                .map(|entry| entry.path())
                .filter(|path| path.join("project.godot").is_file())
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default();
    children.sort();

    match children.as_slice() {
        [single] => Ok(single.clone()),
        [] => Err(format!(
            "No project.godot found in {} or its parent directories; pass project_path",
            cwd.display()
        )),
        several => Err(format!(
            "Several Godot projects found in {} ({}); pass project_path to choose one",
            cwd.display(),
            several
                .iter()
                .map(|path| path.display().to_string())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join(", ")
        )),
    }
}

/// Resolves a `res://` path, a project-relative path, or an absolute path to a location
/// inside `project`.
///
/// Rejects `..` segments (a name merely containing two dots is fine) and anything that lands
/// outside the project, including through symlinks of the part that already exists.
pub fn resolve_in_project(project: &Path, requested: &str) -> Result<PathBuf, String> {
    let trimmed = requested.trim();
    if trimmed.is_empty() {
        return Err("Path is empty".into());
    }

    let relative = trimmed.strip_prefix(RES_PREFIX).unwrap_or(trimmed);
    let normalized = relative.replace('\\', "/");
    let path = Path::new(&normalized);

    let is_absolute = path.has_root()
        || path
            .components()
            .any(|component| matches!(component, Component::Prefix(_)));
    let full = if is_absolute {
        path.to_path_buf()
    } else {
        project.join(path)
    };

    let mut resolved = PathBuf::new();
    for component in full.components() {
        match component {
            Component::ParentDir => {
                return Err(format!(
                    "Parent directory segments ('..') are not allowed: {requested}"
                ));
            }
            Component::CurDir => {}
            other => resolved.push(other.as_os_str()),
        }
    }

    let project_root = project.canonicalize().map_err(|err| {
        format!(
            "Cannot access project directory {}: {err}",
            project.display()
        )
    })?;
    let existing_ancestor = resolved
        .ancestors()
        .find(|candidate| candidate.exists())
        .and_then(|candidate| candidate.canonicalize().ok());
    match existing_ancestor {
        Some(ancestor) if ancestor.starts_with(&project_root) => Ok(resolved),
        _ => Err(format!(
            "Path is outside the project directory {}: {requested}",
            project.display()
        )),
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    fn project() -> tempfile::TempDir {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp dir");
        std::fs::write(dir.path().join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").expect("write");
        dir
    }

    #[test]
    fn identifiers_follow_the_strict_pattern() {
        for valid in ["Node", "_private", "Player2D", "a"] {
            assert!(is_valid_identifier(valid), "{valid}");
        }
        for invalid in ["", "2D", "Foo Bar", "Foo\nBar", "a-b", "Node;", "ä"] {
            assert!(!is_valid_identifier(invalid), "{invalid:?}");
        }
    }

    #[test]
    fn res_prefix_and_backslashes_resolve_under_the_project() {
        let dir = project();
        let resolved = resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "res://scripts\\player.gd").expect("resolve");
        assert_eq!(resolved, dir.path().join("scripts").join("player.gd"));
    }

    #[test]
    fn parent_segments_are_rejected_but_double_dots_in_names_are_not() {
        let dir = project();
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "a/../b.gd").is_err());
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "../b.gd").is_err());
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "my..file.gd").is_ok());
    }

    #[test]
    fn absolute_paths_must_stay_inside_the_project() {
        let dir = project();
        let outside = tempfile::tempdir().expect("outside");

        let inside_file = dir.path().join("sub").join("a.gd");
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), &inside_file.to_string_lossy()).is_ok());

        let outside_file = outside.path().join("a.gd");
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), &outside_file.to_string_lossy()).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn empty_paths_are_rejected() {
        let dir = project();
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "  ").is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn project_directory_needs_project_godot() {
        let dir = project();
        assert!(ensure_project_dir(dir.path()).is_ok());

        let empty = tempfile::tempdir().expect("empty");
        let err = ensure_project_dir(empty.path()).expect_err("no project.godot");
        assert!(err.contains("project.godot"));

        let missing = empty.path().join("missing");
        assert!(ensure_project_dir(&missing).is_err());
    }

    #[test]
    fn find_project_walks_up_from_the_working_directory() {
        let dir = project();
        let deep = dir.path().join("scenes").join("levels");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&deep).expect("mkdir");

        assert_eq!(find_project(dir.path()).expect("cwd"), dir.path());
        assert_eq!(find_project(&deep).expect("ancestor"), dir.path());
    }

    #[test]
    fn find_project_accepts_a_single_child_project() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().expect("root");
        let app = root.path().join("app");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&app).expect("mkdir app");
        std::fs::write(app.join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").expect("write");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(root.path().join("docs")).expect("mkdir docs");

        assert_eq!(find_project(root.path()).expect("child"), app);
    }

    #[test]
    fn find_project_prefers_the_working_directory_over_children() {
        let dir = project();
        let nested = dir.path().join("demo");
        std::fs::create_dir_all(&nested).expect("mkdir");
        std::fs::write(nested.join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").expect("write");

        assert_eq!(find_project(dir.path()).expect("cwd"), dir.path());
    }

    #[test]
    fn find_project_without_a_project_asks_for_project_path() {
        let empty = tempfile::tempdir().expect("empty");
        let err = find_project(empty.path()).expect_err("no project");
        assert!(err.contains("project_path"), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn find_project_lists_several_child_projects_instead_of_guessing() {
        let root = tempfile::tempdir().expect("root");
        for name in ["game", "tools"] {
            let child = root.path().join(name);
            std::fs::create_dir_all(&child).expect("mkdir");
            std::fs::write(child.join("project.godot"), "config_version=5\n").expect("write");
        }

        let err = find_project(root.path()).expect_err("ambiguous");
        assert!(err.contains("game") && err.contains("tools"), "{err}");
        assert!(err.contains("project_path"), "{err}");
    }

    #[cfg(unix)]
    #[test]
    fn symlinks_cannot_be_used_to_leave_the_project() {
        let dir = project();
        let outside = tempfile::tempdir().expect("outside");
        std::os::unix::fs::symlink(outside.path(), dir.path().join("link")).expect("symlink");
        assert!(resolve_in_project(dir.path(), "link/a.gd").is_err());
    }
}
```

`src/protocol.rs`:

```rs
use serde::{Deserialize, Serialize};
use serde_json::Value;

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct JsonRpcResponse {
    pub jsonrpc: String,
    pub id: Value,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub result: Option<Value>,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub error: Option<JsonRpcError>,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct JsonRpcError {
    pub code: i64,
    pub message: String,
    #[serde(skip_serializing_if = "Option::is_none")]
    pub data: Option<Value>,
}

impl JsonRpcResponse {
    pub fn success(id: Value, result: Value) -> Self {
        Self {
            jsonrpc: "2.0".to_string(),
            id,
            result: Some(result),
            error: None,
        }
    }

    pub fn error(id: Value, code: i64, message: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            jsonrpc: "2.0".to_string(),
            id,
            result: None,
            error: Some(JsonRpcError {
                code,
                message: message.into(),
                data: None,
            }),
        }
    }
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize, Clone)]
pub struct Tool {
    pub name: String,
    pub description: String,
    #[serde(rename = "inputSchema")]
    pub input_schema: Value,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
pub struct ToolResult {
    pub content: Vec<ToolContent>,
    #[serde(rename = "isError", skip_serializing_if = "std::ops::Not::not")]
    pub is_error: bool,
}

#[derive(Debug, Serialize, Deserialize)]
#[serde(tag = "type")]
pub enum ToolContent {
    #[serde(rename = "text")]
    Text { text: String },
    #[serde(rename = "image")]
    Image {
        data: String,
        #[serde(rename = "mimeType")]
        mime_type: String,
    },
}

impl ToolResult {
    pub fn text(content: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            content: vec![ToolContent::Text {
                text: content.into(),
            }],
            is_error: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn image(base64_data: impl Into<String>, mime_type: impl Into<String>) -> Self {
        Self {
            content: vec![ToolContent::Image {
                data: base64_data.into(),
                mime_type: mime_type.into(),
            }],
            is_error: false,
        }
    }

    /// An image preceded by a short text note (e.g. its dimensions).
    pub fn image_with_text(
        note: impl Into<String>,
        base64_data: impl Into<String>,
        mime_type: impl Into<String>,
    ) -> Self {
        Self {
            content: vec![
                ToolContent::Text { text: note.into() },
                ToolContent::Image {
                    data: base64_data.into(),
                    mime_type: mime_type.into(),
                },
            ],
            is_error: false,
        }
    }

    pub fn error(message: impl Into<String>, recovery_hints: &[&str]) -> Self {
        let mut text = format!("Error: {}", message.into());
        if !recovery_hints.is_empty() {
            text.push_str("\n\nActionable recovery suggestions:\n");
            for hint in recovery_hints {
                text.push_str(&format!("- {hint}\n"));
            }
        }
        Self {
            content: vec![ToolContent::Text { text }],
            is_error: true,
        }
    }
}

```

`src/server.rs`:

```rs
use crate::bridge::BridgeClient;
use crate::cli_runner::CliRunner;
use crate::config::Config;
use crate::error::Result;
use crate::protocol::JsonRpcResponse;
use crate::tools::ToolManager;
use crate::util::lock;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::collections::HashMap;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader};
use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::task::{AbortHandle, JoinSet};
use tracing::{error, info};

/// Newest first: a client asking for anything else is answered with the first entry.
const SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS: [&str; 3] = ["2025-06-18", "2025-03-26", "2024-11-05"];

const PARSE_ERROR: i64 = -32700;
const INVALID_REQUEST: i64 = -32600;
const METHOD_NOT_FOUND: i64 = -32601;
const INVALID_PARAMS: i64 = -32602;

/// Tool calls that are still running, keyed by the JSON text of their request id.
type InFlight = Arc<Mutex<HashMap<String, AbortHandle>>>;

pub struct McpServer {
    tool_manager: Arc<ToolManager>,
}

enum Incoming {
    Request {
        id: Value,
        method: String,
        params: Value,
    },
    Notification {
        method: String,
        params: Value,
    },
    Invalid(JsonRpcResponse),
}

impl McpServer {
    pub fn new(config: &Config) -> Self {
        // The catalog is built on first use, so the feedback mode must be known before it.
        ToolManager::configure_catalog(config.feedback);
        let godot_bin = config.resolve_godot_path();
        info!("Using Godot binary at: {}", godot_bin.display());

        let bridge = BridgeClient::new(config.debug_port);
        let cli = CliRunner::new(godot_bin);
        let tool_manager = Arc::new(
            ToolManager::new(bridge, cli).with_feedback(config.feedback, config.gh_path.clone()),
        );

        Self { tool_manager }
    }

    /// Run standard I/O loop processing JSON-RPC messages.
    ///
    /// Tool calls run concurrently, so a slow editor command never delays `ping` or other
    /// requests, and `notifications/cancelled` can abort one that is still running.
    pub async fn run_stdio(self) -> Result<()> {
        let (out_tx, mut out_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel::<String>();
        let writer = tokio::spawn(async move {
            let mut stdout = tokio::io::stdout();
            while let Some(line) = out_rx.recv().await {
                if write_line(&mut stdout, &line).await.is_err() {
                    break;
                }
            }
        });

        let in_flight: InFlight = Arc::default();
        let mut tasks: JoinSet<()> = JoinSet::new();
        let mut reader = BufReader::new(tokio::io::stdin());
        let mut buffer = Vec::new();

        info!("Godot MCP server listening on stdio");

        loop {
            buffer.clear();
            if reader.read_until(b'\n', &mut buffer).await? == 0 {
                break;
            }
            while tasks.try_join_next().is_some() {}

            let text = String::from_utf8_lossy(&buffer);
            let line = text.trim();
            if line.is_empty() {
                continue;
            }

            match parse_message(line) {
                Incoming::Invalid(response) => send(&out_tx, &response),
                Incoming::Notification { method, params } => {
                    Self::handle_notification(&method, &params, &in_flight);
                }
                Incoming::Request { id, method, params } if method == "tools/call" => {
                    self.spawn_tool_call(&mut tasks, &in_flight, &out_tx, id, &params);
                }
                Incoming::Request { id, method, params } => {
                    send(&out_tx, &self.handle_request(id, &method, &params));
                }
            }
        }

        // The client closed stdin: let running calls finish so their responses are not lost.
        while tasks.join_next().await.is_some() {}
        drop(out_tx);
        let _ = writer.await;

        info!("Godot MCP server stdio session closed");
        Ok(())
    }

    /// Requests that are answered immediately, without touching the editor.
    fn handle_request(&self, id: Value, method: &str, params: &Value) -> JsonRpcResponse {
        match method {
            "initialize" => {
                let requested = params.get("protocolVersion").and_then(Value::as_str);
                let version = SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS
                    .iter()
                    .find(|supported| Some(**supported) == requested)
                    .unwrap_or(&SUPPORTED_PROTOCOL_VERSIONS[0]);
                let client = params.get("clientInfo");
                let client_field = |key: &str| {
                    client
                        .and_then(|info| info.get(key))
                        .and_then(Value::as_str)
                };
                self.tool_manager
                    .note_client(client_field("name"), client_field("version"));
                let mut result = json!({
                    "protocolVersion": version,
                    "capabilities": { "tools": { "listChanged": false } },
                    "serverInfo": {
                        "name": "godot-mcp",
                        "version": env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION")
                    }
                });
                if let Some(guidance) = self.tool_manager.guidance() {
                    result["instructions"] = json!(guidance);
                }
                JsonRpcResponse::success(id, result)
            }
            "ping" => JsonRpcResponse::success(id, json!({})),
            "tools/list" => {
                JsonRpcResponse::success(id, json!({ "tools": ToolManager::list_tools() }))
            }
            _ => {
                JsonRpcResponse::error(id, METHOD_NOT_FOUND, format!("Method '{method}' not found"))
            }
        }
    }

    fn handle_notification(method: &str, params: &Value, in_flight: &InFlight) {
        if method != "notifications/cancelled" {
            return;
        }
        let Some(request_id) = params.get("requestId") else {
            return;
        };
        if let Some(handle) = lock(in_flight).remove(&request_id.to_string()) {
            handle.abort();
            info!("Cancelled request {request_id}");
        }
    }

    fn spawn_tool_call(
        &self,
        tasks: &mut JoinSet<()>,
        in_flight: &InFlight,
        out_tx: &mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>,
        id: Value,
        params: &Value,
    ) {
        let Some(name) = params.get("name").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
            send(
                out_tx,
                &JsonRpcResponse::error(id, INVALID_PARAMS, "Invalid params: 'name' is required"),
            );
            return;
        };
        if !ToolManager::has_tool(name) {
            // A request for a tool that does not exist is a request for a missing capability.
            self.tool_manager.record_unknown_tool(name);
            send(
                out_tx,
                &JsonRpcResponse::error(id, INVALID_PARAMS, format!("Unknown tool: {name}")),
            );
            return;
        }
        let name = name.to_string();
        let arguments = match params.get("arguments") {
            None | Some(Value::Null) => json!({}),
            Some(arguments) => arguments.clone(),
        };

        let key = id.to_string();
        let manager = self.tool_manager.clone();
        let responses = out_tx.clone();
        let registry = in_flight.clone();
        let task_key = key.clone();

        // Holding the lock until the handle is registered keeps a fast task from removing
        // its entry before it exists.
        let mut running = lock(in_flight);
        let handle = tasks.spawn(async move {
            let result = manager.call_tool(&name, arguments).await;
            let payload = serde_json::to_value(result).unwrap_or_else(|err| {
                json!({
                    "content": [{"type": "text", "text": format!("Internal error: {err}")}],
                    "isError": true
                })
            });
            send(&responses, &JsonRpcResponse::success(id, payload));
            lock(&registry).remove(&task_key);
        });
        running.insert(key, handle);
    }
}

fn parse_message(line: &str) -> Incoming {
    let value: Value = match serde_json::from_str(line) {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(err) => {
            error!("Malformed JSON-RPC message: {err}");
            return Incoming::Invalid(JsonRpcResponse::error(
                Value::Null,
                PARSE_ERROR,
                "Parse error",
            ));
        }
    };
    let Value::Object(mut message) = value else {
        return Incoming::Invalid(JsonRpcResponse::error(
            Value::Null,
            INVALID_REQUEST,
            "Invalid Request: expected a JSON object (batch requests are not supported)",
        ));
    };

    let id = message.remove("id");
    let error_id = match &id {
        Some(id) if id.is_string() || id.is_number() => id.clone(),
        _ => Value::Null,
    };
    let invalid = |reason: &str| {
        Incoming::Invalid(JsonRpcResponse::error(
            error_id.clone(),
            INVALID_REQUEST,
            format!("Invalid Request: {reason}"),
        ))
    };

    if message.get("jsonrpc").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("2.0") {
        return invalid("'jsonrpc' must be \"2.0\"");
    }
    let Some(method) = message.get("method").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
        return invalid("'method' must be a string");
    };
    let method = method.to_string();
    let params = message.remove("params").unwrap_or(Value::Null);

    match id {
        None => Incoming::Notification { method, params },
        Some(id) if id.is_string() || id.is_number() => Incoming::Request { id, method, params },
        Some(_) => invalid("'id' must be a string or a number"),
    }
}

fn send(out_tx: &mpsc::UnboundedSender<String>, response: &JsonRpcResponse) {
    match serde_json::to_string(response) {
        Ok(text) => {
            let _ = out_tx.send(text);
        }
        Err(err) => error!("Failed to serialize a response: {err}"),
    }
}

async fn write_line(stdout: &mut tokio::io::Stdout, line: &str) -> std::io::Result<()> {
    stdout.write_all(line.as_bytes()).await?;
    stdout.write_all(b"\n").await?;
    stdout.flush().await
}

```

`src/tools.rs`:

```rs
use crate::bridge::BridgeClient;
use crate::cli_runner::CliRunner;
use crate::config::FeedbackMode;
use crate::error::McpError;
use crate::godot::GameHub;
use crate::journal::EvidenceKind;
use crate::paths::{ensure_project_dir, find_project, is_valid_identifier, resolve_in_project};
use crate::protocol::{Tool, ToolResult};
use crate::validation::validate_arguments;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::io::ErrorKind;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::{Arc, OnceLock};
use tokio::fs;
use tokio::io::AsyncWriteExt;

mod feedback;
mod game;

const PROJECT_PATH_HINTS: &[&str] = &[
    "Pass project_path: the absolute path of a directory that contains project.godot",
    "Or start the MCP server from the project directory (or the folder right above it)",
    "Or open the project in the Godot Editor with the Godot MCP Bridge plugin enabled",
];

pub struct ToolManager {
    bridge: BridgeClient,
    cli: CliRunner,
    /// Where the server was started; the project is looked up from here when no
    /// `project_path` is given and no editor is connected.
    working_dir: Option<PathBuf>,
    feedback: feedback::FeedbackState,
    games: GameHub,
}

/// A project directory plus whether it came from the live editor.
struct ResolvedProject {
    dir: PathBuf,
    from_editor: bool,
}

impl ToolManager {
    pub fn new(bridge: BridgeClient, cli: CliRunner) -> Self {
        let godot = cli.binary().to_path_buf();
        Self {
            bridge,
            cli,
            working_dir: std::env::current_dir().ok(),
            feedback: feedback::FeedbackState::new(FeedbackMode::default(), None),
            games: GameHub::new(godot),
        }
    }

    /// Sets how much of the agent feedback channel is active and where the GitHub client is.
    pub fn with_feedback(mut self, mode: FeedbackMode, gh_path: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {
        self.feedback = feedback::FeedbackState::new(mode, gh_path);
        self
    }

    /// Chooses which feedback tools the catalog holds. Call it once at start-up, before the
    /// catalog is first read; a later call has no effect.
    pub fn configure_catalog(mode: FeedbackMode) {
        let _ = CATALOG_MODE.set(mode);
    }

    /// Looks up implicit projects from `dir` instead of the process working directory.
    pub fn with_working_dir(mut self, dir: PathBuf) -> Self {
        self.working_dir = Some(dir);
        self
    }

    /// Return the list of all registered tools with their input schemas
    pub fn list_tools() -> Vec<Tool> {
        tool_catalog().to_vec()
    }

    pub fn has_tool(name: &str) -> bool {
        tool_catalog().iter().any(|tool| tool.name == name)
    }

    /// Dispatch tool calls to live bridge or CLI runner
    pub async fn call_tool(self: Arc<Self>, name: &str, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let Some(tool) = tool_catalog().iter().find(|tool| tool.name == name) else {
            return ToolResult::error(
                format!("Tool '{name}' is not recognized"),
                &["Check tool name spelling against tools/list"],
            );
        };
        if let Err(problem) = validate_arguments(&tool.input_schema, &args) {
            let result = ToolResult::error(
                format!("Invalid arguments for {name}: {problem}"),
                &["Check the tool's inputSchema returned by tools/list and retry"],
            );
            self.observe(name, &result, EvidenceKind::InvalidArguments);
            return result;
        }

        let result = match name {
            "godot_get_version" => self.handle_get_version().await,
            "godot_get_project_info" => self.handle_bridge_call("get_project_info", args).await,
            "godot_launch_editor" => self.handle_launch_editor(args).await,
            "godot_run_project" => self.handle_run_project(args).await,
            "godot_stop_project" => self.handle_stop_project(args).await,
            "godot_get_run_status" => self.handle_run_status().await,
            "godot_get_scene_tree" => self.handle_bridge_call("get_scene_tree", args).await,
            "godot_get_open_scenes" => self.handle_bridge_call("get_open_scenes", args).await,
            "godot_add_node" => self.handle_bridge_call("add_node", args).await,
            "godot_remove_node" => self.handle_bridge_call("remove_node", args).await,
            "godot_get_node_properties" => {
                self.handle_bridge_call("get_node_properties", args).await
            }
            "godot_set_node_properties" => {
                self.handle_bridge_call("set_node_properties", args).await
            }
            "godot_capture_viewport" => self.handle_capture_viewport(args).await,
            "godot_open_scene" => self.handle_bridge_call("open_scene", args).await,
            "godot_save_scene" => self.handle_bridge_call("save_scene", args).await,
            "godot_create_script" => self.handle_create_script(args).await,
            "godot_validate_script" => self.handle_validate_script(args).await,
            "godot_resave_resources" => self.handle_bridge_call("resave_resources", args).await,
            name if game::handles(name) => game::call(&self, name, args).await,
            feedback::NOTE => self.handle_note_feedback(args).await,
            feedback::DRAFT => self.handle_draft_feedback(args).await,
            feedback::SUBMIT => self.handle_submit_feedback(args).await,
            _ => ToolResult::error(
                format!("Tool '{name}' has no handler"),
                &["This is a server bug; report it with the tool name"],
            ),
        };
        self.observe(name, &result, EvidenceKind::Error);
        result
    }

    async fn handle_get_version(&self) -> ToolResult {
        // Try live editor bridge first
        match self.bridge.send_command("get_version", json!({})).await {
            Ok(result) => {
                return ToolResult::text(format!(
                    "Godot Engine (Live Editor Connected):\n{}",
                    serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap_or_default()
                ));
            }
            // An editor that answers with an error (e.g. a token problem) must not be hidden.
            Err(error @ McpError::ToolExecution(_)) => return bridge_error_result(&error),
            Err(_) => {}
        }

        // Fallback to CLI
        match self.cli.get_version().await {
            Ok(ver) => ToolResult::text(format!(
                "Godot Engine (Headless CLI):\nVersion: {ver}\nNote: Editor bridge is offline. Launch Godot Editor and enable Godot MCP Bridge plugin for live manipulation."
            )),
            Err(e) => ToolResult::error(
                format!("Failed to retrieve Godot version: {e}"),
                &[
                    "Verify GODOT_PATH in .env",
                    "Ensure Godot executable is installed and reachable",
                ],
            ),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_bridge_call(&self, command_type: &str, params: Value) -> ToolResult {
        match self.bridge.send_command(command_type, params).await {
            Ok(res) => ToolResult::text(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&res).unwrap_or_default()),
            Err(error) => bridge_error_result(&error),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_capture_viewport(&self, params: Value) -> ToolResult {
        match self.bridge.send_command("capture_viewport", params).await {
            Ok(res) => {
                let data = res.get("data").and_then(Value::as_str);
                let mime = res.get("mime_type").and_then(Value::as_str);
                match (data, mime) {
                    (Some(data), Some(mime)) => {
                        let note = format!(
                            "Editor viewport capture: {}x{} px ({} view)",
                            res.get("width").and_then(Value::as_u64).unwrap_or(0),
                            res.get("height").and_then(Value::as_u64).unwrap_or(0),
                            res.get("view").and_then(Value::as_str).unwrap_or("2d"),
                        );
                        ToolResult::image_with_text(note, data, mime)
                    }
                    _ => ToolResult::error(
                        "Invalid image response received from the Godot viewport",
                        &["Update the Godot MCP Bridge addon to the same version as the server"],
                    ),
                }
            }
            Err(error) => bridge_error_result(&error),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_launch_editor(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let path = match self.host_project(&args) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(result) => return result,
        };

        match self.cli.launch_editor(&path).await {
            Ok(pid) => ToolResult::text(format!(
                "Godot Editor launched successfully for '{}' (PID: {pid}).",
                path.display()
            )),
            Err(e) => ToolResult::error(
                format!("Failed to launch Godot Editor: {e}"),
                &["Verify GODOT_PATH points to a working Godot executable"],
            ),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_run_project(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let scene = str_arg(&args, "scene");
        if let Some(scene) = scene {
            if !scene.starts_with("res://") || scene.split(['/', '\\']).any(|part| part == "..") {
                return ToolResult::error(
                    format!(
                        "Invalid scene '{scene}': scene must be a res:// path inside the project"
                    ),
                    &["Use a path such as res://scenes/Main.tscn"],
                );
            }
        }
        let mode = str_arg(&args, "mode").unwrap_or("auto");
        if mode == "process" {
            return self.spawn_managed(&args, scene).await;
        }

        // Prefer the live editor: it can be stopped/queried later via
        // godot_stop_project / godot_get_run_status, unlike a spawned process.
        let bridge_params = json!({ "scene_path": scene.unwrap_or("") });
        match self.bridge.send_command("run_project", bridge_params).await {
            Ok(result) => {
                return ToolResult::text(format!(
                    "Project running via live editor:\n{}",
                    serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap_or_default()
                ));
            }
            // Only a missing editor justifies spawning a separate process.
            Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(_)) => {}
            Err(error) => return bridge_error_result(&error),
        }

        self.spawn_managed(&args, scene).await
    }

    async fn spawn_managed(&self, args: &Value, scene: Option<&str>) -> ToolResult {
        let path = match self.host_project(args) {
            Ok(path) => path,
            Err(result) => return result,
        };
        let breakpoints = string_list(args.get("breakpoints"));
        let headless = args
            .get("headless")
            .and_then(Value::as_bool)
            .unwrap_or(false);
        let break_on_errors = args
            .get("break_on_errors")
            .and_then(Value::as_bool)
            .unwrap_or(false);
        match self
            .games
            .start(&path, scene, &breakpoints, headless, break_on_errors)
            .await
        {
            Ok(info) => ToolResult::text(format!(
                "No editor bridge connected. Spawned a managed Godot process (PID: {}, run_id: {}). Stop it with godot_stop_project. Project: '{}'.",
                info.pid,
                info.id,
                path.display()
            )),
            Err(error) => ToolResult::error(
                format!("Failed to run project: {error}"),
                &["Verify GODOT_PATH points to a working Godot executable"],
            ),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_stop_project(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        if let Some(run_id) = str_arg(&args, "run_id") {
            return match self.games.stop(Some(run_id)).await {
                Ok(summary) => {
                    ToolResult::text(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&summary).unwrap_or_default())
                }
                Err(error) => ToolResult::error(
                    error.to_string(),
                    &["Check godot_get_run_status for live run ids"],
                ),
            };
        }
        match self.bridge.send_command("stop_project", json!({})).await {
            Ok(result) => ToolResult::text(format!(
                "Project stopped via live editor:\n{}",
                serde_json::to_string_pretty(&result).unwrap_or_default()
            )),
            Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(detail)) => match self.games.stop(None).await {
                Ok(summary) => ToolResult::text(format!(
                    "Stopped managed Godot process:\n{}",
                    serde_json::to_string_pretty(&summary).unwrap_or_default()
                )),
                Err(_) => bridge_error_result(&McpError::BridgeUnavailable(detail)),
            },
            Err(error) => bridge_error_result(&error),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_run_status(&self) -> ToolResult {
        let processes = self.games.status();
        match self.bridge.send_command("get_run_status", json!({})).await {
            Ok(result) => {
                let mut combined = result;
                if let Value::Object(map) = &mut combined {
                    map.insert("process_runs".into(), Value::Array(processes));
                }
                ToolResult::text(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&combined).unwrap_or_default())
            }
            Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(detail)) if !processes.is_empty() => ToolResult::text(
                serde_json::to_string_pretty(&json!({"editor": detail, "process_runs": processes}))
                    .unwrap_or_default(),
            ),
            Err(error) => bridge_error_result(&error),
        }
    }

    async fn handle_create_script(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let Some(file_path) = str_arg(&args, "file_path") else {
            return missing_argument("file_path");
        };
        if !file_path.ends_with(".gd") {
            return ToolResult::error(
                format!("Script files must have the .gd extension: {file_path}"),
                &["Use a path such as res://scripts/player.gd"],
            );
        }

        let extends_class = str_arg(&args, "extends_class").unwrap_or("Node");
        if !is_valid_identifier(extends_class) {
            return invalid_identifier("extends_class", extends_class);
        }
        let class_name = str_arg(&args, "class_name");
        if let Some(name) = class_name {
            if !is_valid_identifier(name) {
                return invalid_identifier("class_name", name);
            }
        }
        let overwrite = args
            .get("overwrite")
            .and_then(Value::as_bool)
            .unwrap_or(false);

        let project = match self.resolve_project(&args).await {
            Ok(project) => project,
            Err(result) => return result,
        };
        let target = match resolve_in_project(&project.dir, file_path) {
            Ok(target) => target,
            Err(problem) => {
                return ToolResult::error(
                    problem,
                    &["Use a res:// path or a path relative to the project directory"],
                );
            }
        };

        let mut content = format!("extends {extends_class}\n");
        if let Some(name) = class_name {
            content.push_str(&format!("class_name {name}\n"));
        }
        content.push_str("\nfunc _ready() -> void:\n\tpass\n");

        if let Some(parent) = target.parent() {
            if let Err(err) = fs::create_dir_all(parent).await {
                return ToolResult::error(
                    format!("Failed to create directory {}: {err}", parent.display()),
                    &["Check that the project directory is writable"],
                );
            }
        }
        let mut options = fs::OpenOptions::new();
        options.write(true);
        if overwrite {
            options.create(true).truncate(true);
        } else {
            options.create_new(true);
        }
        let write_result = match options.open(&target).await {
            Ok(mut file) => match file.write_all(content.as_bytes()).await {
                // tokio's File writes on a worker thread and does not flush when dropped, so
                // without this the content can be lost after the tool already reported success.
                Ok(()) => file.flush().await,
                Err(err) => Err(err),
            },
            Err(err) => Err(err),
        };
        if let Err(err) = write_result {
            return if err.kind() == ErrorKind::AlreadyExists {
                ToolResult::error(
                    format!("File already exists: {}", target.display()),
                    &["Pass overwrite: true to replace it, or choose another file name"],
                )
            } else {
                ToolResult::error(
                    format!("Failed to write script file {}: {err}", target.display()),
                    &["Check that the project directory is writable"],
                )
            };
        }

        let res_path = target
            .strip_prefix(&project.dir)
            .ok()
            .map(|relative| format!("res://{}", relative.to_string_lossy().replace('\\', "/")));
        if project.from_editor {
            if let Some(res_path) = &res_path {
                // Let the editor pick the file up (and give it a .uid) right away.
                let _ = self
                    .bridge
                    .send_command("resave_resources", json!({ "paths": [res_path] }))
                    .await;
            }
        }

        ToolResult::text(format!(
            "Created GDScript file {} extending '{extends_class}'.",
            res_path.unwrap_or_else(|| target.display().to_string()),
        ))
    }

    async fn handle_validate_script(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let Some(script_path) = str_arg(&args, "script_path") else {
            return missing_argument("script_path");
        };
        if !script_path.ends_with(".gd") {
            return ToolResult::error(
                format!("Only .gd scripts can be validated: {script_path}"),
                &["Use a path such as res://scripts/player.gd"],
            );
        }

        let project_dir =
            if str_arg(&args, "project_path").is_some() || script_path.starts_with("res://") {
                match self.resolve_project(&args).await {
                    Ok(project) => Some(project.dir),
                    Err(result) => return result,
                }
            } else {
                None
            };

        let file = match &project_dir {
            Some(dir) => match resolve_in_project(dir, script_path) {
                Ok(file) => file,
                Err(problem) => {
                    return ToolResult::error(
                        problem,
                        &["Use a res:// path or a path inside the project directory"],
                    );
                }
            },
            None => PathBuf::from(script_path),
        };
        if !file.is_file() {
            return ToolResult::error(
                format!("Script file not found: {}", file.display()),
                &["Check the path; res:// paths are resolved against the project directory"],
            );
        }

        match self
            .cli
            .validate_script(project_dir.as_deref(), Path::new(script_path))
            .await
        {
            Ok(msg) => ToolResult::text(msg),
            Err(e) => ToolResult::error(
                format!("Script validation failed: {e}"),
                &[
                    "Check GDScript syntax line numbers",
                    "Ensure base class and node types exist in the engine",
                ],
            ),
        }
    }

    /// Finds the project directory without asking the editor: an explicit `project_path`,
    /// else the project found from the server's working directory.
    fn host_project(&self, args: &Value) -> Result<PathBuf, ToolResult> {
        let dir = self.locate_host_project(args)?;
        self.feedback.remember_project(&dir);
        Ok(dir)
    }

    fn locate_host_project(&self, args: &Value) -> Result<PathBuf, ToolResult> {
        if let Some(project_path) = str_arg(args, "project_path") {
            let dir = PathBuf::from(project_path);
            return match ensure_project_dir(&dir) {
                Ok(()) => Ok(dir),
                Err(problem) => Err(ToolResult::error(
                    problem,
                    &["Pass the directory that contains project.godot"],
                )),
            };
        }
        self.working_dir_project()
    }

    fn working_dir_project(&self) -> Result<PathBuf, ToolResult> {
        let Some(cwd) = &self.working_dir else {
            return Err(ToolResult::error(
                "No project_path was given and the server's working directory is unknown",
                PROJECT_PATH_HINTS,
            ));
        };
        find_project(cwd).map_err(|problem| ToolResult::error(problem, PROJECT_PATH_HINTS))
    }

    /// Finds the project directory: an explicit `project_path`, else the project open in the
    /// live editor, else the project found from the server's working directory.
    async fn resolve_project(&self, args: &Value) -> Result<ResolvedProject, ToolResult> {
        let project = self.locate_project(args).await?;
        self.feedback.remember_project(&project.dir);
        Ok(project)
    }

    async fn locate_project(&self, args: &Value) -> Result<ResolvedProject, ToolResult> {
        if let Some(project_path) = str_arg(args, "project_path") {
            let dir = PathBuf::from(project_path);
            return match ensure_project_dir(&dir) {
                Ok(()) => Ok(ResolvedProject {
                    dir,
                    from_editor: false,
                }),
                Err(problem) => Err(ToolResult::error(problem, PROJECT_PATH_HINTS)),
            };
        }

        match self
            .bridge
            .send_command("get_project_info", json!({}))
            .await
        {
            Ok(info) => match info.get("project_dir").and_then(Value::as_str) {
                Some(dir) => {
                    let dir = PathBuf::from(dir);
                    ensure_project_dir(&dir)
                        .map_err(|problem| ToolResult::error(problem, PROJECT_PATH_HINTS))?;
                    Ok(ResolvedProject {
                        dir,
                        from_editor: true,
                    })
                }
                None => Err(ToolResult::error(
                    "The editor did not report its project directory",
                    &["Update the Godot MCP Bridge addon to the same version as the server"],
                )),
            },
            // Only a missing editor justifies looking in the working directory: an editor that
            // answers with an error may have a different project open.
            Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(_)) => {
                self.working_dir_project().map(|dir| ResolvedProject {
                    dir,
                    from_editor: false,
                })
            }
            Err(error) => Err(bridge_error_result(&error)),
        }
    }
}

fn str_arg<'a>(args: &'a Value, key: &str) -> Option<&'a str> {
    args.get(key).and_then(Value::as_str)
}

fn string_list(value: Option<&Value>) -> Vec<String> {
    value
        .and_then(Value::as_array)
        .map(|items| {
            items
                .iter()
                .filter_map(Value::as_str)
                .map(str::to_string)
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

fn missing_argument(name: &str) -> ToolResult {
    ToolResult::error(
        format!("Missing required parameter: {name}"),
        &["Check the tool's inputSchema returned by tools/list"],
    )
}

fn invalid_identifier(parameter: &str, value: &str) -> ToolResult {
    ToolResult::error(
        format!(
            "Invalid {parameter} {value:?}: use letters, digits and underscores only, not starting with a digit"
        ),
        &["Example: Node2D, CharacterBody3D, Player"],
    )
}

/// Turns a bridge failure into a tool error whose recovery hints match what actually failed.
fn bridge_error_result(error: &McpError) -> ToolResult {
    match error {
        McpError::BridgeUnavailable(detail) => ToolResult::error(
            format!("The Godot Editor bridge is not reachable ({detail})"),
            &[
                "Launch Godot Editor with your project (godot_launch_editor can do it)",
                "Ensure 'Godot MCP Bridge' plugin is enabled in Project Settings -> Plugins",
                "Verify GODOT_DEBUG_PORT matches the bridge port (default: 9333)",
            ],
        ),
        McpError::Bridge(detail) => ToolResult::error(
            detail.clone(),
            &[
                "The editor may be busy (importing, modal dialog) or restarting; retry in a moment",
                "Look for [Godot MCP] messages in the editor's Output panel",
            ],
        ),
        McpError::ToolExecution(message) => {
            ToolResult::error(message.clone(), editor_error_hints(message))
        }
        other => ToolResult::error(other.to_string(), &[]),
    }
}

fn editor_error_hints(message: &str) -> &'static [&'static str] {
    let lower = message.to_lowercase();
    if lower.contains("unauthorized") {
        &[
            "Restart the MCP client so the server re-reads the bridge token",
            "Run the MCP server and the Godot editor as the same OS user, or set the same GODOT_MCP_TOKEN for both",
        ]
    } else if lower.contains("no active scene") {
        &["Open a scene first with godot_open_scene"]
    } else if lower.contains("not found")
        || lower.contains("does not exist")
        || lower.contains("not currently open")
    {
        &["Verify the path with godot_get_scene_tree or godot_get_open_scenes"]
    } else {
        &["Correct the arguments according to the message above and retry"]
    }
}

/// Which feedback tools the catalog holds; set once at start-up by `configure_catalog`.
static CATALOG_MODE: OnceLock<FeedbackMode> = OnceLock::new();

fn tool_catalog() -> &'static [Tool] {
    static CATALOG: OnceLock<Vec<Tool>> = OnceLock::new();
    CATALOG.get_or_init(|| build_catalog(CATALOG_MODE.get().copied().unwrap_or_default()))
}

fn tool(name: &str, description: &str, properties: Value, required: &[&str]) -> Tool {
    let mut input_schema = json!({ "type": "object", "properties": properties });
    if !required.is_empty() {
        input_schema["required"] = json!(required);
    }
    Tool {
        name: name.into(),
        description: description.into(),
        input_schema,
    }
}
```
