```

`src/tools/feedback.rs`:

```rs
//! The tools of the agent feedback channel: record a note, draft a report, submit it. What the
//! channel guarantees is specified in terms of the journal (`crate::journal`), the text helpers
//! (`crate::feedback`) and the GitHub client (`crate::issues`); this module connects them to the
//! tool manager.

use super::{ToolManager, tool};
use crate::config::FeedbackMode;
use crate::error::McpError;
use crate::feedback::ScrubContext;
use crate::issues::{IssueClient, RepoAddress, locate_gh};
use crate::journal::{
    BuiltDraft, Draft, DraftRequest, Environment, EvidenceKind, Impact, Journal, Kind, NewNote,
    Outcome, Refusal, SubmitRefusal, fenced_as,
};
use crate::paths::resolve_in_project;
use crate::protocol::{Tool, ToolContent, ToolResult};
use crate::util::lock;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::io::ErrorKind;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Mutex;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::time::timeout;

/// How long the editor and the engine have, together, to say which versions they are.
const ENVIRONMENT_BUDGET: Duration = Duration::from_secs(5);

pub(super) const NOTE: &str = "godot_note_feedback";
pub(super) const DRAFT: &str = "godot_draft_feedback";
pub(super) const SUBMIT: &str = "godot_submit_feedback";

pub(super) fn is_feedback_tool(name: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(name, NOTE | DRAFT | SUBMIT)
}

/// Everything the channel keeps beside the tool manager's own fields.
pub(super) struct FeedbackState {
    pub(super) mode: FeedbackMode,
    pub(super) journal: Journal,
    pub(super) gh_path: Option<PathBuf>,
    pub(super) repo: Option<RepoAddress>,
    /// The project directory this session last resolved, so it can be hidden in reports.
    project_dir: Mutex<Option<PathBuf>>,
}

impl FeedbackState {
    pub(super) fn new(mode: FeedbackMode, gh_path: Option<PathBuf>) -> Self {
        Self {
            mode,
            journal: Journal::new(),
            gh_path,
            repo: RepoAddress::manifest(),
            project_dir: Mutex::new(None),
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn remember_project(&self, dir: &Path) {
        *lock(&self.project_dir) = Some(dir.to_path_buf());
    }

    pub(super) fn known_project(&self) -> Option<PathBuf> {
        lock(&self.project_dir).clone()
    }
}

/// The catalog entries the mode allows.
pub(super) fn catalog(mode: FeedbackMode) -> Vec<Tool> {
    let mut tools = Vec::new();
    if mode.enabled() {
        tools.push(tool(
            NOTE,
            "Records one observation for the godot-mcp maintainers: something missing, broken or misleading in godot-mcp itself (a tool, its message or schema, the editor addon, the README). Not for your own mistakes or problems in the user's project. Write in English: what you tried, what came back, what you expected; one problem per note. The note stays in memory and nothing is sent. Later, godot_draft_feedback turns notes into a report for the human.",
            json!({
                "kind": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["bug", "missing_feature", "improvement", "docs"],
                    "description": "bug: a tool misbehaves or fails for a reason it does not explain. missing_feature: you needed a capability no tool offers. improvement: it works but could be clearer, faster or safer. docs: a description, schema or README is wrong or unclear."
                },
                "summary": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "One sentence, at most 120 characters."
                },
                "details": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "What you tried, what came back, what you expected. At most 2000 characters. No secrets and no file contents."
                },
                "tool": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Name of the godot-mcp tool involved, such as godot_add_node. Lets the report quote that tool's recent failures."
                },
                "impact": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "enum": ["blocked", "workaround", "minor"],
                    "description": "blocked: you could not finish the task. workaround: you found another way. minor: a nuisance."
                },
                "suggestion": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "What should change, at most 500 characters."
                }
            }),
            &["kind", "summary", "details"],
        ));
        let submission = if mode.submits() {
            "have it submitted with godot_submit_feedback, "
        } else {
            ""
        };
        tools.push(tool(
            DRAFT,
            &format!(
                "Builds one report for the godot-mcp maintainers from your notes (by default every note not yet in a report; pass note_ids to choose, one report per problem) plus what the server observed: versions, platform, failures of the tools your notes name and requests for tools that do not exist. It contacts no remote service. Call it when the user's request is completed or abandoned, or when the user asks for feedback, and only when notes exist. Then show the user the COMPLETE report, in the user's language, and offer: copy it, open the link, {submission}or discard it."
            ),
            json!({
                "note_ids": {
                    "type": "array",
                    "items": { "type": "string" },
                    "description": "Ids of the notes to include, such as [\"n1\", \"n3\"]. Default: every note not yet in a report."
                },
                "title": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "Optional title; by default it is built from the first note."
                }
            }),
            &[],
        ));
    }
    if mode.submits() {
        tools.push(tool(
            SUBMIT,
            "Publishes a draft from godot_draft_feedback as a PUBLIC GitHub issue of the godot-mcp project, through the user's own signed-in GitHub CLI. It takes only the draft id, so what is published is exactly the report the user saw. Call it ONLY after the user has seen the complete report and said an explicit yes in this conversation; then pass confirmed: true. It reports whether the issue was created, a duplicate already exists, or nothing was created.",
            json!({
                "draft_id": {
                    "type": "string",
                    "description": "The id returned by godot_draft_feedback, such as d1."
                },
                "confirmed": {
                    "type": "boolean",
                    "description": "true only when the user explicitly approved publishing this exact report."
                },
                "allow_duplicate": {
                    "type": "boolean",
                    "description": "Create the issue although one with the same fingerprint exists. Only when the user has looked at that issue and still wants a new one."
                }
            }),
            &["draft_id", "confirmed"],
        ));
    }
    tools
}

/// The guidance sent at session start, or `None` when the channel is off.
pub fn instructions(mode: FeedbackMode) -> Option<String> {
    if !mode.enabled() {
        return None;
    }
    let ending = if mode.submits() {
        "offer: copy it, open the link, submit it with godot_submit_feedback, or discard it. \
         Never call godot_submit_feedback without the user's explicit yes in this conversation."
    } else {
        "offer: copy it, open the link, or discard it. The report can only be handed over as text."
    };
    Some(format!(
        "godot-mcp feedback channel. While you work, if godot-mcp itself blocks or misleads you \
         (a tool fails for a reason its message does not explain, a needed capability has no tool, \
         a description or schema is wrong), record it with godot_note_feedback: English, what you \
         tried, what came back, what you expected, one problem per note. Do not note your own \
         mistakes or problems in the user's project. When the user's request is completed or \
         abandoned, or when asked for feedback, and notes exist, call godot_draft_feedback, show \
         the user the complete report, and {ending} Notes stay in memory; nothing leaves this \
         machine otherwise."
    ))
}

/// Returns a draft that was being submitted to `Pending` when the call that claimed it is
/// dropped before it stored an outcome. After an outcome is stored this does nothing.
struct SubmitGuard<'a> {
    journal: &'a Journal,
    id: String,
}

impl Drop for SubmitGuard<'_> {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.journal.abort_submit(&self.id);
    }
}

/// What the agent is told about the outcome of a submission.
fn outcome_result(id: &str, outcome: &Outcome, notes: &[String], repeated: bool) -> ToolResult {
    let mut extra = String::new();
    for note in notes {
        extra.push_str(&format!(
            "
{note}"
        ));
    }
    let earlier = if repeated {
        " (submitted earlier in this session; nothing new was sent)"
    } else {
        ""
    };
    match outcome {
        Outcome::Created(address) => ToolResult::text(format!(
            "Created{earlier}: {address}

Draft {id} is now a PUBLIC issue under the user's GitHub account. Tell the user the address.{extra}"
        )),
        Outcome::Duplicate(address) => ToolResult::text(format!(
            "Not created: an issue with the same fingerprint already exists: {address}

Tell the user. If their problem differs from that issue, they can ask for a new one; then call again with allow_duplicate: true.{extra}"
        )),
        Outcome::NotCreated { reason, steps } => {
            let steps: Vec<&str> = steps.iter().map(String::as_str).collect();
            ToolResult::error(format!("Not created: {reason}"), &steps)
        }
        Outcome::Unknown(detail) => ToolResult::error(
            format!("Unknown whether the issue was created{earlier}: {detail}"),
            &[
                "Check the project's issues page for the new issue before submitting again",
                "Or file it by hand: give the user the report text, or the prefilled link from the draft",
            ],
        ),
    }
}

fn refusal_result(refusal: Refusal) -> ToolResult {
    ToolResult::error(refusal.message, &[refusal.hint.as_str()])
}

fn first_error_line(result: &ToolResult) -> &str {
    result
        .content
        .iter()
        .find_map(|content| match content {
            ToolContent::Text { text } => text.lines().next(),
            ToolContent::Image { .. } => None,
        })
        .map(|line| line.strip_prefix("Error: ").unwrap_or(line))
        .unwrap_or("")
}

impl ToolManager {
    /// The guidance for the `initialize` answer.
    pub fn guidance(&self) -> Option<String> {
        instructions(self.feedback.mode)
    }

    /// Remembers which client the session talks to.
    pub fn note_client(&self, name: Option<&str>, version: Option<&str>) {
        if self.feedback.mode.enabled() {
            self.feedback
                .journal
                .set_client(name, version, &self.scrub_context());
        }
    }

    /// A call for a tool name the server does not have is a request for a missing capability.
    pub fn record_unknown_tool(&self, name: &str) {
        if self.feedback.mode.enabled() {
            self.feedback.journal.record_evidence(
                EvidenceKind::UnknownTool,
                name,
                "",
                &self.scrub_context(),
            );
        }
    }

    pub(super) fn scrub_context(&self) -> ScrubContext {
        ScrubContext::from_environment(self.bridge.secret(), self.feedback.known_project())
    }

    /// Counts a finished call and keeps the first line of a failure. The result is not touched.
    pub(super) fn observe(&self, name: &str, result: &ToolResult, kind: EvidenceKind) {
        if !self.feedback.mode.enabled() || is_feedback_tool(name) {
            return;
        }
        self.feedback.journal.record_call(name, result.is_error);
        if result.is_error {
            self.feedback.journal.record_evidence(
                kind,
                name,
                first_error_line(result),
                &self.scrub_context(),
            );
        }
    }

    pub(super) async fn handle_note_feedback(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let text = |key: &str| args.get(key).and_then(Value::as_str).map(str::to_string);
        let Some(kind) = text("kind").and_then(|kind| Kind::parse(&kind)) else {
            return ToolResult::error(
                "kind must be one of bug, missing_feature, improvement, docs",
                &["Pick the kind that fits the observation and call again"],
            );
        };
        let note = NewNote {
            kind,
            summary: text("summary").unwrap_or_default(),
            details: text("details").unwrap_or_default(),
            tool: text("tool"),
            impact: text("impact").and_then(|impact| Impact::parse(&impact)),
            suggestion: text("suggestion"),
        };
        match self.feedback.journal.add_note(note, &self.scrub_context()) {
            Ok((id, pending)) => {
                let mut answer = format!(
                    "Noted as {id}. Notes not yet in a report ({}):\n",
                    pending.len()
                );
                for note in &pending {
                    answer.push_str(&format!(
                        "- {} ({}): {}\n",
                        note.id,
                        note.kind.as_str(),
                        note.summary
                    ));
                }
                answer.push_str(
                    "\nKeep working. When the user's request is completed or abandoned, call godot_draft_feedback.",
                );
                ToolResult::text(answer)
            }
            Err(refusal) => refusal_result(refusal),
        }
    }

    pub(super) async fn handle_draft_feedback(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let note_ids = args.get("note_ids").and_then(Value::as_array).map(|items| {
            items
                .iter()
                .filter_map(Value::as_str)
                .map(str::to_string)
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        });
        let title = args
            .get("title")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .map(str::to_string);

        // Without any note there is nothing to look up, so refuse before asking the editor.
        let nothing_to_draft =
            note_ids.is_none() && self.feedback.journal.pending_notes().is_empty();
        let environment = if nothing_to_draft {
            self.unknown_environment()
        } else {
            self.collect_environment().await
        };
        let link_base = self
            .feedback
            .repo
            .as_ref()
            .map(RepoAddress::new_issue_url)
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let built = self.feedback.journal.build_draft(
            DraftRequest { note_ids, title },
            &environment,
            &link_base,
            &self.scrub_context(),
        );
        match built {
            Ok(built) => ToolResult::text(self.draft_answer(&built)),
            Err(refusal) => refusal_result(refusal),
        }
    }

    fn unknown_environment(&self) -> Environment {
        let unknown = || "unknown".to_string();
        Environment {
            server_version: env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION").to_string(),
            addon_version: unknown(),
            engine_version: unknown(),
            platform: format!("{} {}", std::env::consts::OS, std::env::consts::ARCH),
            bridge_state: unknown(),
            feedback_mode: self.feedback.mode.as_str().to_string(),
        }
    }

    /// The facts a report quotes about this setup. The editor and the engine get a shared time
    /// budget; whatever has not answered by then is reported as unknown.
    async fn collect_environment(&self) -> Environment {
        let no_arguments = json!({});
        let lookups = async {
            let (engine_and_bridge, project) = tokio::join!(
                self.engine_version_and_bridge_state(),
                self.resolve_project(&no_arguments)
            );
            (engine_and_bridge, project.ok().map(|project| project.dir))
        };
        let ((engine_version, bridge_state), project_dir) =
            match timeout(ENVIRONMENT_BUDGET, lookups).await {
                Ok(found) => found,
                Err(_) => (
                    (
                        "unknown".to_string(),
                        format!(
                            "the editor did not answer within {} s",
                            ENVIRONMENT_BUDGET.as_secs()
                        ),
                    ),
                    self.feedback.known_project(),
                ),
            };
        let addon_version = match project_dir {
            Some(dir) => read_addon_version(&dir).await,
            None => "unknown".to_string(),
        };
        Environment {
            engine_version,
            addon_version,
            bridge_state,
            ..self.unknown_environment()
        }
    }

    async fn engine_version_and_bridge_state(&self) -> (String, String) {
        match self.bridge.send_command("get_version", json!({})).await {
            Ok(result) => (
                result
                    .get("version_string")
                    .and_then(Value::as_str)
                    .unwrap_or("unknown")
                    .to_string(),
                "connected".to_string(),
            ),
            Err(McpError::BridgeUnavailable(_)) => (
                self.cli
                    .get_version()
                    .await
                    .ok()
                    .filter(|version| !version.is_empty())
                    .unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string()),
                "not reachable (editor closed or plugin disabled)".to_string(),
            ),
            Err(McpError::ToolExecution(message)) => (
                "unknown".to_string(),
                format!("the editor answered with an error: {message}"),
            ),
            Err(other) => (
                "unknown".to_string(),
                format!("connection problem: {other}"),
            ),
        }
    }

    fn gh_available(&self) -> bool {
        locate_gh(self.feedback.gh_path.as_deref()).is_some()
    }

    /// What the agent gets back for a new draft: the whole report, the ways to deliver it and
    /// what to do next.
    fn draft_answer(&self, built: &BuiltDraft) -> String {
        let draft = &built.draft;
        let submit = self.feedback.mode.submits() && self.gh_available();
        let mut out = format!(
            "Draft {} is ready. Nothing has been sent anywhere.\n\nTitle: {}\nFingerprint: {}\n\n\
             Report (the complete text, exactly as it would be published):\n\n",
            draft.id, draft.title, draft.fingerprint
        );
        out.push_str(&fenced_as(&draft.body, "markdown"));
        out.push_str("\n\nWays to deliver it:\n");
        out.push_str(
            "- Copy: always available. The user copies the report text above into a new issue.\n",
        );
        match &draft.link {
            Ok(link) => out.push_str(&format!(
                "- Link: {link}\n  It opens a prefilled new-issue page; the user reviews it and presses the button.\n"
            )),
            Err(reason) => out.push_str(&format!("- Link: not available ({reason})\n")),
        }
        if self.feedback.mode.submits() {
            if submit {
                out.push_str(&format!(
                    "- Submit: available through godot_submit_feedback with draft_id \"{}\". It publishes the report publicly under the user's GitHub account.\n",
                    draft.id
                ));
            } else {
                out.push_str(
                    "- Submit: not available: the GitHub CLI was not found. Install it and sign in with `gh auth login`, or set GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH.\n",
                );
            }
        }
        if !built.left_out.is_empty() {
            out.push_str("\nNotes not in any report yet:\n");
            for note in &built.left_out {
                out.push_str(&format!(
                    "- {} ({}): {}\n",
                    note.id,
                    note.kind.as_str(),
                    note.summary
                ));
            }
        }
        out.push_str(
            "\nNext steps for you:\n1. Show the user the COMPLETE report above in your reply. Keep the report text as it is; explain around it in the user's language.\n",
        );
        if submit {
            out.push_str(
                "2. Offer these choices: copy the text, open the link, have you submit it, or discard it.\n3. Call godot_submit_feedback only after the user has explicitly said yes in this conversation.\n",
            );
        } else {
            out.push_str("2. Offer these choices: copy the text, open the link, or discard it.\n");
        }
        out
    }

    pub(super) async fn handle_submit_feedback(&self, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
        let id = args
            .get("draft_id")
            .and_then(Value::as_str)
            .unwrap_or_default();
        let flag = |key: &str| args.get(key).and_then(Value::as_bool).unwrap_or(false);
        if !flag("confirmed") {
            return ToolResult::error(
                "Nothing was sent: confirmed must be true, and only after the user approved publishing this report",
                &[
                    "Show the user the complete report from godot_draft_feedback",
                    "Ask for an explicit yes in this conversation, then call again with confirmed: true",
                    "Or hand the report over as text or as the prefilled link",
                ],
            );
        }

        let draft = match self.feedback.journal.begin_submit(id) {
            Ok(draft) => draft,
            Err(SubmitRefusal::UnknownDraft { known }) => {
                let hint = if known.is_empty() {
                    "No draft exists yet: record notes and call godot_draft_feedback first"
                        .to_string()
                } else {
                    format!("Known draft ids: {}", known.join(", "))
                };
                return ToolResult::error(format!("Unknown draft id {id:?}"), &[hint.as_str()]);
            }
            Err(SubmitRefusal::InProgress) => {
                return ToolResult::error(
                    format!("Draft {id} is being submitted by another call"),
                    &["Wait for that call's outcome instead of submitting again"],
                );
            }
            Err(SubmitRefusal::AlreadyDone(outcome)) => {
                return outcome_result(id, &outcome, &[], true);
            }
        };
        // From here the draft is `Submitting`; the guard returns it to `Pending` if this call is
        // cancelled before an outcome is stored (a repeat then finds an issue that was created).
        let _guard = SubmitGuard {
            journal: &self.feedback.journal,
            id: draft.id.clone(),
        };
        let (outcome, notes) = self.publish(&draft, flag("allow_duplicate")).await;
        self.feedback
            .journal
            .finish_submit(&draft.id, outcome.clone());
        outcome_result(&draft.id, &outcome, &notes, false)
    }

    /// Looks for an existing issue with the draft's fingerprint, then creates the issue.
    async fn publish(&self, draft: &Draft, allow_duplicate: bool) -> (Outcome, Vec<String>) {
        let not_created = |reason: &str, steps: &[&str]| Outcome::NotCreated {
            reason: reason.to_string(),
            steps: steps.iter().map(|step| step.to_string()).collect(),
        };
        let Some(repo) = self.feedback.repo.clone() else {
            return (
                not_created(
                    "the project's repository address is unknown",
                    &["Give the user the report text to file by hand"],
                ),
                Vec::new(),
            );
        };
        let Some(gh) = locate_gh(self.feedback.gh_path.as_deref()) else {
            return (
                not_created(
                    "the GitHub CLI was not found",
                    &[
                        "Install the GitHub CLI (https://cli.github.com) and sign in with `gh auth login`",
                        "Or set GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH to the full path of the gh executable",
                        "Or file it by hand: give the user the report text, or the prefilled link from the draft",
                    ],
                ),
                Vec::new(),
            );
        };
        let client = IssueClient::new(gh, repo);
        let mut notes = Vec::new();
        if allow_duplicate {
            notes.push("The duplicate check was skipped at the user's request.".to_string());
        } else {
            match client.find_duplicate(&draft.fingerprint).await {
                Ok(Some(address)) => return (Outcome::Duplicate(address), notes),
                Ok(None) => {}
                Err(problem) => notes.push(format!(
                    "The duplicate check failed ({problem}), so the issue was created without it."
                )),
            }
        }
        (client.create(&draft.title, &draft.body).await, notes)
    }
}

/// The version the addon's manifest in the project declares.
async fn read_addon_version(project: &Path) -> String {
    let Ok(manifest) = resolve_in_project(project, "res://addons/godot_mcp/plugin.cfg") else {
        return "unknown".to_string();
    };
    match tokio::fs::read_to_string(&manifest).await {
        Ok(text) => parse_manifest_version(&text).unwrap_or_else(|| "unknown".to_string()),
        Err(err) if err.kind() == ErrorKind::NotFound => "not installed in the project".to_string(),
        Err(_) => "unknown".to_string(),
    }
}

fn parse_manifest_version(text: &str) -> Option<String> {
    text.lines()
        .map(str::trim)
        .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix("version="))
        .map(|value| value.trim().trim_matches('"').to_string())
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
}

```

`src/tools/game.rs`:

```rs
//! Running-game inspection, input and screenshots.
//!
//! Process runs speak Godot's remote debugger. Editor Play is reached through the
//! plugin's debugger relay. Method calls require an explicit `permit` list.

use super::{ToolManager, str_arg, string_list};
use crate::error::McpError;
use crate::protocol::{Tool, ToolResult};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::time::Duration;

const DENY_METHODS: &[&str] = &[
    "quit",
    "set_script",
    "call",
    "callv",
    "call_deferred",
    "rpc",
    "rpc_id",
    "free",
];

pub fn handles(name: &str) -> bool {
    matches!(
        name,
        "godot_run_output"
            | "godot_game_tree"
            | "godot_game_get"
            | "godot_game_set"
            | "godot_game_call"
            | "godot_game_signals"
            | "godot_game_observe"
            | "godot_game_snapshot"
            | "godot_game_input"
            | "godot_game_input_release"
            | "godot_game_wait"
            | "godot_game_screenshot"
    )
}

pub fn catalog() -> Vec<Tool> {
    vec![
        tool(
            "godot_run_output",
            "Reads logs, warnings and errors (with stack traces) from a managed process run or the editor's running game.",
            json!({"run_id": s("Optional run id. Default: newest process run, else the editor game."), "since": i("Return entries with seq >= since."), "level": s("all (default), warning, or error.")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_tree",
            "Lists live nodes in the running game (path, class, position, visible text).",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "root": s("Default: current scene. \"/root\" includes autoloads."), "depth": i(""), "max_nodes": i("Default 150."), "name": s(""), "class": s(""), "group": s(""), "text": s("")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_get",
            "Reads a live node's properties, groups and, optionally, signals and methods.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "path": s("Node path relative to the current scene, or /root/..."), "properties": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "signals": b(), "methods": b()}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_set",
            "Sets properties on a live node. Refuses `script` and private names. Nothing is saved to disk.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "path": s(""), "properties": {"type": "object"}}),
            &["properties"],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_call",
            "Calls one method on a live node. The method must be listed in permit and is rejected if it can quit, eval, or trampoline (call/rpc/free).",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "path": s(""), "method": s(""), "args": {"type": "array"}, "permit": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}, "description": "Methods this call is allowed to invoke. Must include method."}}),
            &["method", "permit"],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_signals",
            "Lists a live node's signals and current connections.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "path": s("")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_observe",
            "Connects to signals for a few frames and returns the ones that fired, then disconnects.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "path": s(""), "signals": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}}, "frames": i("How many frames to watch (default 1, max 600).")}),
            &["signals"],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_snapshot",
            "Compact snapshot of the running game: scene, frame, pause flag, and the current scene's children.",
            json!({"run_id": s("")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_input",
            "Injects input into the running game: key, text, mouse_move, mouse_button, touch, joy_button, joy_axis, wait. phase is tap (default), press, or release.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "steps": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "object"}}}),
            &["steps"],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_input_release",
            "Releases every key, mouse button, joypad button, axis and touch this server is still holding.",
            json!({"run_id": s("")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_wait",
            "Waits for process frames in the running game so input and screenshots can observe the result.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "frames": i("Default 1.")}),
            &[],
        ),
        tool(
            "godot_game_screenshot",
            "Captures the running game window, a viewport, or a Camera2D/Camera3D as a PNG. Fails instead of returning an empty image. Headless games have nothing to capture.",
            json!({"run_id": s(""), "node": s("Viewport or node whose viewport to capture."), "camera": s("Camera2D or Camera3D to make current for the shot."), "max_dimension": i("Longest side in pixels. Default 768.")}),
            &[],
        ),
    ]
}

fn s(description: &str) -> Value {
    if description.is_empty() {
        json!({"type": "string"})
    } else {
        json!({"type": "string", "description": description})
    }
}

fn i(description: &str) -> Value {
    if description.is_empty() {
        json!({"type": "integer"})
    } else {
        json!({"type": "integer", "description": description})
    }
}

fn b() -> Value {
    json!({"type": "boolean"})
}

fn tool(name: &str, description: &str, properties: Value, required: &[&str]) -> Tool {
    super::tool(name, description, properties, required)
}

pub async fn call(tools: &ToolManager, name: &str, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
    match name {
        "godot_run_output" => output(tools, &args).await,
        "godot_game_tree" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.tree").await,
        "godot_game_get" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.get").await,
        "godot_game_set" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.set").await,
        "godot_game_call" => game_call(tools, &args).await,
        "godot_game_signals" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.signals").await,
        "godot_game_observe" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.observe").await,
        "godot_game_snapshot" => runtime(tools, &args, "game.snapshot").await,
        "godot_game_input" => runtime(tools, &args, "input.run").await,
        "godot_game_input_release" => runtime(tools, &args, "input.release_all").await,
        "godot_game_wait" => runtime(tools, &args, "input.wait").await,
        "godot_game_screenshot" => screenshot(tools, &args).await,
        _ => ToolResult::error(format!("Tool '{name}' has no handler"), &[]),
    }
}

fn params_without_run(args: &Value) -> Value {
    let mut copy = args.clone();
    if let Value::Object(map) = &mut copy {
        map.remove("run_id");
        map.remove("project_path");
    }
    copy
}

async fn runtime(tools: &ToolManager, args: &Value, method: &str) -> ToolResult {
    match tools
        .runtime_call(str_arg(args, "run_id"), method, params_without_run(args))
        .await
    {
        Ok(value) => ToolResult::text(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&value).unwrap_or_default()),
        Err(error) => runtime_error(&error),
    }
}

async fn output(tools: &ToolManager, args: &Value) -> ToolResult {
    let since = args.get("since").and_then(Value::as_u64).unwrap_or(0);
    let level = str_arg(args, "level").unwrap_or("all");
    match tools.games.logs(str_arg(args, "run_id"), since, level) {
        Ok(value) => ToolResult::text(serde_json::to_string_pretty(&value).unwrap_or_default()),
        Err(_) => runtime(tools, args, "rt.output").await,
    }
}

async fn game_call(tools: &ToolManager, args: &Value) -> ToolResult {
    let Some(method) = str_arg(args, "method") else {
        return ToolResult::error("Missing method", &["Pass method and permit"]);
    };
    if !crate::paths::is_valid_identifier(method)
        || DENY_METHODS.contains(&method)
        || method.starts_with('_')
    {
        return ToolResult::error(
            format!("Method '{method}' is not allowed"),
            &["Call a normal gameplay method and list it in permit"],
        );
    }
    let permit = string_list(args.get("permit"));
    if !permit.iter().any(|item| item == method) {
        return ToolResult::error(
            format!("Method '{method}' is not in permit"),
            &["Pass permit: [\"the_method\"] for this call only"],
        );
    }
    runtime(tools, args, "game.call").await
}

async fn screenshot(tools: &ToolManager, args: &Value) -> ToolResult {
    let value = match tools
        .runtime_call(
            str_arg(args, "run_id"),
            "game.screenshot",
            params_without_run(args),
        )
        .await
    {
        Ok(value) => value,
        Err(error) => return runtime_error(&error),
    };
    let Some(data) = value.get("data").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
        return ToolResult::error(
            "The game did not return image data",
            &["Start a windowed game and retry godot_game_screenshot"],
        );
    };
    let bytes = match base64_decode(data) {
        Some(bytes) => bytes,
        None => {
            return ToolResult::error(
                "Screenshot payload was not valid base64",
                &["Update the Godot MCP addon runtime"],
            );
        }
    };
    let Some((width, height)) = png_dimensions(&bytes) else {
        return ToolResult::error(
            "Screenshot payload was not a PNG",
            &["The capture failed before a real image was produced"],
        );
    };
    let source = value
        .get("source")
        .and_then(Value::as_str)
        .unwrap_or("viewport");
    ToolResult::image_with_text(
        format!("Game screenshot {width}x{height} ({source})"),
        data,
        "image/png",
    )
}

fn runtime_error(error: &McpError) -> ToolResult {
    match error {
        McpError::BridgeUnavailable(_) | McpError::Bridge(_) | McpError::ToolExecution(_) => {
            super::bridge_error_result(error)
        }
        other => ToolResult::error(
            other.to_string(),
            &[
                "Start the game with godot_run_project",
                "For breakpoints, the server must launch the process (editor Play uses Godot's own debugger)",
            ],
        ),
    }
}

impl ToolManager {
    pub(super) async fn runtime_call(
        &self,
        run_id: Option<&str>,
        method: &str,
        params: Value,
    ) -> Result<Value, McpError> {
        if run_id != Some("editor") {
            match self.games.select(run_id) {
                Ok(Some(run)) => {
                    return run.runtime(method, params, Duration::from_secs(20)).await;
                }
                Ok(None) => {}
                Err(error) => return Err(error),
            }
        }
        editor_runtime(self, method, params).await
    }
}

async fn editor_runtime(
    tools: &ToolManager,
    method: &str,
    params: Value,
) -> Result<Value, McpError> {
    let mut events = tools.bridge.subscribe_events();
    let id = NEXT_EDITOR.fetch_add(1, std::sync::atomic::Ordering::Relaxed);
    let payload = serde_json::to_string(&json!({"id": id, "method": method, "params": params}))?;
    tools
        .bridge
        .send_command(
            "game_send",
            json!({"command": "mcp:request", "data": [payload], "session": -1}),
        )
        .await?;
    let deadline = tokio::time::Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(20);
    loop {
        let left = deadline.saturating_duration_since(tokio::time::Instant::now());
        if left.is_zero() {
            return Err(McpError::Process(
                "timed out waiting for the editor's running game".to_string(),
            ));
        }
        let event = match tokio::time::timeout(left, events.recv()).await {
            Ok(Some(event)) => event,
            Ok(None) => {
                return Err(McpError::Process(
                    "the editor bridge closed while waiting for the game".to_string(),
                ));
            }
            Err(_) => {
                return Err(McpError::Process(
                    "timed out waiting for the editor's running game".to_string(),
                ));
            }
        };
        if event.get("event").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("game.message") {
            continue;
        }
        if event.pointer("/data/message").and_then(Value::as_str) != Some("mcp:reply") {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(text) = event.pointer("/data/data/0").and_then(Value::as_str) else {
            continue;
        };
        let parsed: Value = serde_json::from_str(text)?;
        if !crate::godot::json_id_eq(parsed.get("id"), id) {
            continue;
        }
        if let Some(err) = parsed.get("error") {
            return Err(McpError::ToolExecution(err.to_string()));
        }
        return Ok(parsed.get("result").cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null));
    }
}

static NEXT_EDITOR: std::sync::atomic::AtomicI64 = std::sync::atomic::AtomicI64::new(1);

fn png_dimensions(bytes: &[u8]) -> Option<(u32, u32)> {
    if bytes.len() < 24 || &bytes[..8] != b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n" {
        return None;
    }
    let width = u32::from_be_bytes(bytes[16..20].try_into().ok()?);
    let height = u32::from_be_bytes(bytes[20..24].try_into().ok()?);
    if width == 0 || height == 0 {
        None
    } else {
        Some((width, height))
    }
}

fn base64_decode(text: &str) -> Option<Vec<u8>> {
    const TABLE: &[u8; 128] = &{
        let mut table = [255_u8; 128];
        let alphabet = b"ABCDEFGHIJKLMNOPQRSTUVWXYZabcdefghijklmnopqrstuvwxyz0123456789+/";
        let mut index = 0;
        while index < alphabet.len() {
            table[alphabet[index] as usize] = index as u8;
            index += 1;
        }
        table
    };
    let mut out = Vec::new();
    let mut buffer = 0_u32;
    let mut bits = 0;
    for byte in text.bytes() {
        if byte == b'=' || byte.is_ascii_whitespace() {
            continue;
        }
        if byte > 127 || TABLE[byte as usize] == 255 {
            return None;
        }
        buffer = (buffer << 6) | u32::from(TABLE[byte as usize]);
        bits += 6;
        if bits >= 8 {
            bits -= 8;
            out.push((buffer >> bits) as u8);
            buffer &= (1 << bits) - 1;
        }
    }
    Some(out)
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;

    #[test]
    fn png_magic_is_rejected_when_absent() {
        assert!(base64_decode("aaaa").is_some());
        assert!(base64_decode("@@@").is_none());
        assert!(png_dimensions(b"not a png").is_none());
        let mut png = b"\x89PNG\r\n\x1a\n".to_vec();
        png.extend_from_slice(&[0; 8]);
        png.extend_from_slice(&32u32.to_be_bytes());
        png.extend_from_slice(&16u32.to_be_bytes());
        assert_eq!(png_dimensions(&png), Some((32, 16)));
        png[16..20].copy_from_slice(&0u32.to_be_bytes());
        assert!(png_dimensions(&png).is_none());
    }
}

`src/util.rs`:

```rs
use std::sync::{Mutex, MutexGuard, PoisonError};

/// Locks a mutex, recovering the data if another thread panicked while holding it.
///
/// The guarded maps in this crate stay structurally valid after a panic, so continuing is
/// safer than propagating the poison and taking the whole server down.
pub fn lock<T>(mutex: &Mutex<T>) -> MutexGuard<'_, T> {
    mutex.lock().unwrap_or_else(PoisonError::into_inner)
}

`src/validation.rs`:

```rs
//! Minimal JSON-Schema checks for tool arguments: required keys, primitive types, enums.
//!
//! Catching wrong types here gives the caller an immediate, specific error instead of a
//! script error inside the editor that would only surface as a timeout.

use serde_json::{Map, Value};

pub fn validate_arguments(schema: &Value, arguments: &Value) -> Result<(), String> {
    let empty = Map::new();
    let arguments = match arguments {
        Value::Object(map) => map,
        Value::Null => &empty,
        other => {
            return Err(format!(
                "arguments must be an object, got {}",
                type_name(other)
            ));
        }
    };

    if let Some(required) = schema.get("required").and_then(Value::as_array) {
        for name in required.iter().filter_map(Value::as_str) {
            if arguments.get(name).is_none_or(Value::is_null) {
                return Err(format!("Missing required parameter: {name}"));
            }
        }
    }

    let Some(properties) = schema.get("properties").and_then(Value::as_object) else {
        return Ok(());
    };
    for (name, value) in arguments {
        if value.is_null() {
            continue;
        }
        let Some(property) = properties.get(name) else {
            continue;
        };
        check_value(name, value, property)?;
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn check_value(name: &str, value: &Value, property: &Value) -> Result<(), String> {
    if let Some(expected) = property.get("type").and_then(Value::as_str) {
        if !matches_type(value, expected) {
            return Err(format!(
                "Parameter '{name}' must be {} {expected}, got {}",
                article(expected),
                type_name(value)
            ));
        }
        if expected == "array" {
            if let (Some(items), Some(item_type)) = (
                value.as_array(),
                property
                    .get("items")
                    .and_then(|items| items.get("type"))
                    .and_then(Value::as_str),
            ) {
                if let Some(bad) = items.iter().find(|item| !matches_type(item, item_type)) {
                    return Err(format!(
                        "Every item of '{name}' must be {} {item_type}, got {}",
                        article(item_type),
                        type_name(bad)
                    ));
                }
            }
        }
    }

    if let Some(allowed) = property.get("enum").and_then(Value::as_array) {
        if !allowed.contains(value) {
            let options: Vec<String> = allowed.iter().map(Value::to_string).collect();
            return Err(format!(
                "Parameter '{name}' must be one of {}",
                options.join(", ")
            ));
        }
    }
    Ok(())
}

fn matches_type(value: &Value, expected: &str) -> bool {
    match expected {
        "string" => value.is_string(),
        "boolean" => value.is_boolean(),
        "object" => value.is_object(),
        "array" => value.is_array(),
        "number" => value.is_number(),
        "integer" => {
            value.is_i64()
                || value.is_u64()
                || value.as_f64().is_some_and(|number| number.fract() == 0.0)
        }
        _ => true,
    }
}

fn type_name(value: &Value) -> &'static str {
    match value {
        Value::Null => "null",
        Value::Bool(_) => "boolean",
        Value::Number(_) => "number",
        Value::String(_) => "string",
        Value::Array(_) => "array",
        Value::Object(_) => "object",
    }
}

fn article(type_name: &str) -> &'static str {
    if type_name.starts_with(['a', 'e', 'i', 'o', 'u']) {
        "an"
    } else {
        "a"
    }
}

#[cfg(test)]
mod tests {
    use super::*;
    use serde_json::json;

    fn schema() -> Value {
        json!({
            "type": "object",
            "properties": {
                "path": {"type": "string"},
                "count": {"type": "integer"},
                "flag": {"type": "boolean"},
                "map": {"type": "object"},
                "list": {"type": "array", "items": {"type": "string"}},
                "mode": {"type": "string", "enum": ["a", "b"]}
            },
            "required": ["path"]
        })
    }

    #[test]
    fn accepts_valid_arguments() {
        let args =
            json!({"path": "x", "count": 3, "flag": true, "map": {}, "list": ["a"], "mode": "a"});
        assert_eq!(validate_arguments(&schema(), &args), Ok(()));
        assert_eq!(
            validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": "x", "count": 3.0})),
            Ok(())
        );
    }

    #[test]
    fn missing_or_null_required_argument_is_named() {
        let err = validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({})).expect_err("missing");
        assert!(err.contains("path"), "{err}");
        let err = validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": null})).expect_err("null");
        assert!(err.contains("path"), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn wrong_types_name_the_parameter_and_both_types() {
        let err = validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": 5})).expect_err("type");
        assert!(
            err.contains("'path'") && err.contains("string") && err.contains("number"),
            "{err}"
        );

        let err =
            validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": "x", "count": 1.5})).expect_err("int");
        assert!(err.contains("integer"), "{err}");

        let err =
            validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": "x", "list": [1]})).expect_err("items");
        assert!(err.contains("list"), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn enum_violations_list_the_options() {
        let err =
            validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": "x", "mode": "c"})).expect_err("enum");
        assert!(err.contains("\"a\"") && err.contains("\"b\""), "{err}");
    }

    #[test]
    fn non_object_arguments_are_rejected_and_null_means_empty() {
        assert!(validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!("text")).is_err());
        let optional = json!({"type": "object", "properties": {}});
        assert_eq!(validate_arguments(&optional, &Value::Null), Ok(()));
    }

    #[test]
    fn unknown_properties_are_ignored() {
        assert_eq!(
            validate_arguments(&schema(), &json!({"path": "x", "extra": 1})),
            Ok(())
        );
    }
}

`tests/addon_export_test.rs`:

```rs
//! The addon must stay out of exported games. Runs a real headless Godot, so the tests are
//! ignored by default, like the editor end-to-end tests:
//!
//! ```bash
//! GODOT_PATH=/path/to/godot cargo test --test addon_export_test -- --ignored
//! ```
//!
//! A pack export needs no export templates, so no extra setup is required.

mod common;

use godot_mcp::bridge::token_path;
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// A script error in a `--script` run leaves Godot running instead of exiting.
const GODOT_TIMEOUT: Duration = Duration::from_secs(180);

/// Checks `should_skip` directly and writes "ok" or the first mismatch to the file named by
/// the first user argument. The function is static: an EditorExportPlugin can only be
/// instantiated by a running editor.
const SHOULD_SKIP_TEST: &str = r#"extends SceneTree

func _init() -> void:
    var out_path: String = OS.get_cmdline_user_args()[0]
    var plugin: Script = load("res://addons/godot_mcp/export_plugin.gd")
    var addon_dir: String = plugin.resource_path.get_base_dir()
    var cases: Dictionary = {
        "res://addons/godot_mcp/plugin.gd": true,
        "res://addons/godot_mcp/bridge_server.gd": true,
        "res://addons/godot_mcp/export_plugin.gd": true,
        "res://addons/godot_mcp/sub/data.tres": true,
        "res://addons/godot_mcp_other/plugin.gd": false,
        "res://addons/other/addons/godot_mcp/x.gd": false,
        "res://main.gd": false,
        "res://addons/godot_mcp": false,
    }
    var result: String = "ok"
    for path: String in cases:
        if plugin.call("should_skip", path, addon_dir) != cases[path]:
            result = "should_skip(%s, %s) != %s" % [path, addon_dir, cases[path]]
            break
    if plugin.call("should_skip", "res://main.gd", ""):
        result = "an unknown addon folder must not skip anything"
    var file: FileAccess = FileAccess.open(out_path, FileAccess.WRITE)
    file.store_string(result)
    file.close()
    quit()
"#;

/// Writes the names of the files in the zip named by the first user argument, one per line,
/// to the file named by the second, and the exported global class list to the third.
const LIST_ZIP: &str = r#"extends SceneTree

func _init() -> void:
    var args: PackedStringArray = OS.get_cmdline_user_args()
    var reader: ZIPReader = ZIPReader.new()
    var lines: String = ""
    var classes: String = ""
    if reader.open(args[0]) == OK:
        lines = "\n".join(reader.get_files())
        var class_list: String = ".godot/global_script_class_cache.cfg"
        if reader.file_exists(class_list):
            classes = reader.read_file(class_list).get_string_from_utf8()
        reader.close()
    var file: FileAccess = FileAccess.open(args[1], FileAccess.WRITE)
    file.store_string(lines)
    file.close()
    file = FileAccess.open(args[2], FileAccess.WRITE)
    file.store_string(classes)
    file.close()
    quit()
"#;

const MAIN_SCRIPT: &str = "extends Node\n";

const MAIN_SCENE: &str = r#"[gd_scene format=3]

[ext_resource type="Script" path="res://main.gd" id="1"]

[node name="Main" type="Node"]
script = ExtResource("1")
"#;

const EXPORT_PRESETS: &str = r#"[preset.0]

name="Pack"
platform="Linux"
runnable=true
export_filter="all_resources"
include_filter=""
exclude_filter=""
export_path=""

[preset.0.options]
"#;

fn godot_binary() -> PathBuf {
    let _ = dotenvy::dotenv();
    std::env::var("GODOT_PATH")
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .expect("set GODOT_PATH to run the addon export tests")
}

/// A project with a main scene and a copy of the addon, enabled.
struct Project {
    dir: tempfile::TempDir,
    port: u16,
}

impl Project {
    fn new() -> Self {
        let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("project dir");
        let root = dir.path();
        copy_dir(
            &Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("addons/godot_mcp"),
            &root.join("addons/godot_mcp"),
        );
        std::fs::write(
            root.join("project.godot"),
            "config_version=5\n\n[application]\n\nconfig/name=\"Export check\"\n\
             run/main_scene=\"res://main.tscn\"\n\n[editor_plugins]\n\n\
             enabled=PackedStringArray(\"res://addons/godot_mcp/plugin.cfg\")\n",
        )
        .expect("project.godot");
        std::fs::write(root.join("main.gd"), MAIN_SCRIPT).expect("main.gd");
        std::fs::write(root.join("main.tscn"), MAIN_SCENE).expect("main.tscn");
        std::fs::write(root.join("export_presets.cfg"), EXPORT_PRESETS).expect("presets");
        std::fs::write(root.join("should_skip_test.gd"), SHOULD_SKIP_TEST).expect("test script");
        std::fs::write(root.join("list_zip.gd"), LIST_ZIP).expect("list script");
        Self {
            dir,
            // The import and the export start the editor, and with it the bridge.
            port: common::free_port(),
        }
    }

    fn path(&self) -> &Path {
        self.dir.path()
    }

    fn godot(&self, args: &[&str]) {
        let mut child = Command::new(godot_binary())
            .arg("--headless")
            .arg("--path")
            .arg(self.path())
            .args(args)
            .env("GODOT_DEBUG_PORT", self.port.to_string())
            .stdin(Stdio::null())
            .stdout(Stdio::null())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .spawn()
            .expect("run godot");
        let started = Instant::now();
        let status = loop {
            if let Some(status) = child.try_wait().expect("wait for godot") {
                break status;
            }
            if started.elapsed() > GODOT_TIMEOUT {
                let _ = child.kill();
                panic!("godot {args:?} did not exit within {GODOT_TIMEOUT:?}");
            }
            std::thread::sleep(Duration::from_millis(100));
        };
        assert!(status.success(), "godot {args:?} failed: {status}");
    }
}

impl Drop for Project {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if let Some(path) = token_path(self.port) {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
        }
    }
}

fn copy_dir(from: &Path, to: &Path) {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(to).expect("create dir");
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(from).expect("read dir") {
        let entry = entry.expect("dir entry");
        let target = to.join(entry.file_name());
        if entry.file_type().expect("file type").is_dir() {
            copy_dir(&entry.path(), &target);
        } else {
            std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target).expect("copy file");
        }
    }
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
fn should_skip_matches_the_addon_folder_and_nothing_else() {
    let project = Project::new();
    let out = project.path().join("should_skip.txt");

    project.godot(&[
        "--script",
        "res://should_skip_test.gd",
        "--",
        &out.to_string_lossy(),
    ]);

    let result = std::fs::read_to_string(&out).expect("the test script wrote its result");
    assert_eq!(result, "ok");
}

#[test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
fn exported_pack_contains_the_game_but_not_the_addon() {
    let project = Project::new();
    let pack = project.path().join("out.zip");
    let listing = project.path().join("listing.txt");
    let class_list = project.path().join("classes.txt");

    project.godot(&["--import"]);
    project.godot(&["--export-pack", "Pack", &pack.to_string_lossy()]);
    project.godot(&[
        "--script",
        "res://list_zip.gd",
        "--",
        &pack.to_string_lossy(),
        &listing.to_string_lossy(),
        &class_list.to_string_lossy(),
    ]);

    let files = std::fs::read_to_string(&listing).expect("listing written");
    assert!(
        files.lines().any(|file| file.starts_with("main.gd")),
        "the game itself must be exported:\n{files}"
    );
    let leaked: Vec<&str> = files
        .lines()
        .filter(|file| file.starts_with("addons/godot_mcp/"))
        .collect();
    assert!(leaked.is_empty(), "addon files were exported: {leaked:?}");

    // The class list is exported with every game; a class whose script was left out would
    // dangle in it.
    let classes = std::fs::read_to_string(&class_list).expect("class list written");
    assert!(
        !classes.contains("addons/godot_mcp"),
        "the exported class list still names the addon:\n{classes}"
    );
}

`tests/bridge_client_test.rs`:

```rs
//! `BridgeClient` against a fake editor: error classes, multiplexing, reconnects.

mod common;

use common::{MockBridge, Reply, free_port};
use godot_mcp::bridge::BridgeClient;
use godot_mcp::error::McpError;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

#[tokio::test]
async fn reports_unavailable_when_nothing_listens() {
    let client = BridgeClient::new(free_port());
    let err = client
        .send_command("ping", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect_err("no editor is running");
    assert!(matches!(err, McpError::BridgeUnavailable(_)), "{err:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn editor_reported_errors_keep_their_message() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Err("Node not found at path: X".into())).await;
    let client = BridgeClient::new(bridge.port);

    let err = client
        .send_command("remove_node", json!({"node_path": "X"}))
        .await
        .expect_err("editor reports an error");

    match err {
        McpError::ToolExecution(message) => assert_eq!(message, "Node not found at path: X"),
        other => panic!("unexpected error class: {other:?}"),
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn concurrent_commands_are_matched_by_command_id() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|cmd| match cmd["type"].as_str() {
        Some("slow") => Reply::After(Duration::from_millis(600), json!({"which": "slow"})),
        _ => Reply::Ok(json!({"which": "fast"})),
    })
    .await;
    let client = BridgeClient::new(bridge.port);

    let started = Instant::now();
    let (slow, fast) = tokio::join!(
        async {
            let value = client.send_command("slow", json!({})).await;
            (value, started.elapsed())
        },
        async {
            let value = client.send_command("fast", json!({})).await;
            (value, started.elapsed())
        }
    );

    assert_eq!(slow.0.expect("slow ok")["which"], "slow");
    assert_eq!(fast.0.expect("fast ok")["which"], "fast");
    assert!(
        fast.1 < Duration::from_millis(400),
        "fast reply was held back by the slow one: {:?}",
        fast.1
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn reconnects_after_the_editor_restarts() {
    let first = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Ok(json!({"gen": 1}))).await;
    let port = first.port;
    let client = BridgeClient::new(port);
    assert_eq!(
        client.send_command("ping", json!({})).await.expect("first")["gen"],
        1
    );

    first.shutdown();
    drop(first);
    let _second = MockBridge::start_on(port, |_| Reply::Ok(json!({"gen": 2}))).await;

    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(5);
    let mut last: Option<Value> = None;
    while Instant::now() < deadline {
        if let Ok(value) = client.send_command("ping", json!({})).await {
            last = Some(value);
            break;
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(200)).await;
    }
    assert_eq!(last.expect("client must reconnect")["gen"], 2);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn unanswered_commands_time_out_with_a_bridge_error() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Never).await;
    let client = BridgeClient::new(bridge.port).with_timeout(Duration::from_millis(300));

    let err = client
        .send_command("ping", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect_err("nobody answers");
    assert!(matches!(err, McpError::Bridge(_)), "{err:?}");
}

`tests/common/mod.rs`:

```rs
//! Shared helpers for the integration tests: an MCP stdio session that drives the real
//! `godot-mcp` binary, and a scriptable fake Godot editor bridge.
#![allow(dead_code)]

use futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::sync::{Arc, Mutex};
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader, Lines};
use tokio::net::TcpListener;
use tokio::process::{Child, ChildStdin, ChildStdout, Command};
use tokio::sync::mpsc;
use tokio::task::JoinHandle;
use tokio::time::timeout;
use tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::Message;

/// Returns a TCP port on 127.0.0.1 that nothing is listening on right now.
pub fn free_port() -> u16 {
    let listener = std::net::TcpListener::bind("127.0.0.1:0").expect("bind ephemeral port");
    listener.local_addr().expect("local addr").port()
}

/// Creates a directory that looks like a Godot project (contains `project.godot`).
pub fn project_dir() -> tempfile::TempDir {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("temp project");
    std::fs::write(
        dir.path().join("project.godot"),
        "config_version=5\n\n[application]\n\nconfig/name=\"Test\"\n",
    )
    .expect("write project.godot");
    dir
}

/// A stand-in for the Godot executable that writes noise to stdout and stderr, like a real
/// game process would.
pub fn fake_godot(dir: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        let path = dir.join("fake_godot.bat");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "@echo off\r\necho NOISE_ON_STDOUT\r\necho NOISE_ON_STDERR 1>&2\r\n",
        )
        .expect("write fake godot");
        path
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let path = dir.join("fake_godot.sh");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "#!/bin/sh\necho NOISE_ON_STDOUT\necho NOISE_ON_STDERR >&2\n",
        )
        .expect("write fake godot");
        std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755))
            .expect("chmod fake godot");
        path
    }
}

/// A stand-in for the Godot executable that stays alive for a few seconds, like an editor.
pub fn sleeping_godot(dir: &Path) -> PathBuf {
    #[cfg(windows)]
    {
        let path = dir.join("sleeping_godot.bat");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "@echo off
ping -n 9 127.0.0.1 > nul
",
        )
        .expect("write sleeping godot");
        path
    }
    #[cfg(unix)]
    {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let path = dir.join("sleeping_godot.sh");
        std::fs::write(
            &path,
            "#!/bin/sh
sleep 8
",
        )
        .expect("write sleeping godot");
        std::fs::set_permissions(&path, std::fs::Permissions::from_mode(0o755))
            .expect("chmod sleeping godot");
        path
    }
}

/// Concatenated text content of a `tools/call` response.
pub fn tool_text(response: &Value) -> String {
    response["result"]["content"]
        .as_array()
        .map(|items| {
            items
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|item| item["text"].as_str())
                .collect::<Vec<_>>()
                .join("\n")
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

pub fn is_tool_error(response: &Value) -> bool {
    response["result"]["isError"].as_bool().unwrap_or(false)
}

/// An MCP client talking to a freshly spawned `godot-mcp` process over stdio.
pub struct Session {
    _child: Child,
    stdin: ChildStdin,
    stdout: Lines<BufReader<ChildStdout>>,
    next_id: u64,
    _cwd: tempfile::TempDir,
}

impl Session {
    pub async fn start(bridge_port: u16, godot: &Path) -> Self {
        Self::start_with_env(bridge_port, godot, &[]).await
    }

    pub async fn start_with_env(bridge_port: u16, godot: &Path, env: &[(&str, &str)]) -> Self {
        let cwd = tempfile::tempdir().expect("session cwd");
        Self::spawn(cwd, |command| {
            command
                .env("GODOT_PATH", godot)
                .env("GODOT_DEBUG_PORT", bridge_port.to_string());
            for (key, value) in env {
                command.env(key, value);
            }
        })
        .await
    }

    /// Starts the server with no GODOT_* variables in its environment but a `.env` file with
    /// the given contents in its working directory.
    pub async fn start_with_dotenv(contents: &str) -> Self {
        let cwd = tempfile::tempdir().expect("session cwd");
        std::fs::write(cwd.path().join(".env"), contents).expect("write .env");
        Self::spawn(cwd, |_| {}).await
    }

    async fn spawn(cwd: tempfile::TempDir, configure: impl FnOnce(&mut Command)) -> Self {
        let mut command = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_godot-mcp"));
        for key in [
            "GODOT_PATH",
            "GODOT_CONSOLE_PATH",
            "GODOT_DEBUG_PORT",
            "GODOT_MCP_TOKEN",
            "GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK",
            "GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH",
        ] {
            command.env_remove(key);
        }
        // Keep the run hermetic: never pick up a real bridge token file of the developer.
        command
            .env("APPDATA", cwd.path())
            .env("HOME", cwd.path())
            .env("XDG_DATA_HOME", cwd.path());
        configure(&mut command);
        command
            .current_dir(cwd.path())
            .stdin(Stdio::piped())
            .stdout(Stdio::piped())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .kill_on_drop(true);

        let mut child = command.spawn().expect("spawn godot-mcp");
        let stdin = child.stdin.take().expect("child stdin");
        let stdout = BufReader::new(child.stdout.take().expect("child stdout")).lines();
        let mut session = Self {
            _child: child,
            stdin,
            stdout,
            next_id: 1,
            _cwd: cwd,
        };
        // Wait until the server answers, so tests that assert on timing do not include the
        // process start-up (a freshly built executable can take seconds to launch on Windows).
        session
            .request("ping", json!({}), Duration::from_secs(60))
            .await;
        session
    }

    pub async fn send_raw(&mut self, line: &str) {
        self.stdin
            .write_all(format!("{line}\n").as_bytes())
            .await
            .expect("write to godot-mcp stdin");
        self.stdin.flush().await.expect("flush godot-mcp stdin");
    }

    pub async fn send(&mut self, value: &Value) {
        self.send_raw(&value.to_string()).await;
    }

    /// Next stdout frame, or `None` if nothing arrived in time. Panics when stdout carries
    /// anything that is not a JSON document: the channel is reserved for JSON-RPC frames.
    pub async fn recv(&mut self, within: Duration) -> Option<Value> {
        match timeout(within, self.stdout.next_line()).await {
            Err(_) | Ok(Ok(None)) => None,
            Ok(Ok(Some(line))) => Some(
                serde_json::from_str(&line)
                    .unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("non-JSON line on MCP stdout: {line:?}")),
            ),
            Ok(Err(err)) => panic!("failed reading MCP stdout: {err}"),
        }
    }

    /// Sends a request and waits for the response carrying the same id.
    pub async fn request(&mut self, method: &str, params: Value, within: Duration) -> Value {
        let id = self.next_id;
        self.next_id += 1;
        self.send(&json!({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "method": method, "params": params}))
            .await;
        loop {
            let frame = self
                .recv(within)
                .await
                .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no response to {method} within {within:?}"));
            if frame["id"] == json!(id) {
                return frame;
            }
        }
    }

    pub async fn call_tool(&mut self, name: &str, arguments: Value, within: Duration) -> Value {
        self.request(
            "tools/call",
            json!({"name": name, "arguments": arguments}),
            within,
        )
        .await
    }
}

/// How the fake editor answers a single command.
pub enum Reply {
    Ok(Value),
    Err(String),
    After(Duration, Value),
    Never,
}

/// A fake Godot editor bridge: a WebSocket server answering commands from a closure.
pub struct MockBridge {
    pub port: u16,
    pub received: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Value>>>,
    accept_task: JoinHandle<()>,
    connections: Arc<Mutex<Vec<JoinHandle<()>>>>,
}

impl MockBridge {
    pub async fn start<F>(handler: F) -> Self
    where
        F: Fn(&Value) -> Reply + Send + Sync + 'static,
    {
        Self::start_on(free_port(), handler).await
    }

    pub async fn start_on<F>(port: u16, handler: F) -> Self
    where
        F: Fn(&Value) -> Reply + Send + Sync + 'static,
    {
        let listener = bind_with_retry(port).await;
        let handler = Arc::new(handler);
        let received = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));
        let connections = Arc::new(Mutex::new(Vec::new()));

        let accept_task = tokio::spawn({
            let received = received.clone();
            let connections = connections.clone();
            async move {
                while let Ok((tcp, _)) = listener.accept().await {
                    let task = tokio::spawn(serve(tcp, handler.clone(), received.clone()));
                    connections.lock().expect("connections lock").push(task);
                }
            }
        });

        Self {
            port,
            received,
            accept_task,
            connections,
        }
    }

    /// Stops accepting and drops every open connection, like an editor being closed.
    pub fn shutdown(&self) {
        self.accept_task.abort();
        for task in self.connections.lock().expect("connections lock").drain(..) {
            task.abort();
        }
    }

    pub fn received(&self) -> Vec<Value> {
        self.received.lock().expect("received lock").clone()
    }
}

impl Drop for MockBridge {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        self.shutdown();
    }
}

async fn bind_with_retry(port: u16) -> TcpListener {
    for _ in 0..40 {
        if let Ok(listener) = TcpListener::bind(("127.0.0.1", port)).await {
            return listener;
        }
        tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
    panic!("could not bind mock bridge on port {port}");
}

async fn serve<F>(tcp: tokio::net::TcpStream, handler: Arc<F>, received: Arc<Mutex<Vec<Value>>>)
where
    F: Fn(&Value) -> Reply + Send + Sync + 'static,
{
    let Ok(ws) = tokio_tungstenite::accept_async(tcp).await else {
        return;
    };
    let (mut sink, mut stream) = ws.split();
    let (out_tx, mut out_rx) = mpsc::unbounded_channel::<String>();
    let writer = tokio::spawn(async move {
        while let Some(text) = out_rx.recv().await {
            if sink.send(Message::Text(text.into())).await.is_err() {
                break;
            }
        }
    });

    while let Some(Ok(message)) = stream.next().await {
        let Message::Text(text) = message else {
            continue;
        };
        let Ok(command) = serde_json::from_str::<Value>(text.as_str()) else {
            continue;
        };
        received
            .lock()
            .expect("received lock")
            .push(command.clone());
        let id = command["commandId"].clone();
        match handler(&command) {
            Reply::Ok(result) => {
                let _ = out_tx.send(success(&id, result));
            }
            Reply::Err(message) => {
                let _ = out_tx.send(
                    json!({"commandId": id, "status": "error", "error": message}).to_string(),
                );
            }
            Reply::After(delay, result) => {
                let tx = out_tx.clone();
                tokio::spawn(async move {
                    tokio::time::sleep(delay).await;
                    let _ = tx.send(success(&id, result));
                });
            }
            Reply::Never => {}
        }
    }
    writer.abort();
}

fn success(id: &Value, result: Value) -> String {
    json!({"commandId": id, "status": "success", "result": result}).to_string()
}

/// The stand-in for the GitHub command-line client: an example target of this package. A test
/// run that did not build it (`cargo test --test ...` builds only the named test) builds it here,
/// once, with the profile of the running test; an up-to-date build costs a moment.
pub fn fake_gh() -> PathBuf {
    static PATH: std::sync::OnceLock<PathBuf> = std::sync::OnceLock::new();
    PATH.get_or_init(|| {
        let exe = std::env::current_exe().expect("test executable path");
        let profile_dir = exe
            .parent()
            .and_then(Path::parent)
            .expect("profile directory")
            .to_path_buf();
        let name = if cfg!(windows) {
            "fake_gh.exe"
        } else {
            "fake_gh"
        };
        let built = profile_dir.join("examples").join(name);
        {
            let mut command = std::process::Command::new(env!("CARGO"));
            command
                .args(["build", "--example", "fake_gh", "--manifest-path"])
                .arg(concat!(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR"), "/Cargo.toml"));
            match profile_dir.file_name().and_then(|name| name.to_str()) {
                Some("debug") | None => {}
                Some("release") => {
                    command.arg("--release");
                }
                Some(other) => {
                    command.args(["--profile", other]);
                }
            }
            let status = command.status().expect("run cargo for the fake gh example");
            assert!(status.success(), "building the fake gh example failed");
        }
        assert!(built.exists(), "fake gh not found at {}", built.display());
        built
    })
    .clone()
}

/// Environment pairs as the `&str` pairs the session helpers take.
pub fn as_pairs(owned: &[(String, String)]) -> Vec<(&str, &str)> {
    owned
        .iter()
        .map(|(key, value)| (key.as_str(), value.as_str()))
        .collect()
}

/// A scripted stand-in for `gh`: what it answers is set through its environment, and what it
/// was called with is read back from the files it writes.
pub struct FakeGh {
    dir: tempfile::TempDir,
    script: Vec<(String, String)>,
}

impl FakeGh {
    pub fn new() -> Self {
        Self {
            dir: tempfile::tempdir().expect("fake gh directory"),
            script: Vec::new(),
        }
    }

    /// Sets one scripting variable (`FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT`, `FAKE_GH_LIST_EXIT`, ...).
    pub fn script(mut self, key: &str, value: &str) -> Self {
        self.script.push((key.to_string(), value.to_string()));
        self
    }

    fn log_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.dir.path().join("calls.log")
    }

    fn body_path(&self) -> PathBuf {
        self.dir.path().join("body.txt")
    }

    /// Variables that make a process run the stand-in as scripted and record into this object.
    pub fn env_pairs(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        let mut pairs = vec![
            (
                "FAKE_GH_LOG".to_string(),
                self.log_path().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            ),
            (
                "FAKE_GH_BODY".to_string(),
                self.body_path().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
            ),
        ];
        pairs.extend(self.script.clone());
        pairs
    }

    /// The same, plus the setting that makes the server use the stand-in as its `gh`.
    pub fn server_env(&self) -> Vec<(String, String)> {
        let mut pairs = self.env_pairs();
        pairs.push((
            "GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH".to_string(),
            fake_gh().to_string_lossy().into_owned(),
        ));
        pairs
    }

    /// Every invocation so far, as its argument list.
    pub fn calls(&self) -> Vec<Vec<String>> {
        let Ok(log) = std::fs::read_to_string(self.log_path()) else {
            return Vec::new();
        };
        log.split("[call]\n")
            .skip(1)
            .map(|block| block.lines().map(str::to_string).collect())
            .collect()
    }

    /// Standard input of the last `issue create`, if there was one.
    pub fn body(&self) -> Option<String> {
        std::fs::read_to_string(self.body_path()).ok()
    }
}

`tests/detach_test.rs`:

```rs
//! A process launched for the user (editor, game) must not stay tied to the MCP server.

mod common;

use common::{free_port, project_dir, sleeping_godot, tool_text};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::Duration;
use tokio::io::{AsyncBufReadExt, AsyncReadExt, AsyncWriteExt, BufReader};
use tokio::process::Command;
use tokio::time::timeout;

/// An MCP client reads the server's stderr until it closes. A launched editor that inherited
/// the pipe keeps it open for as long as the editor runs, so the client would wait forever
/// after the server is gone.
#[tokio::test]
async fn launched_process_does_not_hold_the_servers_stderr_open() {
    let project = project_dir();
    let godot = sleeping_godot(project.path());
    let home = tempfile::tempdir().expect("hermetic home");

    let mut server = Command::new(env!("CARGO_BIN_EXE_godot-mcp"))
        .env("GODOT_PATH", &godot)
        .env("GODOT_DEBUG_PORT", free_port().to_string())
        .env_remove("GODOT_MCP_TOKEN")
        .env("APPDATA", home.path())
        .env("HOME", home.path())
        .env("XDG_DATA_HOME", home.path())
        .current_dir(home.path())
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .kill_on_drop(true)
        .spawn()
        .expect("spawn godot-mcp");

    let mut stdin = server.stdin.take().expect("stdin");
    let mut stdout = BufReader::new(server.stdout.take().expect("stdout")).lines();
    let mut stderr = server.stderr.take().expect("stderr");

    let request = json!({"jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 1, "method": "tools/call",
        "params": {"name": "godot_launch_editor",
                   "arguments": {"project_path": project.path()}}});
    stdin
        .write_all(format!("{request}\n").as_bytes())
        .await
        .expect("send request");
    stdin.flush().await.expect("flush request");

    let line = timeout(Duration::from_secs(60), stdout.next_line())
        .await
        .expect("a response in time")
        .expect("read response")
        .expect("a response line");
    let response: Value = serde_json::from_str(&line).expect("JSON response");
    let reply = tool_text(&json!({ "result": response["result"] }));
    assert!(reply.contains("launched"), "{reply}");

    // The launched process now runs for several seconds; the server goes away first.
    server.kill().await.expect("kill server");

    let mut output = Vec::new();
    let closed = timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), stderr.read_to_end(&mut output)).await;
    assert!(
        closed.is_ok(),
        "the launched process still holds the server's stderr open"
    );
}
