`tests/editor_e2e_test.rs`:

```rs
//! End-to-end tests against a real headless Godot editor with the addon enabled.
//!
//! Ignored by default because they need a Godot 4.5+ executable. Run them with:
//!
//! ```bash
//! GODOT_PATH=/path/to/godot cargo test --test editor_e2e_test -- --ignored
//! ```
//!
//! `GODOT_PATH` may also come from a `.env` file. `GODOT_CONSOLE_PATH` (Windows) is used
//! for the editor process when set, so its log can be inspected.

mod common;

use godot_mcp::bridge::{BridgeClient, token_path};
use godot_mcp::error::McpError;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::process::Stdio;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};
use tokio::process::{Child, Command};
use tokio::time::{sleep, timeout};

const IGNORE_REASON: &str = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable";

struct Editor {
    _dir: tempfile::TempDir,
    project: PathBuf,
    port: u16,
    bridge: BridgeClient,
    log: PathBuf,
    _child: Child,
}

impl Drop for Editor {
    fn drop(&mut self) {
        if std::thread::panicking() {
            // A failed assertion rarely explains itself; the editor's own output usually does.
            let log = self.log_text();
            let start = log.len().saturating_sub(6000);
            let start = (start..log.len())
                .find(|&index| log.is_char_boundary(index))
                .unwrap_or(0);
            eprintln!("--- editor log (tail) ---\n{}", &log[start..]);
        }
        if let Some(path) = token_path(self.port) {
            let _ = std::fs::remove_file(path);
        }
    }
}

impl Editor {
    fn log_text(&self) -> String {
        std::fs::read_to_string(&self.log).unwrap_or_default()
    }
}

fn godot_binaries() -> (PathBuf, PathBuf) {
    let _ = dotenvy::dotenv();
    let godot = std::env::var("GODOT_PATH")
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .expect("set GODOT_PATH to run the editor end-to-end tests");
    let console = std::env::var("GODOT_CONSOLE_PATH")
        .ok()
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .filter(|path| path.exists())
        .unwrap_or_else(|| godot.clone());
    (godot, console)
}

fn copy_dir(from: &Path, to: &Path) {
    std::fs::create_dir_all(to).expect("create dir");
    for entry in std::fs::read_dir(from).expect("read dir") {
        let entry = entry.expect("dir entry");
        let target = to.join(entry.file_name());
        if entry.file_type().expect("file type").is_dir() {
            copy_dir(&entry.path(), &target);
        } else {
            std::fs::copy(entry.path(), target).expect("copy file");
        }
    }
}

const MAIN_SCENE: &str = r#"[gd_scene format=3]

[node name="Main" type="Node2D"]

[node name="Player" type="Node2D" parent="."]
position = Vector2(5, 7)

[node name="Body" type="Sprite2D" parent="Player"]

[node name="Weapon" type="Node2D" parent="Player"]

[node name="UI" type="Control" parent="."]
"#;

/// Test-only command that undoes the last editor action, appended to a *copy* of the addon.
const UNDO_HOOK: &str = r#"        "__test_undo":
            var history_id: int = undo_redo.get_object_history_id(EditorInterface.get_edited_scene_root())
            undo_redo.get_history_undo_redo(history_id).undo()
            send_success(peer, cmd_id, {"undone": true})
        "__test_hand_edit":
            var root: Node = EditorInterface.get_edited_scene_root()
            var node := Node.new()
            node.name = "ByHand"
            undo_redo.create_action("Hand edit", UndoRedo.MERGE_DISABLE, root)
            undo_redo.add_do_method(root, "add_child", node, true)
            undo_redo.add_do_property(node, "owner", root)
            undo_redo.add_do_reference(node)
            undo_redo.add_undo_method(root, "remove_child", node)
            undo_redo.commit_action()
            send_success(peer, cmd_id, {"added": true})
        "__test_open_directly":
            EditorInterface.open_scene_from_path(str(params.get("scene_path", "")))
            send_success(peer, cmd_id, {"opened": true})
        "ping":
"#;

async fn start_editor(with_undo_hook: bool) -> Editor {
    start_editor_configured(with_undo_hook, "").await
}

async fn start_editor_configured(with_undo_hook: bool, project_tail: &str) -> Editor {
    let (godot, console) = godot_binaries();
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("project dir");
    let project = dir.path().to_path_buf();

    std::fs::write(
        project.join("project.godot"),
        format!(
            "config_version=5\n\n[application]\n\nconfig/name=\"MCP E2E Fixture\"\n\
             run/main_scene=\"res://main.tscn\"\nconfig/features=PackedStringArray(\"4.5\")\n\n\
             [editor_plugins]\n\nenabled=PackedStringArray(\"res://addons/godot_mcp/plugin.cfg\")\n{project_tail}"
        ),
    )
    .expect("project.godot");
    std::fs::write(project.join("main.tscn"), MAIN_SCENE).expect("main.tscn");
    std::fs::write(project.join("script.gd"), "extends Node\n").expect("script.gd");

    let mut big = String::from("[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Big\" type=\"Node2D\"]\n");
    for i in 0..3000 {
        big.push_str(&format!(
            "\n[node name=\"Node{i:04}\" type=\"Node2D\" parent=\".\"]\n"
        ));
    }
    std::fs::write(project.join("big.tscn"), big).expect("big.tscn");

    let addon = project.join("addons/godot_mcp");
    copy_dir(
        &Path::new(env!("CARGO_MANIFEST_DIR")).join("addons/godot_mcp"),
        &addon,
    );
    if with_undo_hook {
        let script_path = addon.join("bridge_server.gd");
        let source = std::fs::read_to_string(&script_path).expect("bridge script");
        let anchor = "        \"ping\":\n";
        assert!(source.contains(anchor), "dispatch table anchor not found");
        std::fs::write(&script_path, source.replacen(anchor, UNDO_HOOK, 1))
            .expect("patch bridge script");
    }

    let imported = timeout(
        Duration::from_secs(180),
        Command::new(&godot)
            .args(["--headless", "--path"])
            .arg(&project)
            .arg("--import")
            .stdout(Stdio::null())
            .stderr(Stdio::null())
            .status(),
    )
    .await
    .expect("import timed out")
    .expect("run godot --import");
    assert!(imported.success(), "godot --import failed");

    let port = common::free_port();
    let log = project.join("editor.log");
    let log_file = std::fs::File::create(&log).expect("log file");
    let child = Command::new(&console)
        .args(["--headless", "--editor", "--path"])
        .arg(&project)
        .env("GODOT_DEBUG_PORT", port.to_string())
        .stdout(Stdio::from(log_file.try_clone().expect("clone log")))
        .stderr(Stdio::from(log_file))
        .kill_on_drop(true)
        .spawn()
        .expect("spawn godot editor");

    let bridge = BridgeClient::new(port);
    let deadline = Instant::now() + Duration::from_secs(120);
    loop {
        if bridge.send_command("ping", json!({})).await.is_ok() {
            break;
        }
        assert!(
            Instant::now() < deadline,
            "editor bridge did not come up; log:\n{}",
            std::fs::read_to_string(&log).unwrap_or_default()
        );
        sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
    }

    Editor {
        _dir: dir,
        project,
        port,
        bridge,
        log,
        _child: child,
    }
}

fn expect_error(result: Result<Value, McpError>, needle: &str) {
    match result {
        Err(McpError::ToolExecution(message)) => assert!(
            message.contains(needle),
            "error {message:?} should mention {needle:?}"
        ),
        other => panic!("expected an editor error mentioning {needle:?}, got {other:?}"),
    }
}

fn find<'a>(node: &'a Value, name: &str) -> Option<&'a Value> {
    if node["name"] == name {
        return Some(node);
    }
    node["children"]
        .as_array()?
        .iter()
        .find_map(|child| find(child, name))
}

fn count_nodes(node: &Value) -> usize {
    1 + node["children"]
        .as_array()
        .map(|children| children.iter().map(count_nodes).sum())
        .unwrap_or(0)
}

fn child_names(node: &Value) -> Vec<String> {
    node["children"]
        .as_array()
        .map(|children| {
            children
                .iter()
                .filter_map(|child| child["name"].as_str().map(String::from))
                .collect()
        })
        .unwrap_or_default()
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
async fn bridge_is_loopback_only_and_requires_the_token() {
    let _ = IGNORE_REASON;
    use futures_util::{SinkExt, StreamExt};
    use tokio_tungstenite::connect_async;
    use tokio_tungstenite::tungstenite::Message;

    let editor = start_editor(false).await;
    let url = format!("ws://127.0.0.1:{}", editor.port);

    for token in [None, Some("wrong-token")] {
        let (mut ws, _) = connect_async(&url).await.expect("loopback handshake");
        let mut command = json!({"commandId": "t", "type": "get_project_info", "params": {}});
        if let Some(token) = token {
            command["token"] = json!(token);
        }
        ws.send(Message::Text(command.to_string().into()))
            .await
            .expect("send");
        let reply = timeout(Duration::from_secs(5), ws.next())
            .await
            .expect("reply in time")
            .expect("stream open")
            .expect("frame");
        let reply: Value =
            serde_json::from_str(reply.to_text().expect("text frame")).expect("json");
        assert_eq!(reply["status"], "error", "{reply}");
        assert!(
            reply["error"]
                .as_str()
                .unwrap_or_default()
                .contains("Unauthorized"),
            "{reply}"
        );
    }

    // The authenticated client works.
    let info = editor
        .bridge
        .send_command("get_project_info", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("authorized call");
    assert_eq!(info["name"], "MCP E2E Fixture");

    // Other OS users cannot read the token file (it is not written when GODOT_MCP_TOKEN is set).
    #[cfg(unix)]
    if std::env::var_os("GODOT_MCP_TOKEN").is_none() {
        use std::os::unix::fs::PermissionsExt;
        let path = token_path(editor.port).expect("token path");
        let mode = std::fs::metadata(&path)
            .expect("token file")
            .permissions()
            .mode()
            & 0o777;
        assert_eq!(
            mode,
            0o600,
            "token file {} has mode {mode:o}",
            path.display()
        );
    }

    // Not reachable through any non-loopback address of this host.
    let lan_ip = std::net::UdpSocket::bind("0.0.0.0:0")
        .and_then(|socket| {
            socket.connect("192.0.2.1:9")?;
            socket.local_addr()
        })
        .map(|addr| addr.ip())
        .ok()
        .filter(|ip| !ip.is_loopback() && !ip.is_unspecified());
    if let Some(ip) = lan_ip {
        let attempt = timeout(
            Duration::from_secs(3),
            tokio::net::TcpStream::connect((ip, editor.port)),
        )
        .await;
        assert!(
            !matches!(attempt, Ok(Ok(_))),
            "bridge accepted a connection on {ip}"
        );
    }
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
async fn scene_and_property_tools_behave() {
    let editor = start_editor(false).await;
    let bridge = &editor.bridge;
    let call = |command: &'static str, params: Value| async move {
        bridge.send_command(command, params).await
    };

    call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://main.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("open main");

    // An already-open scene edited on disk must be reloaded. open_scene used to
    // report success while get_scene_tree still showed the in-memory copy.
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("stale.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Stale\" type=\"Node2D\"]\n\n[node name=\"Before\" type=\"Node\" parent=\".\"]\n",
    )
    .expect("stale scene");
    let first = call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://stale.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("open stale");
    assert_eq!(first["reloaded"], false, "{first}");
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("stale.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Stale\" type=\"Node2D\"]\n\n[node name=\"FromDisk\" type=\"Node\" parent=\".\"]\n",
    )
    .expect("rewrite stale scene");
    let unread = call("get_scene_tree", json!({"scene_path": "res://stale.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("tree before reload");
    assert_eq!(unread["stale"], true, "{unread}");
    assert_eq!(unread["reloaded"], false, "{unread}");
    assert!(
        find(&unread["tree"], "Before").is_some(),
        "a read reloaded the scene: {unread}"
    );
    let reloaded = call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://stale.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("reload stale");
    assert_eq!(reloaded["reloaded"], true, "{reloaded}");
    let stale_tree = call("get_scene_tree", json!({"scene_path": "res://stale.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("stale tree");
    assert!(
        find(&stale_tree["tree"], "FromDisk").is_some(),
        "in-memory scene was not reloaded: {stale_tree}"
    );
    assert!(
        find(&stale_tree["tree"], "Before").is_none(),
        "old node survived the reload: {stale_tree}"
    );
    call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://main.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("return to main");

    // Paths in responses are relative to the edited scene.
    let tree = call("get_scene_tree", json!({})).await.expect("tree");
    assert_eq!(tree["tree"]["path"], ".");
    assert_eq!(
        find(&tree["tree"], "Body").expect("Body")["path"],
        "Player/Body"
    );

    // Node paths cannot leave the edited scene and the scene root cannot be removed.
    for path in ["..", "../..", "/root"] {
        expect_error(
            call("get_node_properties", json!({"node_path": path})).await,
            "",
        );
        expect_error(
            call(
                "set_node_properties",
                json!({"node_path": path, "properties": {"name": "x"}}),
            )
            .await,
            "",
        );
        expect_error(call("remove_node", json!({"node_path": path})).await, "");
    }
    expect_error(call("remove_node", json!({"node_path": "."})).await, "root");
    expect_error(call("remove_node", json!({"node_path": ""})).await, "");

    // Wrongly typed parameters are rejected immediately instead of hanging the call.
    let started = Instant::now();
    expect_error(
        call("get_node_properties", json!({"node_path": 5})).await,
        "node_path",
    );
    expect_error(
        call("resave_resources", json!({"paths": "res://icon.svg"})).await,
        "paths",
    );
    assert!(started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3));

    // Properties come with their types and round-trip through typed conversion.
    let props = call("get_node_properties", json!({"node_path": "Player"}))
        .await
        .expect("props");
    assert_eq!(
        props["properties"]["position"],
        json!({"type": "Vector2", "value": "Vector2(5, 7)"})
    );

    let read = |name: &'static str| async move {
        let props = bridge
            .send_command("get_node_properties", json!({"node_path": "Player"}))
            .await
            .expect("props");
        props["properties"][name]["value"].clone()
    };

    call(
        "set_node_properties",
        json!({"node_path": "Player", "properties": {"position": [10, 20]}}),
    )
    .await
    .expect("set position");
    assert_eq!(read("position").await, "Vector2(10, 20)");

    call(
        "set_node_properties",
        json!({"node_path": "Player", "properties": {"modulate": "#ff0000", "z_index": 3, "visible": false}}),
    )
    .await
    .expect("set mixed");
    assert_eq!(read("modulate").await, "Color(1, 0, 0, 1)");
    assert_eq!(read("z_index").await, "3");
    assert_eq!(read("visible").await, "false");

    expect_error(
        call(
            "set_node_properties",
            json!({"node_path": "Player", "properties": {"position": [1, 2], "definitely_not_a_prop": 1}}),
        )
        .await,
        "definitely_not_a_prop",
    );
    assert_eq!(
        read("position").await,
        "Vector2(10, 20)",
        "a rejected batch must not be partially applied"
    );

    // Only real, instantiable Node classes can be added.
    call(
        "add_node",
        json!({"parent_node_path": "Player", "node_type": "Sprite2D", "node_name": "Hero"}),
    )
    .await
    .expect("add Sprite2D");
    for class in ["Object", "Resource", "CanvasItem", "NotAClass"] {
        expect_error(call("add_node", json!({"node_type": class})).await, class);
    }

    // open_scene accepts a project file given as an OS path but nothing outside the project.
    let absolute = editor.project.join("main.tscn");
    let opened = call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": absolute}))
        .await
        .expect("absolute path inside the project");
    assert_eq!(opened["scene_path"], "res://main.tscn");
    let outside = tempfile::tempdir().expect("outside dir");
    std::fs::write(outside.path().join("x.tscn"), MAIN_SCENE).expect("outside scene");
    expect_error(
        call(
            "open_scene",
            json!({"scene_path": outside.path().join("x.tscn")}),
        )
        .await,
        "res://",
    );

    // A big scene is delivered intact (responses far above 64 KiB).
    call("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://big.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("open big");
    let big = call("get_scene_tree", json!({})).await.expect("big tree");
    assert!(count_nodes(&big["tree"]) >= 3001, "tree was truncated");

    // A tab that is not active can be inspected without editor errors.
    let main = call("get_scene_tree", json!({"scene_path": "res://main.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("inactive tab");
    assert_eq!(
        find(&main["tree"], "Hero").expect("Hero")["path"],
        "Player/Hero"
    );
    assert!(
        !editor.log_text().contains("not in a scene tree"),
        "editor reported path errors:\n{}",
        editor.log_text()
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
async fn undo_and_resource_refresh_behave() {
    let editor = start_editor(true).await;
    let bridge = &editor.bridge;

    bridge
        .send_command("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://main.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("open main");
    bridge
        .send_command(
            "add_node",
            json!({"parent_node_path": "Player/Body", "node_type": "Node2D", "node_name": "Child"}),
        )
        .await
        .expect("add Child");

    bridge
        .send_command("remove_node", json!({"node_path": "Player/Body"}))
        .await
        .expect("remove Body");
    bridge
        .send_command("__test_undo", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("undo");

    let tree = bridge
        .send_command("get_scene_tree", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("tree");
    let player = find(&tree["tree"], "Player").expect("Player");
    assert_eq!(
        child_names(player),
        ["Body", "Weapon"],
        "sibling order lost"
    );
    assert_eq!(child_names(find(player, "Body").expect("Body")), ["Child"]);

    bridge
        .send_command("save_scene", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("save");
    let saved = std::fs::read_to_string(editor.project.join("main.tscn")).expect("saved scene");
    assert!(
        saved.contains("[node name=\"Child\"") && saved.contains("parent=\"Player/Body\""),
        "owner of the restored subtree was lost:\n{saved}"
    );

    // Non-importable files are refreshed, not pushed through the importer.
    let refreshed = bridge
        .send_command("resave_resources", json!({"paths": ["res://script.gd"]}))
        .await
        .expect("refresh script");
    assert_eq!(refreshed["mode"], "refresh");
    assert!(
        !editor.log_text().contains("BUG: File queued for import"),
        "{}",
        editor.log_text()
    );

    // A node added through the editor's own undo history, not through a bridge
    // command, is still unsaved. Reloading must not throw it away unless asked.
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("hand.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Hand\" type=\"Node2D\"]\n",
    )
    .expect("hand scene");
    bridge
        .send_command("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://hand.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("open hand");
    bridge
        .send_command("__test_hand_edit", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("hand edit");
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("hand.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]\n\n[node name=\"Hand\" type=\"Node2D\"]\n\n[node name=\"FromDisk\" type=\"Node\" parent=\".\"]\n",
    )
    .expect("rewrite hand scene");
    let blocked = bridge
        .send_command("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://hand.tscn"}))
        .await;
    expect_error(blocked, "unsaved");
    let kept = bridge
        .send_command("get_scene_tree", json!({"scene_path": "res://hand.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("kept tree");
    assert!(
        find(&kept["tree"], "ByHand").is_some(),
        "hand edit was discarded: {kept}"
    );
    let discarded = bridge
        .send_command(
            "open_scene",
            json!({"scene_path": "res://hand.tscn", "discard": true}),
        )
        .await
        .expect("discard hand edits");
    assert_eq!(discarded["reloaded"], true, "{discarded}");
    let after = bridge
        .send_command("get_scene_tree", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("tree after discard");
    assert!(find(&after["tree"], "ByHand").is_none(), "{after}");
    assert!(find(&after["tree"], "FromDisk").is_some(), "{after}");

    // The same when the person opened the scene themselves: the bridge never saw it open,
    // yet its unsaved edits must survive a reload from disk.
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("opened.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]

[node name=\"Opened\" type=\"Node2D\"]
",
    )
    .expect("opened scene");
    bridge
        .send_command(
            "__test_open_directly",
            json!({"scene_path": "res://opened.tscn"}),
        )
        .await
        .expect("open directly");
    sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
    bridge
        .send_command("__test_hand_edit", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("hand edit in the directly opened scene");
    std::fs::write(
        editor.project.join("opened.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene format=3]

[node name=\"Opened\" type=\"Node2D\"]

[node name=\"FromDisk\" type=\"Node\" parent=\".\"]
",
    )
    .expect("rewrite opened scene");
    expect_error(
        bridge
            .send_command("open_scene", json!({"scene_path": "res://opened.tscn"}))
            .await,
        "unsaved",
    );
    let still = bridge
        .send_command("get_scene_tree", json!({"scene_path": "res://opened.tscn"}))
        .await
        .expect("tree of the directly opened scene");
    assert!(
        find(&still["tree"], "ByHand").is_some(),
        "hand edit in a scene the bridge never opened was discarded: {still}"
    );
    let scan = bridge
        .send_command("resave_resources", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("full scan");
    assert_eq!(scan["mode"], "full_scan");
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
async fn play_with_runtime_disabled_does_not_write_override() {
    let editor = start_editor_configured(false, "\n[godot_mcp]\n\nruntime_on_play=false\n").await;
    let bridge = &editor.bridge;
    bridge
        .send_command("run_project", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("play");
    let override_path = editor.project.join("override.cfg");
    let mut running = false;
    for _ in 0..40 {
        let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&override_path).unwrap_or_default();
        assert!(
            !text.contains("godot_mcp: temporary runtime autoload"),
            "play wrote override.cfg while runtime_on_play is off: {text}"
        );
        let status = bridge
            .send_command("get_run_status", json!({}))
            .await
            .expect("status");
        if status["running"] == true {
            running = true;
            break;
        }
        sleep(Duration::from_millis(50)).await;
    }
    assert!(running, "editor play did not start:\n{}", editor.log_text());
    let text = std::fs::read_to_string(&override_path).unwrap_or_default();
    assert!(
        !text.contains("godot_mcp: temporary runtime autoload"),
        "play wrote override.cfg while runtime_on_play is off: {text}"
    );
    bridge
        .send_command("stop_project", json!({}))
        .await
        .expect("stop");
}

```

`tests/feedback_test.rs`:

```rs
//! The agent feedback channel, tested against the real server binary over stdio, with a
//! stand-in for the GitHub command-line client.

mod common;

use common::{FakeGh, fake_gh};
use std::io::Write;
use std::process::{Command, Stdio};
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Runs the stand-in directly with the given arguments and standard input.
fn run_fake_gh(gh: &FakeGh, args: &[&str], input: &str) -> std::process::Output {
    let mut child = Command::new(fake_gh())
        .args(args)
        .envs(gh.env_pairs())
        .stdin(Stdio::piped())
        .stdout(Stdio::piped())
        .stderr(Stdio::piped())
        .spawn()
        .expect("start the stand-in");
    child
        .stdin
        .take()
        .expect("stand-in stdin")
        .write_all(input.as_bytes())
        .expect("write the body");
    child.wait_with_output().expect("stand-in output")
}

#[test]
fn the_stand_in_records_arguments_and_input_and_answers_as_scripted() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new()
        .script(
            "FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT",
            "Creating issue\nhttps://github.com/o/r/issues/5\n",
        )
        .script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDERR", "a warning\n");
    let output = run_fake_gh(
        &gh,
        &["issue", "create", "--title=--odd title", "--body-file", "-"],
        "the body\nsecond line",
    );

    assert!(output.status.success());
    assert_eq!(
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stdout),
        "Creating issue\nhttps://github.com/o/r/issues/5\n"
    );
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr), "a warning\n");
    assert_eq!(
        gh.calls(),
        vec![vec![
            "issue",
            "create",
            "--title=--odd title",
            "--body-file",
            "-"
        ]]
    );
    assert_eq!(gh.body().as_deref(), Some("the body\nsecond line"));
}

#[test]
fn the_stand_in_defaults_are_an_empty_search_and_a_created_issue() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let list = run_fake_gh(&gh, &["issue", "list"], "");
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&list.stdout), "[]");
    let create = run_fake_gh(&gh, &["issue", "create", "--body-file", "-"], "x");
    assert!(
        String::from_utf8_lossy(&create.stdout)
            .trim()
            .ends_with("/issues/1")
    );
    assert_eq!(gh.calls().len(), 2);
}

#[test]
fn the_stand_in_can_fail_and_can_be_slow() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new()
        .script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_EXIT", "4")
        .script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDERR", "gh auth login\n")
        .script("FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS", "300");
    let started = Instant::now();
    let output = run_fake_gh(&gh, &["issue", "create", "--body-file", "-"], "x");
    assert!(started.elapsed() >= Duration::from_millis(250));
    assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(4));
    assert_eq!(String::from_utf8_lossy(&output.stderr), "gh auth login\n");
}

#[test]
fn the_stand_in_refuses_commands_the_channel_never_issues() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let output = run_fake_gh(&gh, &["repo", "delete", "x"], "");
    assert_eq!(output.status.code(), Some(64));
}

use common::{Session, free_port, is_tool_error, tool_text};
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::path::PathBuf;

const FAST: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

fn no_godot() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from("godot-binary-that-does-not-exist")
}

async fn session() -> Session {
    Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await
}

fn note(kind: &str, summary: &str) -> Value {
    json!({"kind": kind, "summary": summary, "details": "Tried it; it did not work."})
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_note_is_stored_and_the_answer_lists_what_is_not_in_a_report_yet() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    let first = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_note_feedback",
            note("bug", "add_node ignores names"),
            FAST,
        )
        .await;
    assert!(!is_tool_error(&first), "{first}");
    let text = tool_text(&first);
    assert!(text.contains("n1"), "{text}");

    let second = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_note_feedback",
            note("docs", "README tool table is stale"),
            FAST,
        )
        .await;
    let text = tool_text(&second);
    assert!(text.contains("n2"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("n1") && text.contains("add_node ignores names"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("godot_draft_feedback"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_unknown_kind_is_rejected_by_the_schema_before_anything_is_stored() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_note_feedback", note("complaint", "x"), FAST)
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    assert!(tool_text(&reply).contains("kind"), "{reply}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn over_long_text_is_refused_with_the_limit_and_the_way_to_fix_it() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_note_feedback", note("bug", &"s".repeat(121)), FAST)
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains("summary") && text.contains("120"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Actionable recovery"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_twenty_first_note_is_refused() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    for index in 0..20 {
        let reply = session
            .call_tool(
                "godot_note_feedback",
                note("bug", &format!("note {index}")),
                FAST,
            )
            .await;
        assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    }
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_note_feedback", note("bug", "one too many"), FAST)
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    assert!(tool_text(&reply).contains("20"), "{reply}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_failing_tool_answers_identically_with_the_channel_on_and_off() {
    let failing = json!({"file_path": "not-a-script.txt"});
    let mut full =
        Session::start_with_env(free_port(), &no_godot(), &[("GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK", "full")]).await;
    let mut off =
        Session::start_with_env(free_port(), &no_godot(), &[("GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK", "off")]).await;
    let with_channel = full
        .call_tool("godot_create_script", failing.clone(), FAST)
        .await;
    let without = off.call_tool("godot_create_script", failing, FAST).await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&with_channel));
    assert_eq!(with_channel["result"], without["result"]);
}

// ---- drafts ----

use common::{MockBridge, Reply, project_dir};

async fn note_with(session: &mut Session, args: Value) {
    let reply = session.call_tool("godot_note_feedback", args, FAST).await;
    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
}

async fn draft_text(session: &mut Session, args: Value) -> String {
    let reply = session.call_tool("godot_draft_feedback", args, FAST).await;
    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    tool_text(&reply)
}

fn row(text: &str, label: &str) -> String {
    text.lines()
        .find(|line| line.starts_with(&format!("| {label} |")))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no {label} row in\n{text}"))
        .to_string()
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_draft_without_notes_says_to_record_one_first() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_draft_feedback", json!({}), FAST)
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    assert!(tool_text(&reply).contains("godot_note_feedback"), "{reply}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_draft_shows_the_whole_report_and_every_way_to_deliver_it() {
    let mut session = Session::start_with_env(
        free_port(),
        &no_godot(),
        &[("GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH", "no-such-gh-executable")],
    )
    .await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "add_node ignores names")).await;

    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert!(text.contains("Draft d1"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Nothing has been sent"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("[agent-feedback] bug: add_node ignores names"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(
        text.contains("## Summary *(written by the agent)*"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("## Report details"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("gmfb"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("Link: https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/new?title="),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("Copy: always available"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Submit: not available"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("COMPLETE report"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        !text.contains("explicitly said yes"),
        "submission is not offered when it is not available: {text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn submission_is_listed_as_available_when_the_client_is_found() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let env = gh.server_env();
    let mut session =
        Session::start_with_env(free_port(), &no_godot(), &common::as_pairs(&env)).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;
    assert!(
        text.contains("Submit: available through godot_submit_feedback"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains(r#"draft_id "d1""#), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("explicitly said yes"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn draft_mode_never_mentions_submission() {
    let mut session =
        Session::start_with_env(free_port(), &no_godot(), &[("GODOT_MCP_FEEDBACK", "draft")]).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;
    assert!(!text.to_lowercase().contains("submit"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Copy: always available"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn the_environment_block_comes_from_the_editor_and_the_project() {
    let project = project_dir();
    let addon = project.path().join("addons").join("godot_mcp");
    std::fs::create_dir_all(&addon).expect("addon directory");
    std::fs::write(
        addon.join("plugin.cfg"),
        "[plugin]\n\nname=\"Godot MCP Bridge\"\nversion=\"0.3.0\"\nscript=\"plugin.gd\"\n",
    )
    .expect("plugin.cfg");
    let directory = project.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(move |command| match command["type"].as_str() {
        Some("get_version") => Reply::Ok(json!({"version_string": "4.5.stable.official"})),
        Some("get_project_info") => Reply::Ok(json!({"name": "Game", "project_dir": directory})),
        other => Reply::Err(format!("unexpected command {other:?}")),
    })
    .await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;
    // A failing call that resolves the project makes the server know its directory.
    let failed = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_validate_script",
            json!({"script_path": "res://missing.gd"}),
            FAST,
        )
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&failed));
    let mut with_path = note("bug", "x");
    with_path["tool"] = json!("godot_validate_script");
    with_path["details"] = json!(format!("it failed in {}", project.path().display()));
    note_with(&mut session, with_path).await;

    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Godot"), "| Godot | 4.5.stable.official |");
    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Addon"), "| Addon | 0.3.0 |");
    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Editor bridge"), "| Editor bridge | connected |");
    assert_eq!(
        row(&text, "godot-mcp"),
        format!("| godot-mcp | {} |", env!("CARGO_PKG_VERSION"))
    );
    assert!(row(&text, "Platform").contains(std::env::consts::OS));
    assert!(text.contains("it failed in {project}"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Script file not found: {project}"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        !text.contains(&project.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned()),
        "{text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn without_an_editor_or_a_project_the_unknowns_are_said_so() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;
    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Godot"), "| Godot | unknown |");
    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Addon"), "| Addon | unknown |");
    assert!(
        row(&text, "Editor bridge").contains("not reachable"),
        "{text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_project_without_the_addon_is_reported_as_such() {
    let project = project_dir();
    let directory = project.path().to_string_lossy().into_owned();
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(move |command| match command["type"].as_str() {
        Some("get_version") => Reply::Ok(json!({"version_string": "4.5.stable"})),
        Some("get_project_info") => Reply::Ok(json!({"project_dir": directory})),
        _ => Reply::Err("unexpected".into()),
    })
    .await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;
    assert_eq!(
        row(&text, "Addon"),
        "| Addon | not installed in the project |"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_stalled_editor_costs_the_draft_no_more_than_its_budget() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Never).await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;

    let started = Instant::now();
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert!(
        started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(9),
        "{:?}",
        started.elapsed()
    );
    assert_eq!(row(&text, "Godot"), "| Godot | unknown |");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn secrets_paths_and_mentions_never_reach_the_answer() {
    let mut session = Session::start_with_env(
        free_port(),
        &no_godot(),
        &[
            ("GODOT_MCP_TOKEN", "supersecrettoken1234567890abcdef"),
            ("HOME", "/home/tester"),
            ("USERPROFILE", r"C:\Users\tester"),
        ],
    )
    .await;
    let mut sensitive = note("bug", "leaky");
    sensitive["details"] = json!(
        "token supersecrettoken1234567890abcdef, files /home/tester/game/a.gd and \
         C:\\Users\\tester\\game\\a.gd, ping @maintainer, see #12"
    );
    note_with(&mut session, sensitive).await;

    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert!(!text.contains("supersecrettoken"), "{text}");
    assert!(!text.contains("tester"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("@\u{200B}maintainer"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("#\u{200B}12"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("{redacted}"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn failures_of_the_named_tool_are_quoted_and_other_failures_are_not() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    let failed = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_create_script",
            json!({"file_path": "not-a-script.txt"}),
            FAST,
        )
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&failed));
    let other = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_validate_script",
            json!({"script_path": "elsewhere.txt"}),
            FAST,
        )
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&other));
    let mut named = note("bug", "create_script message");
    named["tool"] = json!("godot_create_script");
    note_with(&mut session, named).await;

    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert!(
        text.contains("Script files must have the .gd extension"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(
        text.contains("godot_create_script: 1 call, 1 failure"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(
        !text.contains("Only .gd scripts can be validated"),
        "{text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn requests_for_missing_tools_become_evidence_for_a_missing_feature() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    for _ in 0..2 {
        let reply = session
            .call_tool("godot_create_scene", json!({}), FAST)
            .await;
        assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32602);
    }
    note_with(
        &mut session,
        note("missing_feature", "no way to create scenes"),
    )
    .await;

    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;

    assert!(text.contains("godot_create_scene"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("requested 2 times"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_draft_marks_its_notes_and_reports_the_ones_left_out() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("docs", "readme is stale")).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "crash on open")).await;

    let first = draft_text(&mut session, json!({"note_ids": ["n2"]})).await;
    assert!(first.contains("Draft d1"), "{first}");
    assert!(first.contains("Notes not in any report yet"), "{first}");
    assert!(first.contains("n1 (docs): readme is stale"), "{first}");

    let second = draft_text(&mut session, json!({})).await;
    assert!(second.contains("Draft d2"), "{second}");
    assert!(second.contains("readme is stale"), "{second}");
    assert!(!second.contains("crash on open"), "{second}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_chosen_title_is_prefixed_and_a_bad_selection_names_the_known_ids() {
    let mut session = session().await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    let text = draft_text(&mut session, json!({"title": "Custom title"})).await;
    assert!(text.contains("[agent-feedback] Custom title"), "{text}");

    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_draft_feedback", json!({"note_ids": ["n9"]}), FAST)
        .await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let refusal = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        refusal.contains("n9") && refusal.contains("n1"),
        "{refusal}"
    );
}

// ---- submission ----

use godot_mcp::issues::{IssueClient, RepoAddress};
use godot_mcp::journal::Outcome;

const ISSUE_PREFIX: &str = "https://github.com/zeegzoog/godot-mcp/issues/";

async fn started(gh: &FakeGh) -> Session {
    let env = gh.server_env();
    Session::start_with_env(free_port(), &no_godot(), &common::as_pairs(&env)).await
}

/// Records one note and drafts it; returns the draft answer.
async fn drafted(session: &mut Session) -> String {
    note_with(session, note("bug", "add_node ignores names")).await;
    draft_text(session, json!({})).await
}

fn line_after(text: &str, prefix: &str) -> String {
    text.lines()
        .find_map(|line| line.strip_prefix(prefix))
        .unwrap_or_else(|| panic!("no {prefix:?} line in\n{text}"))
        .trim()
        .to_string()
}

/// The report between the outer fence of a draft answer.
fn report_in(text: &str) -> String {
    let mut lines = text.lines();
    let fence = lines
        .find(|line| {
            line.ends_with("markdown")
                && line.trim_end_matches("markdown").chars().all(|c| c == '`')
        })
        .expect("the opening fence")
        .trim_end_matches("markdown")
        .to_string();
    let mut body: Vec<&str> = Vec::new();
    for line in lines {
        if line == fence {
            break;
        }
        body.push(line);
    }
    body.join("\n")
}

async fn submit(session: &mut Session, args: Value) -> Value {
    session
        .call_tool("godot_submit_feedback", args, Duration::from_secs(30))
        .await
}

fn confirmed(draft: &str) -> Value {
    json!({"draft_id": draft, "confirmed": true})
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn without_confirmation_nothing_is_sent_and_the_reply_says_what_to_do() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(&mut session, json!({"draft_id": "d1", "confirmed": false})).await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        text.contains("confirmed") && text.contains("explicit yes"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("Actionable recovery"), "{text}");
    assert!(gh.calls().is_empty(), "the client must not be started");

    let missing = submit(&mut session, json!({"draft_id": "d1"})).await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&missing));
    assert!(tool_text(&missing).contains("confirmed"), "{missing}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_unknown_draft_lists_the_known_ones() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;
    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d9")).await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains("d9") && text.contains("d1"), "{text}");
    assert!(gh.calls().is_empty());

    let mut fresh = started(&FakeGh::new()).await;
    let reply = submit(&mut fresh, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(
        tool_text(&reply).contains("godot_draft_feedback"),
        "{reply}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_confirmed_draft_is_published_exactly_as_shown_and_only_once() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script(
        "FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT",
        format!("Creating issue in zeegzoog/godot-mcp\n\n{ISSUE_PREFIX}42\n").as_str(),
    );
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    let draft = drafted(&mut session).await;
    let fingerprint = line_after(&draft, "Fingerprint: ");
    let title = line_after(&draft, "Title: ");

    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;

    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains(&format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}42")), "{text}");
    assert!(text.to_lowercase().contains("public"), "{text}");
    assert_eq!(
        gh.calls(),
        vec![
            vec![
                "issue".to_string(),
                "list".into(),
                "--repo".into(),
                "zeegzoog/godot-mcp".into(),
                "--state".into(),
                "all".into(),
                "--search".into(),
                format!("{fingerprint} in:body"),
                "--json".into(),
                "number,title,url,body".into(),
                "--limit".into(),
                "5".into(),
            ],
            vec![
                "issue".to_string(),
                "create".into(),
                "--repo".into(),
                "zeegzoog/godot-mcp".into(),
                format!("--title={title}"),
                "--body-file".into(),
                "-".into(),
            ],
        ]
    );
    assert_eq!(gh.body(), Some(report_in(&draft)));

    let again = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(
        tool_text(&again).contains(&format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}42")),
        "{again}"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        gh.calls().len(),
        2,
        "a repeat must not start the client again"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn an_existing_issue_with_the_same_fingerprint_is_reported_instead_of_a_new_one() {
    let hit = format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}7");
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_LIST_HIT_URL", &hit);
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;

    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        text.contains(&hit) && text.contains("already exists"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("allow_duplicate"), "{text}");
    assert_eq!(gh.calls().len(), 1, "only the look-up ran");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn allow_duplicate_creates_the_issue_anyway_without_looking() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_LIST_HIT_URL", &format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}7"));
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(
        &mut session,
        json!({"draft_id": "d1", "confirmed": true, "allow_duplicate": true}),
    )
    .await;

    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    assert!(
        tool_text(&reply).contains(&format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}1")),
        "{reply}"
    );
    let calls = gh.calls();
    assert_eq!(calls.len(), 1);
    assert_eq!(calls[0][1], "create");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_failed_look_up_does_not_stop_the_creation_and_is_reported() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new()
        .script("FAKE_GH_LIST_EXIT", "1")
        .script("FAKE_GH_LIST_STDERR", "search is down\n");
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;

    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains(&format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}1")), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("duplicate check failed") && text.contains("search is down"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert_eq!(gh.calls().len(), 2);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_client_that_is_not_signed_in_leaves_the_draft_ready_for_another_try() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_EXIT", "4").script(
        "FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDERR",
        "To get started with GitHub CLI, please run:  gh auth login\n",
    );
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        text.contains("Not created") && text.contains("not signed in"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(
        text.contains("gh auth login") && text.contains("by hand"),
        "{text}"
    );

    let retry = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&retry));
    assert_eq!(gh.calls().len(), 4, "the retry asked the client again");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_missing_client_is_reported_with_the_way_to_install_it_and_the_hand_path() {
    let mut session = Session::start_with_env(
        free_port(),
        &no_godot(),
        &[("GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH", "no-such-gh-executable")],
    )
    .await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;
    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains("not found"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        text.contains("GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH") && text.contains("by hand"),
        "{text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn success_without_an_issue_address_is_unknown_and_is_never_retried() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT", "Created!\n");
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        text.contains("Unknown whether the issue was created"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(text.contains("before submitting again"), "{text}");

    let again = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(tool_text(&again).contains("Unknown whether"), "{again}");
    assert_eq!(gh.calls().len(), 2, "no second creation");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn gh_output_never_reaches_the_protocol_channel() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new()
        .script("FAKE_GH_LIST_STDOUT", "[]\nnoise from the look-up\n")
        .script("FAKE_GH_LIST_STDERR", "stderr noise\n")
        .script(
            "FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDOUT",
            format!("noise before\n{ISSUE_PREFIX}5\n").as_str(),
        )
        .script("FAKE_GH_CREATE_STDERR", "more stderr noise\n");
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;
    // `Session::recv` fails the test on any line of standard output that is not JSON.
    let reply = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(
        tool_text(&reply).contains(&format!("{ISSUE_PREFIX}5")),
        "{reply}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn two_submissions_of_one_draft_at_the_same_time_create_one_issue() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS", "700");
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    for id in [9001, 9002] {
        session
            .send(&json!({
                "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": id, "method": "tools/call",
                "params": {"name": "godot_submit_feedback", "arguments": confirmed("d1")}
            }))
            .await;
    }
    let mut replies = Vec::new();
    while replies.len() < 2 {
        replies.push(
            session
                .recv(Duration::from_secs(30))
                .await
                .expect("a reply to each call"),
        );
    }

    let outcomes: Vec<String> = replies.iter().map(tool_text).collect();
    assert_eq!(
        outcomes
            .iter()
            .filter(|text| text.contains(ISSUE_PREFIX))
            .count(),
        1,
        "{outcomes:?}"
    );
    assert_eq!(
        outcomes
            .iter()
            .filter(|text| text.contains("being submitted"))
            .count(),
        1,
        "{outcomes:?}"
    );
    let creates = gh.calls().iter().filter(|call| call[1] == "create").count();
    assert_eq!(creates, 1);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_cancelled_submission_leaves_the_draft_ready_for_another_try() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new().script("FAKE_GH_DELAY_MS", "1500");
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    drafted(&mut session).await;

    session
        .send(&json!({
            "jsonrpc": "2.0", "id": 9100, "method": "tools/call",
            "params": {"name": "godot_submit_feedback", "arguments": confirmed("d1")}
        }))
        .await;
    tokio::time::sleep(Duration::from_millis(500)).await;
    session
        .send(&json!({
            "jsonrpc": "2.0", "method": "notifications/cancelled",
            "params": {"requestId": 9100}
        }))
        .await;
    assert!(session.recv(Duration::from_millis(800)).await.is_none());

    let retry = submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    assert!(!is_tool_error(&retry), "{retry}");
    assert!(tool_text(&retry).contains(ISSUE_PREFIX), "{retry}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_title_that_looks_like_an_option_is_still_one_argument() {
    let gh = FakeGh::new();
    let mut session = started(&gh).await;
    note_with(&mut session, note("bug", "x")).await;
    draft_text(&mut session, json!({"title": "--web --repo evil/repo"})).await;
    submit(&mut session, confirmed("d1")).await;
    let create = gh
        .calls()
        .into_iter()
        .find(|call| call[1] == "create")
        .expect("a create call");
    assert_eq!(create.len(), 7, "{create:?}");
    assert_eq!(create[3], "zeegzoog/godot-mcp");
    assert_eq!(create[4], "--title=[agent-feedback] --web --repo evil/repo");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_client_that_does_not_answer_in_time_is_unknown_for_a_create_and_a_failed_look_up() {
    let repo = RepoAddress::manifest().expect("repository");
    let client = IssueClient::new(fake_gh(), repo)
        .with_limits(Duration::from_millis(300), Duration::from_millis(300));

    let lookup = client.find_duplicate("FAKE_GH_SLEEP_3000").await;
    assert!(lookup.is_err(), "{lookup:?}");

    let created = client.create("FAKE_GH_SLEEP_3000", "body").await;
    assert!(matches!(created, Outcome::Unknown(_)), "{created:?}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn a_client_that_cannot_be_started_created_nothing() {
    let repo = RepoAddress::manifest().expect("repository");
    let client = IssueClient::new(PathBuf::from("no-such-gh-executable"), repo);
    match client.create("title", "body").await {
        Outcome::NotCreated { reason, steps } => {
            assert!(reason.contains("could not be started"), "{reason}");
            assert!(steps.iter().any(|step| step.contains("GODOT_MCP_GH_PATH")));
        }
        other => panic!("expected NotCreated, got {other:?}"),
    }
}

`tests/runtime_e2e_test.rs`:

```rs
//! Behavior tests for a real headless Godot process: live inspection, injected input,
//! logs, and the remote debugger. Ignored unless `GODOT_PATH` points at Godot 4.5+.
//!
//! ```bash
//! GODOT_PATH=/path/to/godot cargo test --test runtime_e2e_test -- --ignored --nocapture
//! ```

use godot_mcp::bridge::BridgeClient;
use godot_mcp::cli_runner::CliRunner;
use godot_mcp::protocol::ToolResult;
use godot_mcp::tools::ToolManager;
use serde_json::{Value, json};
use std::path::{Path, PathBuf};
use std::sync::Arc;

const IGNORE: &str = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable";

fn godot_path() -> PathBuf {
    let _ = dotenvy::dotenv();
    std::env::var("GODOT_PATH")
        .ok()
        .filter(|value| !value.is_empty())
        .map(PathBuf::from)
        .filter(|path| path.exists())
        .expect("set GODOT_PATH to run the runtime end-to-end tests")
}

fn text_of(result: &ToolResult) -> String {
    result
        .content
        .iter()
        .filter_map(|item| match item {
            godot_mcp::protocol::ToolContent::Text { text } => Some(text.as_str()),
            godot_mcp::protocol::ToolContent::Image { .. } => None,
        })
        .collect::<Vec<_>>()
        .join("\n")
}

fn json_of(result: &ToolResult) -> Value {
    let text = text_of(result);
    serde_json::from_str(&text).unwrap_or_else(|_| panic!("not json: {text}"))
}

fn project() -> tempfile::TempDir {
    let dir = tempfile::tempdir().expect("project");
    let root = dir.path();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("scenes")).unwrap();
    std::fs::create_dir_all(root.join("scripts")).unwrap();
    std::fs::write(
        root.join("project.godot"),
        "config_version=5\n\n[application]\n\nconfig/name=\"RuntimeProbe\"\nrun/main_scene=\"res://scenes/main.tscn\"\nconfig/features=PackedStringArray(\"4.5\")\n",
    )
    .unwrap();
    std::fs::write(
        root.join("scripts/probe.gd"),
        r#"extends Node2D
var last_key := ""
var mouse_presses := 0
var touch_presses := 0
var joy_button := ""
var joy_axis := 0.0
var hits := 0
signal pinged(value)

func _ready() -> void:
    push_error("mcp-probe-error")

func _process(_delta: float) -> void:
    hits += 1

func _input(event: InputEvent) -> void:
    if event is InputEventKey and event.pressed and not event.echo:
        last_key = OS.get_keycode_string(event.keycode)
    elif event is InputEventMouseButton and event.pressed:
        mouse_presses += 1
    elif event is InputEventScreenTouch and event.pressed:
        touch_presses += 1
    elif event is InputEventJoypadButton and event.pressed:
        joy_button = str(event.button_index)
    elif event is InputEventJoypadMotion:
        joy_axis = event.axis_value

func poke(value: int) -> int:
    pinged.emit(value)
    return value + 1
"#,
    )
    .unwrap();
    std::fs::write(
        root.join("scenes/main.tscn"),
        "[gd_scene load_steps=2 format=3]\n\n[ext_resource type=\"Script\" path=\"res://scripts/probe.gd\" id=\"1\"]\n\n[node name=\"Probe\" type=\"Node2D\"]\nscript = ExtResource(\"1\")\n",
    )
    .unwrap();
    dir
}

fn manager(project: &Path) -> Arc<ToolManager> {
    Arc::new(
        ToolManager::new(BridgeClient::new(9_001), CliRunner::new(godot_path()))
            .with_working_dir(project.to_path_buf()),
    )
}

async fn call(tools: &Arc<ToolManager>, name: &str, args: Value) -> ToolResult {
    Arc::clone(tools).call_tool(name, args).await
}

#[tokio::test]
#[ignore = "needs GODOT_PATH pointing at a Godot 4.5+ executable"]
async fn running_game_accepts_input_and_can_be_inspected() {
    let _ = IGNORE;
    let project = project();
    let tools = manager(project.path());
    let started = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_run_project",
        json!({
            "project_path": project.path(),
            "scene": "res://scenes/main.tscn",
            "mode": "process",
            "headless": true
        }),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!started.is_error, "{}", text_of(&started));
    let started_text = text_of(&started);
    let run_id = started_text
        .split("run_id: ")
        .nth(1)
        .and_then(|rest| rest.split(|c: char| !c.is_ascii_alphanumeric()).next())
        .expect("run id")
        .to_string();

    let tree = call(&tools, "godot_game_tree", json!({"run_id": run_id})).await;
    assert!(!tree.is_error, "{}", text_of(&tree));
    let tree_json = json_of(&tree);
    let nodes = tree_json["nodes"].as_array().expect("nodes");
    assert!(
        nodes.iter().any(|node| node["name"] == "Probe"),
        "{tree_json}"
    );

    let input = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_game_input",
        json!({
            "run_id": run_id,
            "steps": [
                {"type": "key", "key": "A", "phase": "tap"},
                {"type": "mouse_button", "button": "left", "x": 12, "y": 34, "phase": "tap"},
                {"type": "touch", "x": 8, "y": 9, "phase": "tap"},
                {"type": "joy_button", "button": "a", "phase": "press"},
                {"type": "joy_axis", "axis": "left_x", "value": 0.5},
                {"type": "wait", "frames": 1}
            ]
        }),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!input.is_error, "{}", text_of(&input));
    let input_json = json_of(&input);
    assert_eq!(
        input_json["steps"].as_array().map(Vec::len),
        Some(6),
        "{input_json}"
    );

    let props = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_game_get",
        json!({
            "run_id": run_id,
            "path": ".",
            "properties": ["last_key", "mouse_presses", "touch_presses", "joy_button", "joy_axis", "hits"]
        }),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!props.is_error, "{}", text_of(&props));
    let props_json = json_of(&props);
    let values = &props_json["properties"];
    assert_eq!(values["last_key"], "A", "{props_json}");
    assert!(
        values["mouse_presses"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0) >= 1,
        "{props_json}"
    );
    assert!(
        values["touch_presses"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0) >= 1,
        "{props_json}"
    );
    assert_eq!(values["joy_button"], "0", "{props_json}");
    assert!(
        values["joy_axis"].as_f64().unwrap_or(0.0) > 0.4,
        "{props_json}"
    );
    assert!(values["hits"].as_i64().unwrap_or(0) > 0, "{props_json}");

    let called = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_game_call",
        json!({"run_id": run_id, "path": ".", "method": "poke", "permit": ["poke"], "args": [3]}),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!called.is_error, "{}", text_of(&called));
    assert_eq!(json_of(&called)["value"], 4, "{}", text_of(&called));

    let observed = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_game_observe",
        json!({"run_id": run_id, "path": ".", "signals": ["pinged"], "frames": 1}),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!observed.is_error, "{}", text_of(&observed));

    let shot = call(&tools, "godot_game_screenshot", json!({"run_id": run_id})).await;
    assert!(shot.is_error, "{}", text_of(&shot));
    assert!(
        shot.content
            .iter()
            .all(|item| !matches!(item, godot_mcp::protocol::ToolContent::Image { .. })),
        "a headless game must not return image bytes"
    );
    assert!(text_of(&shot).contains("headless"), "{}", text_of(&shot));

    let released = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_game_input_release",
        json!({"run_id": run_id}),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!released.is_error, "{}", text_of(&released));
    let held = &json_of(&released)["held"];
    for key in ["keys", "mouse", "joy_buttons", "joy_axes", "touches"] {
        assert_eq!(
            held[key].as_i64(),
            Some(0),
            "held.{key} should be empty: {held}"
        );
    }

    let logs = call(
        &tools,
        "godot_run_output",
        json!({"run_id": run_id, "level": "error"}),
    )
    .await;
    assert!(!logs.is_error, "{}", text_of(&logs));
    assert!(
        text_of(&logs).contains("mcp-probe-error"),
        "{}",
        text_of(&logs)
    );

    let stopped = call(&tools, "godot_stop_project", json!({"run_id": run_id})).await;
    assert!(!stopped.is_error, "{}", text_of(&stopped));
    let override_cfg =
        std::fs::read_to_string(project.path().join("override.cfg")).unwrap_or_default();
    assert!(
        !override_cfg.contains("godot_mcp: temporary runtime autoload"),
        "override.cfg was left injected: {override_cfg}"
    );
    assert!(
        !project.path().join("addons").exists(),
        "the run left runtime files in a project that had no addon"
    );
}

`tests/server_test.rs`:

```rs
use godot_mcp::protocol::{JsonRpcResponse, ToolResult};
use godot_mcp::tools::ToolManager;
use serde_json::json;

#[test]
fn test_jsonrpc_success_response() {
    let resp = JsonRpcResponse::success(json!(1), json!({"status": "ok"}));
    let serialized = serde_json::to_string(&resp).expect("Serialization failed");

    assert!(serialized.contains("\"jsonrpc\":\"2.0\""));
    assert!(serialized.contains("\"id\":1"));
    assert!(serialized.contains("\"status\":\"ok\""));
}

#[test]
fn test_jsonrpc_error_response() {
    let resp = JsonRpcResponse::error(json!("req-123"), -32601, "Method not found");
    let serialized = serde_json::to_string(&resp).expect("Serialization failed");

    assert!(serialized.contains("\"code\":-32601"));
    assert!(serialized.contains("Method not found"));
}

#[test]
fn test_tool_definitions() {
    let tools = ToolManager::list_tools();
    assert!(!tools.is_empty());

    let tool_names: Vec<&str> = tools.iter().map(|t| t.name.as_str()).collect();
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_get_version"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_get_scene_tree"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_add_node"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_remove_node"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_capture_viewport"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_create_script"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_validate_script"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_stop_project"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_get_run_status"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_get_open_scenes"));
    assert!(tool_names.contains(&"godot_resave_resources"));

    // Verify all input schemas are valid JSON objects
    for tool in tools {
        assert!(tool.input_schema.is_object());
        assert_eq!(tool.input_schema["type"], "object");
    }
}

#[test]
fn test_tool_result_helpers() {
    let text_res = ToolResult::text("Sample output");
    assert!(!text_res.is_error);
    assert_eq!(text_res.content.len(), 1);

    let err_res = ToolResult::error("Sample error", &["Recovery suggestion 1"]);
    assert!(err_res.is_error);
    assert_eq!(err_res.content.len(), 1);
}

`tests/stdio_protocol_test.rs`:

```rs
//! Drives the real `godot-mcp` binary over stdio against a fake editor bridge.

mod common;

use common::{MockBridge, Reply, Session, fake_godot, free_port, is_tool_error, project_dir};
use serde_json::json;
use std::path::PathBuf;
use std::time::{Duration, Instant};

/// Generous upper bound for waiting on a response; speed assertions use their own thresholds.
const FAST: Duration = Duration::from_secs(10);

fn no_godot() -> PathBuf {
    PathBuf::from("godot-binary-that-does-not-exist")
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn initialize_echoes_a_supported_protocol_version() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;

    let reply = session
        .request("initialize", json!({"protocolVersion": "2025-06-18"}), FAST)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(reply["result"]["protocolVersion"], "2025-06-18");

    let reply = session
        .request("initialize", json!({"protocolVersion": "1999-01-01"}), FAST)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(
        reply["result"]["protocolVersion"], "2025-06-18",
        "unsupported versions get the newest one the server speaks"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn malformed_json_is_a_parse_error() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    session.send_raw("{this is not json").await;
    let reply = session.recv(FAST).await.expect("parse error response");
    assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32700);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn request_without_jsonrpc_field_is_an_invalid_request_keeping_its_id() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    session.send_raw(r#"{"id":77,"method":"ping"}"#).await;
    let reply = session.recv(FAST).await.expect("invalid request response");
    assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32600);
    assert_eq!(reply["id"], 77);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn batch_requests_are_rejected_as_invalid_requests() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    session
        .send_raw(r#"[{"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":1,"method":"ping"}]"#)
        .await;
    let reply = session.recv(FAST).await.expect("batch rejection");
    assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32600);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn unknown_tool_is_an_invalid_params_error() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_does_not_exist", json!({}), FAST)
        .await;
    assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32602);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn tool_call_without_a_name_is_an_invalid_params_error() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    let reply = session.request("tools/call", json!({}), FAST).await;
    assert_eq!(reply["error"]["code"], -32602);
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn wrong_argument_type_fails_fast_without_reaching_the_editor() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Never).await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;

    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_get_node_properties", json!({"node_path": 5}), FAST)
        .await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = common::tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(
        text.contains("node_path") && text.contains("string"),
        "{text}"
    );
    assert!(
        bridge.received().is_empty(),
        "invalid call must not be forwarded"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn missing_required_argument_comes_with_a_recovery_hint() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_create_script", json!({}), FAST)
        .await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = common::tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains("file_path"), "{text}");
    assert!(text.contains("Actionable recovery suggestions"), "{text}");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn bridge_reply_is_passed_through_to_the_client() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| {
        Reply::Ok(json!({"open_scenes": ["res://a.tscn"], "active_scene": "res://a.tscn"}))
    })
    .await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;

    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_get_open_scenes", json!({}), FAST)
        .await;

    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply));
    assert!(common::tool_text(&reply).contains("res://a.tscn"));
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn ping_is_answered_while_a_tool_call_is_pending() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Never).await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;

    session
        .send(&json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":100,"method":"tools/call",
            "params":{"name":"godot_get_open_scenes","arguments":{}}}))
        .await;
    let started = Instant::now();
    let reply = session.request("ping", json!({}), FAST).await;

    assert!(reply["result"].is_object());
    assert!(
        started.elapsed() < Duration::from_secs(3),
        "ping must not queue behind the hanging call"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn cancelled_request_never_gets_a_response() {
    let bridge = MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Never).await;
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &no_godot()).await;

    session
        .send(&json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":100,"method":"tools/call",
            "params":{"name":"godot_get_open_scenes","arguments":{}}}))
        .await;
    session
        .send(&json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","method":"notifications/cancelled",
            "params":{"requestId":100,"reason":"user abort"}}))
        .await;
    session
        .send(&json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":101,"method":"ping"}))
        .await;

    let mut ids = Vec::new();
    while let Some(frame) = session.recv(Duration::from_millis(1500)).await {
        ids.push(frame["id"].clone());
    }
    assert_eq!(ids, vec![json!(101)], "only the ping may be answered");
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn offline_bridge_fails_fast_with_an_actionable_error() {
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &no_godot()).await;

    let started = Instant::now();
    let reply = session
        .call_tool("godot_get_scene_tree", json!({}), Duration::from_secs(10))
        .await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    assert!(common::tool_text(&reply).contains("Launch Godot Editor"));
    assert!(
        started.elapsed() < Duration::from_millis(1500),
        "took {:?}",
        started.elapsed()
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn editor_errors_are_reported_as_is_without_cli_fallback() {
    let bridge =
        MockBridge::start(|_| Reply::Err("Scene file does not exist: res://nope.tscn".into()))
            .await;
    let project = project_dir();
    let fake = fake_godot(project.path());
    let mut session = Session::start(bridge.port, &fake).await;

    let reply = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_run_project",
            json!({"scene": "res://nope.tscn", "project_path": project.path()}),
            Duration::from_secs(10),
        )
        .await;

    assert!(is_tool_error(&reply));
    let text = common::tool_text(&reply);
    assert!(text.contains("Scene file does not exist"), "{text}");
    assert!(
        !text.contains("Spawned"),
        "must not fall back to the CLI: {text}"
    );
    assert!(
        !text.contains("Launch Godot Editor"),
        "an editor-reported error is not a connectivity problem: {text}"
    );
}

#[tokio::test]
async fn cli_fallback_keeps_stdout_clean() {
    let project = project_dir();
    let fake = fake_godot(project.path());
    let mut session = Session::start(free_port(), &fake).await;

    let reply = session
        .call_tool(
            "godot_run_project",
            json!({"project_path": project.path()}),
            Duration::from_secs(10),
        )
        .await;
    assert!(!is_tool_error(&reply), "{reply}");

    // `Session::recv` panics on any line that is not JSON, so draining proves stdout stayed clean.
    session
        .send(&json!({"jsonrpc":"2.0","id":900,"method":"ping"}))
        .await;
    let mut frames = 0;
    while session.recv(Duration::from_millis(1200)).await.is_some() {
        frames += 1;
    }
    assert_eq!(frames, 1, "only the ping response may appear on stdout");
}

        .await;

    assert_eq!(received[0]["token"], "s3cret");
    assert_eq!(received[0]["type"], "get_open_scenes");
}
